Tokenisation via Convex Relaxations
Authors: Jan Tempus, Philip Whittington, Craig W. Schmidt, Dennis Komm, Tiago Pimentel
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tokenisation is an integral part of the current NLP pipeline. Current tokenisation algorithms such as BPE and Unigram are greedy algorithms -- they make locally optimal decisions without considering the resulting vocabulary as a whole. We instead formulate tokeniser construction as a linear program and solve it using convex optimisation tools, yielding a new algorithm we call ConvexTok. We find ConvexTok consistently improves intrinsic tokenisation metrics and the bits-per-byte (BpB) achieved by language models; it also improves downstream task performance, but less consistently. Furthermore, ConvexTok allows the user to certify how far their tokeniser is from optimal, with respect to a certain objective, via a lower bound, and we empirically find it to be within 1\% of optimal at common vocabulary sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过凸松弛进行分词
分词是当前自然语言处理流水线的重要组成部分。当前分词算法如BPE和Unigram是贪心算法——它们仅做局部最优决策，而不考虑最终词汇表的整体效果。我们相反将分词器构建形式化为线性规划问题，并使用凸优化工具求解，从而提出一种新算法，称为ConvexTok。我们发现ConvexTok在分词的内在指标和语言模型实现的每字位数（BpB）上持续提升；它也改善下游任务表现，但效果不一致。此外，ConvexTok允许用户通过下界认证其分词器相对于特定目标的最优程度，实证表明在常见词汇规模下，其性能接近最优值的1%以内。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of greedy tokenisation methods like BPE and Unigram, which optimize locally without considering global vocabulary efficiency, this work reformulates tokeniser construction as a linear program solvable via convex optimisation, introducing ConvexTok. Experiments show ConvexTok consistently outperforms existing methods on intrinsic tokenisation metrics and bits-per-byte in language models, with modest improvements in downstream tasks. Additionally, ConvexTok provides a certified lower bound on optimality, empirically within 1% of the theoretical optimum for typical vocabulary sizes.
鉴于BPE和Unigram等贪心式分词方法仅局部优化而忽视整体词汇表质量，本文将分词器构建建模为线性规划问题并借助凸优化求解，提出ConvexTok算法。实验表明，ConvexTok在分词内在指标和语言模型的bits-per-byte上持续优于现有方法，下游任务性能提升虽不显著但可测量。此外，ConvexTok可提供针对特定目标的最优性下界，实验证明其在常见词汇规模下距离最优解不超过1%。
Which Way Did It Move? Diagnosing and Overcoming Directional Motion Blindness in Video-LLMs
Authors: Jongseo Lee, Hyuntak Lee, Sunghun Kim, Sooa Kim, Jihoon Chung, Jinwoo Choi
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 59 pages, including appendix. Code: https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have made rapid progress on temporal video understanding, yet many fail at a basic perceptual primitive: signed image-plane motion direction. On simple videos of a single object moving left, right, up, or down, most Video-LLMs perform near chance, with above-chance cases largely attributable to prediction biases rather than genuine direction understanding. We call this failure directional motion blindness. We localize the failure by tracing motion direction information through the Video-LLM pipeline. Motion direction remains linearly accessible from the vision encoder, projector, and LLM hidden states, but the readout fails to bind this signal to the correct verbal answer option, revealing a direction binding gap. Although synthetic motion direction instruction tuning reduces this gap on the source domain, motion direction concept vector analysis shows that visual complexity weakens the signal magnitude and limits out-of-domain generalization. We introduce MoDirect, a dataset family for motion direction instruction tuning and evaluation, and DeltaDirect, a diagnosis-driven, projector-level objective that predicts normalized 2-D motion vectors from adjacent-frame feature deltas. On MoDirect-SynBench, instruction tuning with DeltaDirect improves motion direction accuracy from 25.9% to 85.4%. On MoDirect-RealBench, DeltaDirect improves real-world motion direction accuracy by 21.9 points over the vanilla baseline without real-world tuning data, while preserving standard video-understanding performance. Code: https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
中文标题/摘要
标题：它向哪个方向移动了？诊断并克服视频大语言模型中的方向运动盲
视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）在时序视频理解方面取得了快速进展，但许多模型在基本的感知原语——带符号的图像平面运动方向上表现失败。在单个物体向左、右、上或下移动的简单视频中，大多数Video-LLMs的表现接近随机水平，高于随机水平的情况主要归因于预测偏差而非真正的方向理解。我们将这种失败称为方向运动盲。我们通过追踪运动方向信息在Video-LLM管道中的传递来定位失败原因。运动方向信息在视觉编码器、投影器和LLM隐藏状态中仍保持线性可访问性，但读出层未能将该信号与正确的口头答案选项绑定，揭示了方向绑定的缺口。尽管合成运动方向指令微调能在源域中减小该缺口，但运动方向概念向量分析表明，视觉复杂性会削弱信号强度并限制跨域泛化能力。我们引入MoDirect，一个用于运动方向指令微调与评估的数据集家族，并提出DeltaDirect，一种诊断驱动、投影器级别的目标函数，可从相邻帧特征差值预测归一化的二维运动向量。在MoDirect-SynBench上，使用DeltaDirect进行指令微调可将运动方向准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%。在MoDirect-RealBench上，DeltaDirect在无需真实世界微调数据的情况下，将真实世界运动方向准确率提升21.9个百分点，同时保持标准视频理解性能不变。代码：https://github.com/KHU-VLL/DeltaDirect
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the failure of Video-LLMs to correctly perceive signed motion direction in videos, a basic perceptual task they often perform at chance levels. The authors diagnose this as 'directional motion blindness,' tracing the failure through the model pipeline and identifying a 'direction binding gap' where motion signals are preserved in early layers but fail to map correctly to verbal outputs. To remedy this, they introduce DeltaDirect, a projector-level objective that predicts normalized 2D motion vectors from frame differences, and MoDirect, a new dataset family for instruction tuning. Experiments show DeltaDirect boosts motion direction accuracy from 25.9% to 85.4% on synthetic data and improves real-world performance by 21.9 points without requiring real-world tuning data, while maintaining general video understanding.
该论文指出视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）在感知图像平面中物体运动方向（左、右、上、下）时存在严重缺陷，称为“方向运动盲”，多数模型表现接近随机。作者通过追踪模型内部流程发现，运动方向信息在视觉编码器、投影器和语言模型隐藏层中仍可线性获取，但最终输出层未能正确绑定该信号至正确答案，形成“方向绑定缺口”。为诊断并解决此问题，作者提出DeltaDirect方法，通过相邻帧特征差值预测归一化二维运动向量，并构建MoDirect数据集用于指令调优。实验表明，DeltaDirect在合成数据集上将方向识别准确率从25.9%提升至85.4%，在真实世界数据上提升21.9个百分点，且未使用真实数据调优时仍保持标准视频理解能力。
Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials
Authors: Carlos Heredia, Daniel Roncel
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: 44 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过神经需求势实现可积弹性
我们提出了可积上下文依赖需求网络（ICDN），这是一种以需求为导向的神经模型，用于多产品零售需求预测。该模型将对数需求学习为对数价格的平滑、上下文条件函数，从而能从所学需求曲面上精确推导出弹性。在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN在样本外泛化能力上优于定向对数-对数基准模型，并产生更稳定、更具经济合理性的弹性估计，尤其对交叉价格效应的弱识别情况表现更佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and stable elasticity estimation in retail demand modeling, the authors introduce the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a neural model that directly learns log-demand as a smooth function of log-prices conditioned on contextual features. This formulation enables exact derivation of elasticities from the learned demand surface, avoiding approximation errors common in traditional methods. Experiments on the Dominick's beer dataset show that ICDN outperforms a directed log-log benchmark in out-of-sample generalization and produces more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, particularly for cross-price effects that are otherwise weakly identified.
为解决多产品零售需求建模中弹性估计不准确且不稳定的问题，作者提出了可积上下文依赖需求网络（ICDN），该模型将需求对数作为价格对数的平滑函数进行学习，并以上下文特征为条件。模型结构确保弹性可从学习到的需求曲面精确推导，避免近似误差。在Dominick's啤酒数据集上的实验表明，ICDN在样本外泛化能力上优于标准的有向对数-对数基准模型，并能生成更稳定、更符合经济现实的弹性估计，尤其在交叉价格弹性较弱时表现突出。
Cambrian-P: Pose-Grounded Video Understanding
Authors: Jihan Yang, Zifan Zhao, Xichen Pan, Shusheng Yang, Junyi Zhang, Bingyi Kang, Hu Xu, Saining Xie
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:45+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cambrian-mllm.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Camera pose matters. The position and orientation of each viewpoint define a shared spatial coordinate frame that relates observations across video frames. Yet this signal is largely absent from multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) for video understanding, which process frames as isolated 2D snapshots, instead of the persistent scene humans perceive. We revisit pose as a lightweight supervisory signal and introduce Cambrian-P, a video MLLM augmented with per-frame learnable camera tokens and a pose regression head. With a carefully designed sampling scheme, the model achieves substantial gains of 4.5-6.5% on spatial reasoning benchmarks such as VSI-Bench, generalizes across eight additional spatial and general video QA benchmarks, and, as a byproduct, achieves state of the art streaming pose estimation on ScanNet. Surprisingly, training on pseudo-annotated poses from in-the-wild video further improves general video QA benchmarks, showing pose helps beyond spatial reasoning. Together, these results position camera pose as a fundamental signal for video models that reason about the physical world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：寒武纪-P：基于姿态的视频理解
摄像机姿态至关重要。每个视角的位置和朝向定义了一个共享的空间坐标系，关联视频帧间的观测。然而，多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视频理解中大多忽略这一信号，将帧视为孤立的2D快照，而非人类感知的持续场景。我们重新审视姿态作为轻量级监督信号，提出寒武纪-P，一种增强的视频MLLM，包含每帧可学习的摄像机标记和姿态回归头。通过精心设计的采样方案，该模型在空间推理基准（如VSI-Bench）上提升4.5-6.5%，在八个额外的空间与通用视频问答基准上泛化良好，并作为副产品，在ScanNet上实现最先进的流式姿态估计。令人惊讶的是，使用野外视频的伪标注姿态训练，进一步提升了通用视频问答基准表现，表明姿态对空间推理之外的领域也有帮助。这些结果将摄像机姿态定位为视频模型理解物理世界时的基本信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the absence of camera pose information in multimodal LLMs for video understanding, which treats frames as isolated 2D images, the authors introduce Cambrian-P, a video MLLM enhanced with per-frame learnable camera tokens and a pose regression head. Through a carefully designed sampling strategy, the model achieves 4.5-6.5% performance gains on spatial reasoning benchmarks like VSI-Bench, generalizes across eight additional video QA tasks, and incidentally sets a new state-of-the-art in streaming pose estimation on ScanNet. Notably, training with pseudo-annotated poses from real-world videos further boosts general video QA performance, indicating that pose signals benefit reasoning beyond spatial tasks.
为弥补视频理解中多模态大语言模型缺乏相机位姿信息的缺陷（当前将帧视为孤立的2D图像），作者提出Cambrian-P，通过为每帧添加可学习的相机标记和位姿回归头增强视频MLLM。借助精心设计的采样策略，该模型在VSI-Bench等空间推理基准上提升4.5–6.5%，并在八项其他空间与通用视频问答任务中实现泛化，同时意外在ScanNet上达到流式位姿估计的最先进水平。进一步使用野外视频的伪标注位姿训练，还能提升通用视频问答性能，表明位姿信号对推理任务具有超越空间推理的价值。
MotiMotion: Motion-Controlled Video Generation with Visual Reasoning
Authors: Lee Hsin-Ying, Hanwen Jiang, Yiqun Mei, Jing Shi, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Zhixin Shu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://motimotion.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current motion-controlled image-to-video generation models rigidly follow user-provided trajectories that are often sparse, imprecise, and causally incomplete. Such reliance often yields unnatural or implausible outcomes, especially by missing secondary causal consequences. To address this, we introduce MotiMotion, a novel framework that reformulates motion control as a reasoning-then-generation problem. To encourage causally grounded and commonsense-consistent interactions, we leverage a training-free vision-language reasoner to refine image-space coordinates of primary trajectories and to hallucinate plausible secondary motions. To further improve motion naturalness, we propose a confidence-aware control scheme that modulates guidance strength, enabling the model to closely follow high-confidence plans while correcting artifacts under low-confidence inputs with its internal generative priors. To support systematic evaluation, we curate a new image-to-video benchmark, MotiBench, consisting of interaction-centric scenes where new events are triggered by motion. Both VLM-based evaluation and a human study on MotiBench demonstrate that MotiMotion produces videos with more plausible object behaviors and interaction, and is preferred over existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MotiMotion：基于视觉推理的运动控制视频生成
当前的运动控制图像到视频生成模型严格遵循用户提供的轨迹，这些轨迹往往稀疏、不精确且因果不完整。这种依赖常导致不自然或不可信的结果，尤其因忽略次级因果后果。为解决此问题，我们提出MotiMotion框架，将运动控制重构为推理后生成问题。为促进因果基础且常识一致的交互，我们利用无训练视觉-语言推理器优化主轨迹的图像空间坐标，并生成合理的次级运动。为进一步提升运动自然性，我们提出一种置信度感知控制方案，调节引导强度，使模型在高置信度输入下紧密跟随计划，而在低置信度输入下利用内部生成先验修正伪影。为支持系统性评估，我们构建了新图像到视频基准MotiBench，包含以交互为中心的场景，其中新事件由运动触发。基于VLM的评估和MotiBench上的人类研究均表明，MotiMotion生成的视频具有更合理的物体行为与交互，且优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
MotiMotion addresses the limitation of current motion-controlled video generation models that rigidly follow sparse, imprecise, and causally incomplete user trajectories, often resulting in unnatural outcomes by ignoring secondary causal effects. The framework reformulates motion control as a reasoning-then-generation task, using a training-free vision-language reasoner to refine primary motion coordinates and hallucinate plausible secondary motions grounded in commonsense. A confidence-aware control mechanism modulates guidance strength to prioritize high-confidence motion plans while correcting artifacts using generative priors for low-confidence inputs. Evaluated on a new benchmark, MotiBench, which features motion-triggered interactions, both VLM-based metrics and human studies confirm that MotiMotion generates videos with more plausible object behaviors and interactions, outperforming existing methods.
MotiMotion 解决了当前运动控制视频生成中因轨迹稀疏、因果不完整导致的不自然结果问题，通过将运动控制重构为先推理后生成的任务，利用无训练视觉-语言模型优化主轨迹并生成符合因果常识的次级运动。为提升运动自然性，引入置信度感知控制机制，根据轨迹置信度动态调节引导强度。在新构建的 MotiBench 数据集（聚焦运动触发事件）上，该方法在物体行为与交互合理性方面显著优于现有方法，且经视觉-语言模型评估与人类偏好测试验证。
Vector Policy Optimization: Training for Diversity Improves Test-Time Search
Authors: Ryan Bahlous-Boldi, Isha Puri, Idan Shenfeld, Akarsh Kumar, Mehul Damani, Sebastian Risi, Omar Khattab, Zhang-Wei Hong, Pulkit Agrawal
First: 2026-05-21T17:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:59:26+00:00
Comments: 24 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language models must now generalize out of the box to novel environments and work inside inference-scaling search procedures, such as AlphaEvolve, that select rollouts with a variety of task-specific reward functions. Unfortunately, the standard paradigm of LLM post-training optimizes a pre-specified scalar reward, often leading current LLMs to produce low-entropy response distributions and thus to struggle at displaying the diversity that inference-time search will require. We propose Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL algorithm that explicitly trains policies to anticipate diverse downstream reward functions and to produce diverse solutions. VPO exploits that rewards are often vector-valued in practice, like per-test-case correctness in code generation or, say, multiple different user personas or reward models. VPO is essentially a drop-in replacement for the GRPO advantage estimator, but it trains the LLM to output a set of solutions where individual solutions specialize to different trade-offs in the vector reward space. Across four tasks, VPO matches or beats the strongest scalar RL baselines on test-time search (e.g. pass@k and best@k), with the gap widening as the search budget grows. For evolutionary search, VPO models unlock problems that GRPO models cannot solve at all. As test-time search becomes more standardized, optimizing for diversity may need to become the default post-training objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：向量策略优化：训练多样性提升测试时搜索性能
语言模型如今必须在未经训练的情况下泛化到新环境，并在推理扩展搜索过程中（如AlphaEvolve）工作，该过程通过多种任务特定奖励函数选择 rollout。不幸的是，标准的LLM后训练范式优化预设标量奖励，常导致当前LLM生成低熵响应分布，从而难以在推理时搜索中展示所需多样性。我们提出向量策略优化（VPO），一种强化学习算法，明确训练策略以预测多样化的下游奖励函数并生成多样化的解决方案。VPO利用实际奖励常为向量值的特点，如代码生成中的每个测试用例正确性，或多种用户角色/奖励模型。VPO本质上是GRPO优势估计器的即插即用替代方案，但训练LLM输出一组解决方案，其中每个解决方案在向量奖励空间中专门针对不同权衡。在四个任务中，VPO在测试时搜索（如pass@k和best@k）上匹配或超越最强标量RL基线，且随着搜索预算增加，差距扩大。对于进化搜索，VPO模型能解决GRPO模型根本无法解决的问题。随着测试时搜索日益标准化，多样性优化或需成为后训练的默认目标。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for language models to generalize to novel environments and support diverse reward functions during inference-time search, the paper introduces Vector Policy Optimization (VPO), an RL method that trains policies to anticipate and generate diverse solutions by leveraging vector-valued rewards. VPO replaces the scalar reward estimator in GRPO with a vector-based one, encouraging the model to produce multiple specialized outputs that cover different trade-offs in reward space. Experiments across four tasks show VPO matches or outperforms scalar RL baselines on test-time search metrics like pass@k and best@k, with performance gaps widening as search budgets increase; notably, VPO enables evolutionary search where GRPO fails entirely.
为使语言模型能适应新环境并支持推理时的多样化奖励函数搜索，本文提出向量策略优化（VPO）方法，通过训练策略以应对向量奖励并生成多样化解决方案。VPO将传统标量奖励优化替换为向量奖励框架，使模型能输出在不同奖励权衡中特化的多样化结果。在四个任务上的实验表明，VPO在pass@k和best@k等推理搜索指标上表现与标量RL基线持平或更优，且随着搜索预算增加，优势更明显。VPO还能解决GRPO模型无法处理的进化搜索问题，表明多样性优化应成为默认的后训练目标。
AwareVLN: Reasoning with Self-awareness for Vision-Language Navigation
Authors: Wenxuan Guo, Xiuwei Xu, Yichen Liu, Xiangyu Li, Hang Yin, Huangxing Chen, Wenzhao Zheng, Jianjiang Feng, Jie Zhou, Jiwen Lu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:58:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:58:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted to CVPR 2026. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to ground language instructions to its own movement within a visual environment. While state-of-the-art methods leverage the reasoning capabilities of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for end-to-end action prediction, they often lack an explicit and explainable understanding of the relationships between the agent, the instruction, and the scene. Conversely, explicitly building a scene map for heuristic planning is intuitively appealing but relies on additional 3D sensors and hinders large-scale vision-language pre-training. To bridge this gap, we propose AwareVLN, a novel framework that equips the navigation model with a self-aware reasoning mechanism, enabling it to understand the agent's state and task progress in a fully end-to-end and data-driven manner. Our approach features two key innovations: (1) a structural reasoning module that fosters spatial and task-oriented self-awareness, and (2) an automatic data engine with progress division for effective training. Extensive experiments on various datasets in Habitat simulator show our AwareVLN significantly outperforms previous state-of-the-art vision-language navigation methods. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AwareVLN：面向视觉-语言导航的自感知推理
视觉-语言导航（VLN）要求代理将语言指令与自身在视觉环境中的运动关联起来。虽然当前最先进的方法利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的推理能力进行端到端动作预测，但它们往往缺乏对代理、指令与场景之间关系的显式且可解释的理解。相反，显式构建场景地图以进行启发式规划虽然直观，但依赖额外的3D传感器，且阻碍大规模视觉-语言预训练。为弥合这一差距，我们提出AwareVLN，一种新颖框架，为导航模型配备自感知推理机制，使其能够以端到端、数据驱动的方式理解代理状态和任务进展。我们的方法包含两项关键创新：（1）促进空间与任务导向自感知的结构化推理模块；（2）具备进度划分的自动数据引擎，以实现高效训练。在Habitat模拟器上的多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，AwareVLN显著优于先前最先进的视觉-语言导航方法。项目页面：https://gwxuan.github.io/AwareVLN/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for explainable and self-aware reasoning in vision-language navigation, AwareVLN introduces a self-aware mechanism that enables agents to understand their own state and task progress without relying on external 3D sensors. The method combines a structural reasoning module to encode spatial and task-aware self-awareness with an automatic data engine that divides training progress for improved learning efficiency. Experiments on Habitat simulator datasets demonstrate that AwareVLN consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods in navigation accuracy and robustness across multiple benchmarks.
为提升视觉语言导航中的可解释性和自感知能力，AwareVLN 提出一种自感知机制，使导航代理无需依赖外部3D传感器即可理解自身状态与任务进度。该方法结合结构化推理模块以增强空间与任务导向的自感知能力，并配备自动数据引擎以按进度划分训练数据，提升学习效率。在Habitat模拟器多个数据集上的实验表明，AwareVLN 显著优于现有最先进方法，验证了其在端到端数据驱动导航中的有效性。
Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration
Authors: Lily Goli, Justin Kerr, Daniele Reda, Alec Jacobson, Andrea Tagliasacchi, Angjoo Kanazawa
First: 2026-05-21T17:58:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:58:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保持好奇心：用于3D探索的 episodic 上下文与持久世界
在稀疏奖励、长时域任务中，探索是学习有用行为的前提，尤其是在3D环境中。好奇心驱动的强化学习通过从代理对世界预测模型与现实之间的不匹配中获得内在奖励来解决这一问题。然而，将这种内在动机迁移到复杂、逼真的环境中仍很困难，因为代理可能陷入局部循环，并在重新访问已遗忘状态时获得新的奖励。本研究证明，这种失败源于缺乏空间持久性和 episodic 上下文。我们表明，有效的探索需要一个持续更新的世界模型，以及一个能维护 episodic 轨迹历史的代理，以便导航至新区域。我们通过在线3D重建作为持久世界模型，同时将代理策略参数化为RGB观测序列模型以保持 episodic 上下文。该设计在训练期间实现有效探索，同时在部署时仅使用RGB帧进行导航。仅在HM3D上通过好奇心训练，我们的代理优于基于强化学习的主动映射基线，并能零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成的世界。我们的端到端策略可高效适应下游任务，如摘苹果和图像目标导航，优于从零开始的基线。请参见视频结果：https://recuriosity.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of effective exploration in sparse-reward 3D environments, this work proposes that curiosity-driven agents fail due to insufficient spatial persistence and episodic context. The method combines an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent world model with a sequence-based policy that retains episodic trajectory history from RGB observations, enabling the agent to navigate toward novel regions. Experiments show that this approach outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines on HM3D and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds, while also achieving superior performance on downstream tasks like apple picking and image-goal navigation without additional training.
为解决稀疏奖励三维环境中探索效率低的问题，该研究指出好奇心驱动的智能体失败源于空间持久性不足和事件上下文缺失。方法结合在线三维重建作为世界持久模型，同时使用基于序列的策略通过RGB图像保持轨迹历史。实验表明，该设计在训练中实现有效探索，并能零样本泛化至Gibson和AI生成环境，优于基于强化学习的主动建图基线。其端到端策略在下游任务如摘苹果和图像目标导航中表现优异，超越从零开始的基线方法。
GesVLA: Gesture-Aware Vision-Language-Action Model Embedded Representations
Authors: Wenxuan Guo, Ziyuan Li, Meng Zhang, Yichen Liu, Yimeng Dong, Chuxi Xu, Yunfei Wei, Ze Chen, Erjin Zhou, Jianjiang Feng
First: 2026-05-21T17:57:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:57:44+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general-purpose robot manipulation by unifying perception and action. However, existing VLA systems primarily rely on textual instructions and struggle to resolve spatial ambiguity in complex scenes with multiple similar objects. To address this limitation, we introduce gesture as a parallel instruction modality and propose a Gesture-aware Vision-Language-Action model (GesVLA). Our approach encodes gesture features directly into the latent space, enabling them to participate in both high-level reasoning and low-level action generation, and adopts a dual-VLM architecture to achieve tight coupling between gesture representations and action policies. At the data level, we construct a scalable gesture data generation pipeline by rendering hand models onto real-world scene images. This reduces the sim-to-real visual gap while producing rich data with diverse motion patterns and corresponding pointing annotations. In addition, we employ a two-stage training strategy to equip the model with both gesture perception and action prediction capabilities. We evaluate our approach on multiple real-world robotic tasks, including a controlled block manipulation task for validation and more practical scenarios such as product and produce selection. Experimental results show that incorporating gesture consistently improves target grounding accuracy and human-robot interaction efficiency, especially in complex and cluttered environments. Project page: https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GesVLA：基于手势感知的视觉-语言-动作模型嵌入表示
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过统一感知与动作，在通用机器人操作中展现出强大潜力。然而，现有VLA系统主要依赖文本指令，难以解决复杂场景中多个相似物体的空间歧义问题。为此，我们引入手势作为并行指令模态，提出手势感知视觉-语言-动作模型（GesVLA）。该方法直接将手势特征编码至潜在空间，使其参与高层推理与底层动作生成，并采用双视觉语言模型架构，实现手势表征与动作策略的紧密耦合。在数据层面，我们通过将手部模型渲染至真实场景图像，构建可扩展的手势数据生成流水线，有效缩小仿真到现实的视觉差距，同时生成包含多样运动模式及对应指向标注的丰富数据。此外，我们采用两阶段训练策略，使模型兼具手势感知与动作预测能力。我们在多个真实机器人任务中评估该方法，包括用于验证的受控积木操作任务及更实用的场景如商品与农产品选择。实验结果表明，引入手势可持续提升目标定位准确率与人机交互效率，尤其在复杂杂乱环境中表现突出。项目页面：https://gwxuan.github.io/GesVLA/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing Vision-Language-Action models in resolving spatial ambiguity through text alone, this work introduces GesVLA, a gesture-aware model that integrates hand gestures as a parallel instruction modality. It encodes gesture features directly into the latent space to enable joint reasoning and action generation, using a dual-VLM architecture to tightly couple gesture representations with action policies. The model is trained via a two-stage strategy and fed with synthetic gesture data generated by rendering hand models onto real-world images, reducing sim-to-real gaps. Experiments on robotic tasks like block manipulation and product selection demonstrate that gesture integration significantly improves target grounding accuracy and interaction efficiency, especially in cluttered environments.
为解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型仅依赖文本指令难以处理复杂场景中空间歧义的问题，本文提出GesVLA模型，将手势作为并行指令模态引入。该模型直接将手势特征编码进潜在空间，使其参与高层推理与底层动作生成，并采用双视觉语言模型架构实现手势表征与动作策略的紧密耦合。通过在真实场景图像上渲染手部模型构建数据生成流水线，有效缩小仿真到现实的视觉差距，并生成包含多样运动模式与指向标注的丰富数据。采用两阶段训练策略使模型同时掌握手势感知与动作预测能力。在积木操作与真实产品选择等任务中，实验表明引入手势显著提升目标定位准确率与人机交互效率，尤其在杂乱环境中表现更优。
Sensor2Sensor: Cross-Embodiment Sensor Conversion for Autonomous Driving
Authors: Jiahao Wang, Bo Sun, Yijing Bai, Vincent Casser, Songyou Peng, Zehao Zhu, Meng-Li Shih, Xander Masotto, Shih-Yang Su, Kanaad V Parvate, Tiancheng Ge, Linn Bieske, Dragomir Anguelov, Mingxing Tan, Chiyu Max Jiang
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:57:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:57:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust training and validation of Autonomous Driving Systems (ADS) require massive, diverse datasets. Proprietary data collected by Autonomous Vehicle (AV) fleets, while high-fidelity, are limited in scale, diversity of sensor configurations, as well as geographic and long-tail-behavioral coverage. In contrast, in-the-wild data from sources like dashcams offers immense scale and diversity, capturing critical long-tail scenarios and novel environments. However, this unstructured, in-the-wild video data is incompatible with ADS expecting structured, multi-modal sensor inputs for validation and training. To bridge this data gap, we propose Sensor2Sensor, a novel generative modeling paradigm that translates in-the-wild monocular dashcam videos into a high-fidelity, multi-modal sensor suite (AV logs) comprising multi-view camera images and LiDAR point clouds. A core challenge is the lack of paired training data. We address this by converting real AV logs into dashcam-style videos via 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) reconstruction and novel-view rendering. Sensor2Sensor then utilizes a diffusion architecture to perform the generative conversion. We perform comprehensive quantitative evaluations on the fidelity and realism of the generated sensor data. We demonstrate Sensor2Sensor's practical utility by converting challenging in-the-wild internet and dashcam footage into realistic, multi-modal data formats, further unlocking vast external data sources for AV development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sensor2Sensor：面向自动驾驶的跨载体传感器转换
自动驾驶系统（ADS）的稳健训练与验证需要海量、多样化的数据集。虽然自动驾驶车队采集的专有数据具有高保真度，但其规模有限，传感器配置多样性不足，且地理覆盖和长尾行为覆盖不全。相比之下，来自行车记录仪等野外数据源的数据规模庞大、多样性高，能捕捉关键长尾场景和新环境。然而，这类无结构的野外视频数据与ADS期望的结构化、多模态传感器输入不兼容。为弥合这一数据鸿沟，我们提出Sensor2Sensor，一种新颖的生成建模范式，可将野外单目行车记录仪视频转换为高保真、多模态传感器套件（AV日志），包含多视角相机图像和激光雷达点云。核心挑战在于缺乏配对训练数据。我们通过4D高斯点云（4DGS）重建及新视角渲染，将真实AV日志转换为行车记录仪风格视频，从而解决该问题。Sensor2Sensor随后利用扩散架构执行生成转换。我们在生成传感器数据的保真度与真实感方面进行了全面定量评估，并通过将挑战性野外互联网及行车记录仪视频转换为真实多模态数据格式，展示了其实际应用价值，进一步释放了大量外部数据源用于自动驾驶开发。
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of diverse, large-scale sensor data for autonomous driving systems, the paper proposes Sensor2Sensor, a generative model that converts unstructured dashcam videos into high-fidelity multi-modal sensor data (including multi-view cameras and LiDAR point clouds). The method tackles the lack of paired training data by first reconstructing real autonomous vehicle logs into dashcam-style videos using 4D Gaussian Splatting and novel-view rendering, then applying a diffusion-based architecture to perform the conversion. Experimental results show that the generated sensor data achieves high fidelity and realism, successfully transforming challenging in-the-wild footage into usable multi-modal formats for training and validation.
为解决自动驾驶系统训练所需大规模、多样化传感器数据稀缺的问题，Sensor2Sensor提出一种生成模型，将无结构的行车记录仪视频转化为高保真多模态传感器数据，包括多视角图像与激光雷达点云。该方法通过4D高斯点渲染技术将真实自动驾驶日志重建为行车记录仪风格视频，再结合扩散模型完成转换，从而解决配对训练数据不足的问题。实验表明，生成的传感器数据在真实性和结构准确性上表现良好，可将野外复杂视频转化为可用的多模态数据，有效拓展自动驾驶开发的数据来源。
Large-scale Score-based Variational Posterior Inference for Bayesian Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Minyoung Kim
First: 2026-02-05T16:51:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:55:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian (deep) neural networks (BNN) are often more attractive than the vanilla point-estimate deep learning in various aspects including uncertainty quantification, robustness to noise, resistance to overfitting, and more. The variational inference (VI) is one of the most widely adopted approximate inference methods. Whereas the ELBO-based variational free energy method is a dominant choice in the literature, in this paper we introduce a score-based alternative for BNN variational inference. Score-based VI can address the known issue of mode collapsing in ELBO-based VI. Although several score-based VI methods have been proposed in the community, most are not adequate for large-scale BNNs for various computational and technical reasons. We propose a novel scalable VI method where the learning objective combines the score matching loss and the proximal penalty term in iterations, which helps our method avoid the reparametrized sampling, and allows for noisy unbiased mini-batch scores through stochastic gradients. This in turn makes our method scalable to large-scale neural networks including Vision Transformers. On several benchmarks including visual recognition and time-series forecasting with large-scale deep networks, we empirically show the effectiveness of our approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于贝叶斯深度神经网络的基于大规模得分的变分后验推断
贝叶斯（深度）神经网络（BNN）在不确定性量化、抗噪性、抗过拟合等方面通常比标准点估计深度学习更具吸引力。变分推断（VI）是最广泛采用的近似推断方法之一。虽然基于ELBO的变分自由能方法在文献中占主导地位，本文提出了一种基于得分的替代方法用于BNN变分推断。基于得分的VI可解决基于ELBO的VI中已知的模式坍缩问题。尽管社区已提出多种基于得分的VI方法，但大多数因计算和技术原因难以适用于大规模BNN。我们提出了一种新颖的可扩展VI方法，其学习目标结合了得分匹配损失和迭代中的近端惩罚项，从而避免重参数化采样，并允许通过随机梯度获得噪声无偏的小批量得分。这使得我们的方法可扩展至包括视觉变换器在内的大规模神经网络。在多个基准测试（包括视觉识别和大规模深度网络的时间序列预测）中，我们实证展示了该方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable Bayesian deep neural networks that avoid mode collapse inherent in ELBO-based variational inference, this paper proposes a score-based variational inference method tailored for large-scale models. The approach combines score matching loss with a proximal penalty term to enable training without reparametrized sampling and to support noisy, unbiased mini-batch gradients, thus improving scalability. Experiments on visual recognition and time-series forecasting benchmarks with large deep networks demonstrate that the method achieves competitive or superior performance compared to ELBO-based baselines, while maintaining computational efficiency.
为解决ELBO变分推断在贝叶斯深度神经网络中易出现的模式坍塌问题，本文提出一种基于分数匹配的可扩展变分推断方法。该方法结合分数匹配损失与近端惩罚项，避免重参数化采样并支持使用噪声无偏的小批量梯度，从而适用于大规模模型如视觉Transformer。在视觉识别与时间序列预测等多个基准测试中，实验表明该方法在性能上可媲美或优于现有方法，同时保持计算效率。
The Matching Principle: A Geometric Theory of Loss Functions for Nuisance-Robust Representation Learning
Authors: Vishal Rajput
First: 2026-05-21T17:53:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:53:28+00:00
Comments: 54 pages. 13 empirical task blocks. Companion software: matching-pmh (PyPI; https://github.com/vishalstark512/matching-pmh). Related arXiv note: 2604.21395 (geometric blind spot / isotropic PMH)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Robustness, domain adaptation, photometric and occlusion invariance, compositional generalisation, temporal robustness, alignment safety, and classical anisotropic regularisation are usually treated as separate problems with separate method families. This paper argues that much of their shared structure is one statistical problem: estimate the covariance of label-preserving deployment nuisance, then regularise the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range covers that covariance (the matching principle). CORAL, adversarial training, IRM, augmentation, metric learning, Jacobian penalties, and alignment-style constraints are different estimators of that object, not independent robustness tricks. In the linear-Gaussian model we prove closed-form optimality (Theorem A), including cube-root water-filling within the matched range; necessity of range coverage for quadratic Jacobian penalties (Theorem G); the same range dichotomy at deep global minima; and two falsification controls (Lemma C; Corollaries E), with seven conditional consistency lemmas (D1-D7) for estimation under standard identifiability assumptions. We introduce the Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI), a label-free probe of embedding sensitivity when task accuracy or Jacobian Frobenius norm is insufficient. Thirteen pre-registered blocks from classical ML through Qwen2.5-7B test the predicted matched, then isotropic, then wrong-W ordering on geometry and deployment drift; twelve pass, and the sole exception (Office-31) is an eigengap failure named before the run. At 7B scale, matched style-PMH improves selective honesty and preserves Style TDI where standard DPO degrades it. The contribution is naming the deployment nuisance covariance, stating what the regulariser must do, and supplying a closed-form falsifiable theory once that object is identified, not universality on every leaderboard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：匹配原理：一种针对干扰鲁棒表示学习的几何损失函数理论
鲁棒性、领域自适应、光度与遮挡不变性、组合泛化、时间鲁棒性、对齐安全性及经典各向异性正则化通常被视为独立问题，各自拥有不同方法体系。本文主张，它们的许多共同结构本质上是同一统计问题：估计标签保持部署干扰的协方差，然后沿覆盖该协方差的矩阵正则化编码器雅可比矩阵（即匹配原理）。CORAL、对抗训练、IRM、数据增强、度量学习、雅可比惩罚及对齐式约束，都是该对象的不同估计器，而非独立的鲁棒性技巧。 在线性高斯模型中，我们证明了闭式最优性（定理A），包括匹配范围内立方根水填充；二次雅可比惩罚需覆盖该范围的必要性（定理G）；深度全局最小值处的相同范围二分性；以及两个伪证控制（引理C；推论E），并提供七个条件一致性引理（D1-D7），用于在标准可识别性假设下进行估计。 我们引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI），作为一种无需标签的嵌入敏感性探测器，当任务准确率或雅可比Frobenius范数不足以反映敏感性时使用。 十三个预注册的经典ML至Qwen2.5-7B任务块，测试了预测匹配、等向及错误-W排序在几何与部署漂移下的表现；十二个通过，唯一例外（Office-31）是此前已知的特征间隙失败。在7B规模下，匹配风格PMH提升选择性诚实性，并在标准DPO退化处保持Style TDI。 本文贡献在于命名部署干扰协方差、明确正则化器必须执行的操作，并在识别该对象后提供一个闭式可证伪理论，而非声称在所有排行榜上普适。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that multiple robustness techniques in representation learning—such as domain adaptation, occlusion invariance, and compositional generalization—stem from a shared geometric principle: regularize the encoder Jacobian along a matrix whose range matches the covariance of deployment nuisances that preserve labels. The authors formalize this as the 'matching principle' and prove its optimality in linear-Gaussian models, showing that Jacobian penalties must cover the full nuisance covariance for quadratic penalties to be effective. Empirical tests across 13 tasks confirm that methods aligned with this principle outperform isotropic or mismatched approaches, with the only failure (Office-31) attributed to an eigengap issue. The Trajectory Deviation Index (TDI) is introduced as a label-free metric to probe embedding sensitivity, and at 7B scale, matched-style PMH improves alignment safety while preserving TDI, unlike standard DPO.
本文提出“匹配原则”作为鲁棒表征学习的统一几何框架，认为多种鲁棒方法（如CORAL、IRM、对抗训练）本质上都在估计部署扰动的协方差并使编码器雅可比矩阵沿该协方差范围进行正则化。作者在线性高斯模型中推导出闭式理论保证，证明雅可比正则化在范围覆盖下的最优性，并提供可证伪的一致性引理。实验证明，在13个从经典ML到Qwen2.5-7B的预注册任务中，匹配正则化优于各向同性或错误匹配方案，12个任务表现稳定，唯一例外（Office-31）源于特征值间隙失效。引入轨迹偏差指数（TDI）作为无标签的敏感性探测器，且在7B规模下，匹配式PMH能提升选择性诚实度并保持TDI，而标准DPO则使其退化。
Symphony for Speech-to-Text: Supporting Real-Time Medical Voice Interfaces
Authors: Arne Nix, Robert James, Lasse Borgholt, Anna B. Ekner, Lana Krumm, Julius Severin, Dan Engel, Lars Maaløe, Jakob Havtorn
First: 2026-05-15T18:39:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:52:56+00:00
Comments: Updated with a correction and improvement to Symphony's performance in spoken punctuation evaluation (R_punct, P_punct)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
After decades of use in dictation and, more recently, ambient documentation, speech is emerging as a primary modality for interacting with technology and AI in healthcare. Yet medical speech recognition remains difficult: systems must capture specialized terminology, resolve contextual ambiguity, and render measurements, abbreviations, and clinical shorthand precisely. Existing solutions are typically optimized either for general-purpose transcription or narrow dictation workflows, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings and their usefulness for broader clinical workflows. We introduce Symphony for Speech-to-Text, a medical-grade speech recognition system for real-time streaming and batch file-based clinical use. Symphony decomposes the transcription process into specialized components for recognition, formatting, and contextual correction to optimize medical term recall while producing clinically structured text in real time and adapting across use cases. Evaluations on public benchmark and medical speech datasets show that Symphony substantially outperforms state-of-the-art systems in clinical settings while matching or exceeding them in general-domain settings, suggesting robust generalization rather than overfitting. We release a clinical benchmark dataset to support reliable validation and further progress in medical speech recognition. Symphony is available through a production-grade API for live dictation, conversational transcription, and batch audio file processing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向医疗语音界面的实时语音转文字交响系统
经过数十年在听写中的应用，以及近年来在环境记录中的使用，语音正逐渐成为医疗领域与技术及人工智能交互的主要方式。然而，医疗语音识别仍面临挑战：系统需准确捕捉专业术语、解决语境歧义，并精确呈现测量值、缩写及临床简写。现有解决方案通常针对通用转录或狭窄听写工作流进行优化，难以在安全关键场景中保证可靠性，也难以支持更广泛的临床工作流。我们提出Symphony语音转文字系统，专为医疗级实时流式及批量音频文件临床应用设计。Symphony将转录过程分解为识别、格式化和语境校正等专用组件，以优化医学术语召回率，实时生成结构化临床文本，并适应不同应用场景。在公开基准及医疗语音数据集上的评估表明，Symphony在临床场景中显著优于当前最先进系统，而在通用领域表现匹配或超越之，表明其具备稳健泛化能力而非过拟合。我们发布临床基准数据集，以支持可靠验证及医疗语音识别领域的进一步发展。Symphony通过生产级API提供实时听写、对话转录及批量音频文件处理服务。

Motivated by the need for accurate, real-time speech recognition in clinical settings where existing systems fail to handle medical terminology and context, Symphony for Speech-to-Text introduces a modular architecture that separates recognition, formatting, and contextual correction to enhance precision and adaptability.
