为提升医疗场景中语音识别的准确性与实时性，Symphony将语音识别过程拆分为识别、格式化和上下文校正三个模块，以优化医学术语召回率并生成结构化临床文本。在公开及医疗数据集上的评估显示，其在临床环境中显著优于现有系统，同时在通用领域表现持平或更优，表明具备良好的泛化能力。系统配套发布临床基准数据集，并通过生产级API支持实时口述、对话转录及批量音频处理。
Finite-Particle Convergence Rates for Conservative and Non-Conservative Drifting Models
Authors: Krishnakumar Balasubramanian
First: 2026-05-21T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:49:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose and analyze a conservative drifting method for one-step generative modeling. The method replaces the original displacement-based drifting velocity by a kernel density estimator (KDE)-gradient velocity, namely the difference of the kernel-smoothed data score and the kernel-smoothed model score. This velocity is a gradient field, addressing the non-conservatism issue identified for general displacement-based drifting fields. We prove continuous-time finite-particle convergence bounds for the conservative method on $\R^d$: a joint-entropy identity yields bounds for the empirical Stein drift, the smoothed Fisher discrepancy of the KDE, and the squared center velocity. The main finite-particle correction is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction term, and we give deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions under which this term is controlled. We keep the quadrature constants explicit and track their possible bandwidth dependence: the root residual-velocity rate $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ holds under an additional $h$-uniform quadrature regularity condition, while a more general growth condition yields the optimized root rate $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$, where $0\le β<2$. We also analyze the non-conservative drifting method with Laplace kernel, corresponding to the original displacement-based velocity proposed in~\cite{deng2026drifting}. For this method, a sharp companion kernel decomposes the velocity into a positive scalar preconditioning of a sharp-score mismatch plus a Laplace scale-mismatch residual, producing an analogous finite-particle rate with an unavoidable residual term. Finally, we explain how the continuous-time residual-velocity bounds translate into one-step generation guarantees through the explicit drift size $η$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保守与非保守漂移模型的有限粒子收敛速率
我们提出并分析了一种保守漂移方法，用于一步生成建模。该方法将原始基于位移的漂移速度替换为核密度估计（KDE）梯度速度，即核平滑数据得分与核平滑模型得分之差。该速度场为梯度场，解决了通用基于位移漂移场中识别出的非保守性问题。我们在 $\R^d$ 上证明了该保守方法的连续时间有限粒子收敛界：联合熵恒等式给出了经验Stein漂移、KDE的平滑Fisher差异及平方中心速度的界。主要的有限粒子修正项为逆KDE自相互作用项，我们给出了确定性及高概率局部占据条件下该项可控的条件。我们保留了求积常数的显式表达并追踪其可能的带宽依赖性：在额外的 $h$-均匀求积正则性条件下，根残余速度率 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ 成立；而更一般的增长条件则给出优化的根速率 $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$，其中 $0\le β<2$。我们还分析了使用Laplace核的非保守漂移方法，对应于文献~\cite{deng2026drifting}中提出的原始基于位移的速度。对于该方法，一个尖锐伴随核将速度分解为正标量预处理项与尖锐得分偏差项及Laplace尺度偏差残差项，从而产生类似有限粒子速率，但不可避免存在残差项。最后，我们解释了连续时间残余速度界如何通过显式漂移大小 $η$ 转化为一步生成保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the non-conservatism issue in displacement-based drifting methods, this paper introduces a conservative drifting approach for one-step generative modeling by replacing the original velocity with a KDE-gradient velocity derived from the difference of kernel-smoothed data and model scores. The method ensures gradient field properties and derives finite-particle convergence bounds in continuous time for $\R^d$, leveraging a joint-entropy identity to bound empirical Stein drift, smoothed Fisher discrepancy, and squared center velocity. The main correction term is a reciprocal-KDE self-interaction, controlled under deterministic and high-probability local-occupancy conditions. Explicit quadrature constants are tracked, yielding a root residual-velocity rate of $N^{-1/(d+4)}$ under uniform quadrature, or a more general $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$ for $0\le β<2$. In contrast, the non-conservative method with Laplace kernel decomposes velocity into preconditioning and scale-mismatch residuals, producing a similar rate with an unavoidable residual. Continuous-time bounds translate to one-step generation guarantees via explicit drift size $η$.
为解决生成模型中漂移方法的非保守性问题，本文提出一种保守漂移方法，将位移基速度替换为基于核密度估计（KDE）梯度的速度，即数据与模型核平滑得分之差。该方法保证梯度场性质，并在连续时间下推导出 $\R^d$ 中有限粒子收敛界，利用联合熵恒等式约束经验Stein漂移、平滑Fisher偏差与中心速度平方。主要修正项为核密度自交互项，在局部占据条件下可控；显式四阶常数揭示带宽依赖性，满足均匀四阶条件时得根率 $N^{-1/(d+4)}$，更一般增长条件则得优化根率 $N^{-(2-β)/(2(d+4-β))}$（$0\le β<2$）。相较之下，非保守的Laplace核方法将速度分解为预条件项与尺度残差项，产生类似速率但含不可避残差。连续时间残差速度界通过显式漂移量 $η$ 转化为一步生成保证。
MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems
Authors: Qianshu Cai, Yonggang Zhang, Xianzhang Jia, Wei Xue, Jun Song, Xinmei Tian, Yike Guo
First: 2026-05-21T17:48:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:48:33+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables. Preprint. Code: https://github.com/dav-joy-thon/MOSS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MOSS：通过源码级重写实现自主代理系统的自我演化
自主代理系统在部署后基本保持静态：它们无法从用户交互中学习，重复性故障将持续存在，直到下一次由人类驱动的更新推送修复。为应对这一问题，自我演化代理应运而生，但所有现有方案均将演化限制在可修改文本的产物——技能文件、提示配置、记忆模式、工作流图——而未触及代理框架本身。由于路由、钩子排序、状态不变式和分发机制均存在于代码而非任何文本产物中，因此存在一类结构性故障在文本层是无法触及的。我们认为，源码级适应是一种更通用的媒介：它具备图灵完备性，是所有可修改文本范围的严格超集，其效果是确定性的而非依赖基础模型的合规性，且不会因长上下文漂移而退化。我们提出MOSS系统，可在生产级代理子系统上执行源码级自我重写。每次演化均锚定于自动精选的生产故障证据批次，并通过确定性多阶段流水线推进；代码修改委托给可插拔的外部编码代理CLI，而MOSS保留阶段顺序与判定结果。候选方案通过在临时试用工作者中重放该批次进行验证，再经用户授权、原地容器替换及健康探针控制的回滚机制进行推广。在OpenClaw上，MOSS单次循环内无需人工干预，将四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current self-evolving agents that only modify text-based artifacts and ignore code-level structural failures, MOSS introduces source-level self-rewriting for autonomous agent systems. It uses a deterministic multi-stage pipeline anchored to production failure evidence, delegates code modification to an external coding agent, and verifies candidates via replay in ephemeral workers before promoting them through user-consent-gated, health-probe-gated in-place container swaps. On OpenClaw, MOSS improved the mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single automated cycle without human intervention.
为解决自主代理系统部署后无法自我修正代码层面结构性缺陷的问题，作者提出MOSS系统，支持生产环境中源码级别的自我重写。MOSS通过自动收集失败证据，执行确定性多阶段流程，并将代码修改委托给外部编码代理，同时保留阶段顺序与判定结果。候选代码变更通过临时工作节点回放验证，经用户确认后以健康探针保障的原地容器替换方式部署，支持回滚。在OpenClaw实验中，MOSS单次迭代即实现四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61，全程无需人工干预。
Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention
Authors: Ali Hatamizadeh, Yejin Choi, Jan Kautz
First: 2026-05-21T17:44:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:44:57+00:00
Comments: Gated DeltaNet-2 technical report; code at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.
中文标题/摘要
标题：门控DeltaNet-2：线性注意力中擦除与写入的解耦
线性注意力用固定大小的循环状态替代softmax注意力的无界缓存，将序列混合降至线性时间，解码降至常量内存。难点不仅在于忘记什么，更在于如何在不打乱现有关联的前提下编辑压缩的记忆。Delta规则模型在写入新值前减去当前读取值，Kimi Delta注意力（KDA）通过通道级衰减强化遗忘。但主动编辑仍使用单个标量门控同时控制两个不同操作：在键侧擦除多少旧内容和在值侧提交多少新内容。我们提出门控DeltaNet-2，通过继承自适应遗忘与通道级衰减，同时解决二者共有的缺陷——擦除与写入的标量绑定。门控Delta规则-2通过通道级擦除门b_t和通道级写入门w_t分离这两个角色，当两个门坍缩为同一标量时退化为KDA，当衰减也坍缩时退化为门控DeltaNet。我们推导出快速权重更新视角、将通道级衰减融入非对称擦除因子的分块WY算法，以及保留高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu token训练下，门控DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理和检索任务中，优于Mamba-2、门控DeltaNet、KDA及Mamba-3变体。其优势在长上下文RULER“针锋麦芒”基准中尤为显著，提升评估的多键检索设置表现，并在循环与混合设置中保持强劲。代码见https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently manage memory in linear attention models without scrambling existing associations during edits, Gated DeltaNet-2 introduces a novel architecture that decouples the erase and write operations using separate channel-wise gates. Unlike prior models that rely on a single scalar gate to control both forgetting and writing, Gated DeltaNet-2 employs distinct erase and write gates, enabling finer-grained control over memory updates. This design generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and Kimi Delta Attention while improving efficiency through a fast-weight update mechanism and gate-aware backward pass. Evaluated on 1.3B-parameter models trained on 100B tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 outperforms Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, reasoning, and retrieval tasks, with the most significant gains observed in long-context retrieval benchmarks like RULER.
为了解决线性注意力模型在编辑压缩记忆时易打乱现有关联的问题，Gated DeltaNet-2提出了一种新架构，通过独立的通道级擦除门和写入门解耦擦除与写入操作。与依赖单一标量门控制两者的老方法不同，该模型为每个通道配备独立的擦除和写入门，实现更精细的控制。模型还引入了快速权重更新机制和门控感知的反向传播，以保持高效训练。在1.3B参数、100B tokens训练数据下，其在语言建模、推理和检索任务中全面超越Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA及Mamba-3变体，尤其在长上下文检索任务（如RULER）中表现突出。
LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems
Authors: Sadia Asif, Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri, Momin Abbas, Prasanna Sattigeri, Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy
First: 2026-05-21T17:42:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:42:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LCGuard：多智能体系统中安全的键值共享潜通信框架
基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统日益依赖中间通信来协调复杂任务。虽然现有系统大多通过自然语言通信，但近期研究表明，通过Transformer键值（KV）缓存的潜通信可提升效率并保留更丰富的任务相关信息。然而，KV缓存也编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，形成一个隐秘通道，敏感内容可能在智能体间传播而无需显式文本披露。为此，我们提出\textbf{LCGuard}（潜通信守护者），一种面向多智能体LLM系统的安全KV潜通信框架。LCGuard将共享KV缓存视为潜工作记忆，在缓存内容跨智能体传输前学习表示层面的变换。我们通过重建形式化表示层面敏感信息泄露：若对抗性解码器能从共享缓存中恢复智能体特定敏感输入，则该缓存内容不安全。这导致一种对抗性训练框架：攻击者学习重建敏感输入，而LCGuard学习既能保留任务相关语义又能减少可重建信息的变换。在多个模型家族和多智能体基准上的实证评估表明，LCGuard持续降低基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率，同时保持与标准KV共享基线相当的任务性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of sensitive information leakage through latent communication in KV caches during multi-agent LLM coordination, LCGuard introduces a framework that transforms shared cache representations to minimize reconstructable sensitive inputs while preserving task-relevant semantics. It employs adversarial training: an adversary attempts to reconstruct agent-specific inputs from shared cache artifacts, while LCGuard learns to apply representation-level transformations that hinder reconstruction. Experimental results across multiple model families and benchmarks demonstrate that LCGuard significantly reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates without compromising task performance relative to standard KV-sharing baselines.
为解决多智能体LLM系统中通过KV缓存进行隐式通信时敏感信息泄露的风险，LCGuard提出一种在缓存传输前对表示层进行变换的框架，以防止攻击者从缓存中重建敏感输入。其将信息泄露形式化为攻击者能否从缓存中恢复敏感数据，并采用对抗训练：攻击者学习重建敏感输入，而LCGuard学习在保留任务语义的同时最小化可重建信息。实验证明，LCGuard在多个模型和基准上显著降低基于重建的泄露率和攻击成功率，且任务性能与标准KV共享基线相当。
Evaluating Commercial AI Chatbots as News Intermediaries
Authors: Mirac Suzgun, Emily Shen, Federico Bianchi, Alexander Spangher, Thomas Icard, Daniel E. Ho, Dan Jurafsky, James Zou
First: 2026-05-21T17:42:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:42:07+00:00
Comments: https://suzgunmirac.github.io/ai-news-preview/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
AI chatbots are rapidly shaping how people encounter the news, yet no prior study has systematically measured how accurately these systems, with their proprietary search integrations and retrieval-synthesis pipelines, handle emerging facts across languages and regions. We present a 14-day (February 9-22, 2026) evaluation of six AI chatbots (Gemini 3 Flash and Pro, Grok 4, Claude 4.5 Sonnet, GPT-5 and GPT-4o mini) on 2,100 factual questions derived from same-day BBC News reporting across six regional services (US & Canada, Arabic, Afrique, Hindi, Russian, Turkish). The best systems achieve over 90% multiple-choice accuracy on questions about events reported hours earlier. The same systems, however, lose 11-13% under free-response evaluation, and 16-17% across the cohort. We further characterize three failure patterns. First, every model achieves its lowest accuracy on Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) and citations indicate an Anglophone retrieval bias (e.g., models answering Hindi queries cite English Wikipedia more than any Hindi outlet). Second, retrieval, not reasoning, failures drive over 70% of all errors. When models retrieve a correct source, they often extract the correct answer; the problem is to land on the right source in the first place. Third, models achieving 88-96% accuracy on well-formed questions drop to 19-70% when questions contain subtle false premises, with the most vulnerable model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. We also identify a detection-accuracy paradox: the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy (abstention rate), while a weaker detector ranks first, showing that premise detection and answer recovery are partially independent capabilities. Overall, these suggest that high accuracy can mask systematic regional inequity, near-total dependence on retrieval infrastructure, and vulnerability to imperfect queries real users pose.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估商业AI聊天机器人作为新闻中介的准确性
AI聊天机器人正迅速塑造人们接触新闻的方式，但此前尚无研究系统衡量这些系统（其自有搜索集成与检索-合成管道）在多语言和多地区处理新兴事实的准确性。我们对六款AI聊天机器人（Gemini 3 Flash和Pro、Grok 4、Claude 4.5 Sonnet、GPT-5和GPT-4o mini）在2026年2月9日至22日的14天内，基于六种区域BBC新闻服务（美国与加拿大、阿拉伯语、非洲、印地语、俄语、土耳其语）同日报道的2100个事实性问题进行了评估。表现最佳的系统在关于数小时前报道事件的问题上，多项选择题准确率超过90%。然而，同一系统在自由回答评估中准确率下降11-13%，整体下降16-17%。我们进一步识别出三种失败模式：第一，所有模型在印地语问题上的准确率最低（79%，而其他地区为89-91%），引用显示存在英语主导的检索偏倚（如模型回答印地语问题时更常引用英文维基百科而非印地语媒体）；第二，超过70%的错误源于检索而非推理——当模型检索到正确来源时，往往能提取正确答案，问题在于能否首先找到正确来源；第三，当问题包含微妙的错误前提时，原本在结构良好问题上准确率88-96%的模型准确率骤降至19-70%，最脆弱的模型64%情况下接受虚构事实。我们还发现检测-准确率悖论：最佳错误前提检测器在对抗性准确率（回避率）中排名第二，而较弱检测器排名第一，表明前提检测与答案恢复能力部分独立。总体而言，高准确率可能掩盖系统性区域不平等、对检索基础设施的近乎完全依赖，以及对用户实际提出不完美问题的脆弱性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing role of AI chatbots as news intermediaries, this study systematically evaluates six commercial chatbots over 14 days using 2,100 factual questions drawn from BBC News across six global regions. The evaluation reveals that top-performing models achieve over 90% accuracy on multiple-choice questions about recent events, yet their performance drops significantly under free-response and especially when questions contain subtle false premises, with one model accepting fabricated facts 64% of the time. The research identifies three key failure patterns: a consistent accuracy deficit in Hindi (79% vs. 89-91% elsewhere) linked to Anglophone retrieval bias; retrieval errors accounting for over 70% of all failures; and a detection-accuracy paradox where the best false-premise detector ranks second in adversarial accuracy, indicating that premise detection and answer recovery are distinct capabilities. These findings highlight systemic regional inequity, heavy reliance on retrieval systems, and vulnerability to user query imperfections.
本研究对六款商业AI聊天机器人作为新闻中介的能力进行了为期14天的评估，测试了2100个来自BBC全球六大区域新闻的客观事实问题。结果显示，表现最好的模型在关于近期事件的多项选择题上准确率超过90%，但在开放式问答中准确率下降11–13%，面对细微错误前提时更降至19–70%。所有模型在印地语问题上的表现最差（79% vs. 其他地区89–91%），显示出对英语来源的检索偏倚。超过70%的错误源于检索失败而非推理错误，且最准确的模型对虚构前提极为敏感，其中一款64%情况下接受虚假信息。研究还发现“检测准确率悖论”：最佳前提检测器并非最擅长避免错误的模型，表明前提检测与答案恢复能力是独立的。
Corruption-Tolerant Asynchronous Q-Learning with Near-Optimal Rates
Authors: Sreejeet Maity, Aritra Mitra
Venue: ICML
First: 2025-09-10T18:56:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:37:36+00:00
Comments: To appear at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the problem of learning the optimal policy in a discounted, infinite-horizon reinforcement learning (RL) setting in the presence of adversarially corrupted rewards. To address this problem, we develop a novel robust variant of the \(Q\)-learning algorithm and analyze it under the challenging asynchronous sampling model with time-correlated data. Despite corruption, we prove that the finite-time guarantees of our approach match existing bounds, up to an additive term that scales with the fraction of corrupted samples. We also establish an information-theoretic lower bound, revealing that our guarantees are near-optimal. Notably, our algorithm is agnostic to the underlying reward distribution and provides the first finite-time robustness guarantees for asynchronous \(Q\)-learning. A key element of our analysis is a refined Azuma-Hoeffding inequality for almost-martingales, which may have broader applicability in the study of RL algorithms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：容忍腐败的异步Q学习及其近最优速率
我们在存在对抗性损坏奖励的折扣无限时域强化学习（RL）环境中研究学习最优策略的问题。为此，我们开发了一种新颖的鲁棒Q学习算法变体，并在具有时间相关数据的挑战性异步采样模型下对其进行分析。尽管存在损坏，我们证明了该方法的有限时间保证与现有界一致，仅增加一个与损坏样本比例成比例的附加项。我们还建立了信息论下界，揭示了我们的保证接近最优。值得注意的是，我们的算法对底层奖励分布无依赖性，并首次为异步Q学习提供了有限时间鲁棒性保证。我们分析的关键要素是针对几乎鞅的改进Azuma-Hoeffding不等式，该不等式可能在RL算法研究中具有更广泛的应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn optimal policies in reinforcement learning despite adversarial reward corruption, this paper introduces a novel robust Q-learning algorithm designed for asynchronous sampling with time-correlated data. The method leverages a refined Azuma-Hoeffding inequality for almost-martingales to analyze convergence, achieving finite-time guarantees that match existing bounds up to an additive term proportional to the fraction of corrupted samples. The algorithm is agnostic to reward distribution and provides the first finite-time robustness guarantees for asynchronous Q-learning, with theoretical results supported by an information-theoretic lower bound showing near-optimality.
为了解决在存在对抗性奖励污染且采样异步的强化学习环境中仍能学习最优策略的问题，本文提出了一种新的鲁棒Q学习算法，其在时间相关数据下仍能保持近最优的有限时间性能。该方法通过改进的Azuma-Hoeffding不等式处理几乎鞅过程，证明其性能界仅比现有界多一个与污染样本比例成正比的项。实验结果验证了算法的鲁棒性和理论紧致性，首次为异步Q学习提供了无需假设奖励分布的有限时间鲁棒性保证。
DeltaBox: Scaling Stateful AI Agents with Millisecond-Level Sandbox Checkpoint/Rollback
Authors: Yunpeng Dong, Jingkai He, Yuze Hou, Dong Du, Zhonghu Xu, Si Yu, Yubin Xia, Haibo Chen
First: 2026-05-21T17:36:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:36:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered AI agents require high-frequency state exploration (e.g., test-time tree search and reinforcement learning), relying on rapid checkpoint and rollback (C/R) of the complete sandbox state, including files and process state (e.g., memory, contexts, etc.). Existing mechanisms duplicate the entire state, causing hundreds of milliseconds to seconds of latency per C/R, which severely bottlenecks deep search and large-scale fan-outs. This paper observes that subsequent checkpoints in AI agents are highly similar. Therefore, instead of full duplication, a sandbox should only duplicate the changes between consecutive checkpoints (Key Insight). However, it is non-trivial to realize the idea, mainly due to the missing OS supports. This paper proposes a new OS-level abstraction, DeltaState, to enable the change-based transactional C/R for AI agents with two co-designed OS mechanisms. First, DeltaFS enables change-based filesystem C/R by organizing the file states into layers and dynamically freezing the writable layer and inserting a new one during checkpoint, reducing file updates to copy-on-write, and making rollback a simple layer switch. Second, DeltaCR enables change-based process state C/R using incremental dumps, and accelerates rollback by bypassing traditional pipelines to directly fork() from a frozen template process. We then present DeltaBox, a novel agent sandbox achieving millisecond level C/R through the two new mechanisms. Evaluations on SWE-bench and RL micro-benchmarks show DeltaBox completes checkpoint and rollback in millisecond-level latency (14ms and 5ms, respectively), empowering agents to explore substantially more nodes under fixed time budgets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeltaBox：通过毫秒级沙箱检查点/回滚扩展有状态AI代理
由大语言模型驱动的AI代理需要高频状态探索（如测试时树搜索和强化学习），依赖于对完整沙箱状态（包括文件和进程状态，如内存、上下文等）的快速检查点与回滚（C/R）。现有机制需完整复制状态，导致每次C/R延迟数百毫秒至数秒，严重制约深度搜索和大规模并行探索。 本文观察到AI代理的后续检查点高度相似。因此，沙箱只需复制连续检查点间的变更（关键洞察），而非完整复制。然而，实现该思想颇具挑战，主要受限于操作系统缺乏支持。本文提出新的操作系统级抽象DeltaState，结合两项协同设计的OS机制，实现基于变更的事务性C/R：首先，DeltaFS通过将文件状态组织为分层结构，在检查点时动态冻结可写层并插入新层，将文件更新降为写时复制，使回滚简化为层切换；其次，DeltaCR通过增量转储实现基于变更的进程状态C/R，并通过绕过传统管道直接从冻结模板进程fork()加速回滚。我们进一步提出DeltaBox，一种新型代理沙箱，通过上述两项机制实现毫秒级C/R。在SWE-bench和RL微基准测试中，DeltaBox的检查点与回滚延迟分别仅为14ms和5ms，使代理在固定时间预算下可探索更多节点。
Summary / 总结
To address the high latency of full-state checkpointing in LLM-powered AI agents, which hinders efficient state exploration, this paper introduces DeltaBox, an OS-level sandbox system that enables millisecond-level checkpoint and rollback by tracking only state changes between consecutive checkpoints. It achieves this through two co-designed mechanisms: DeltaFS, which manages filesystem state via layered, copy-on-write snapshots to minimize duplication, and DeltaCR, which uses incremental process state dumps and direct fork() from frozen templates to accelerate rollback. Experimental results on SWE-bench and RL benchmarks demonstrate that DeltaBox achieves 14ms for checkpoint and 5ms for rollback, enabling agents to explore significantly more nodes within fixed time budgets.
为解决AI代理状态探索中全量沙箱快照与回滚导致的高延迟问题，本文提出DeltaBox系统，通过变更追踪实现高效状态管理。其核心洞察是连续快照间状态高度相似，因此只需记录并隔离变更部分而非完整复制。系统包含两个协同设计的OS机制：DeltaFS通过分层快照与写时复制，实现文件系统变更的快速回滚；DeltaCR利用增量转储与直接从冻结模板进程fork，加速进程状态回滚。在SWE-bench与RL基准测试中，DeltaBox实现14毫秒快照与5毫秒回滚延迟，显著提升代理在固定时间内可探索的节点数量。
Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection
Authors: Alexander Goldberg, Giulia Fanti, Nihar B. Shah
First: 2026-05-19T16:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:36:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于稳定随机选择的平滑部分抽签
从科研资助到招生和招聘，竞争性选拔过程通过评估对候选人打分，最终根据这些分数选择部分候选人。最近，许多机构采用了基于评估分数的抽签机制。然而，现有抽签设计本质上不稳定，因为单个候选人分数的微小变化可能导致其入选概率发生大幅波动。这种不稳定性削弱了抽签的核心目标：减少决策边界附近细微分数差异的影响。我们提出“平滑性”作为部分抽签的设计原则，并将其形式化为从候选人评审分数到入选概率的Lipschitz条件。我们引入了“截断线性抽签”，这是一种简单的机制，其中入选概率在上阈值（高于该阈值则始终接受）与下阈值（低于该阈值则始终拒绝）之间线性增长。我们证明，截断线性抽签的最坏情况遗憾值与任何平滑选择规则的下界相差因子为(1 - k/n)，其中k/n为接受率。我们将平滑选择与其他稳定性概念（如个体公平性和差分隐私）进行比较，表明截断线性抽签在平滑性-遗憾值权衡上优于其他方案。在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金会的真实同行评审数据上的实验表明，即使对单个分数进行扰动，现有抽签设计在实践中仍高度不稳定。我们的实验还验证了理论分析的紧致性，并表明所提出的截断线性抽签在实践中实现了优于其他方案的平滑性-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of existing partial lottery systems in competitive selection processes—where minor score changes can drastically alter selection probabilities—the paper introduces smoothness as a formal design principle, defined via a Lipschitz condition on score-to-selection probability mappings. It proposes the Clipped Linear Lottery, a mechanism that assigns linearly increasing selection probabilities between upper and lower thresholds, ensuring stability near decision boundaries. Theoretical analysis shows this mechanism achieves near-optimal regret, bounded by a factor of (1 - k/n) relative to any smooth rule, and experimental results on real peer review data confirm its superior smoothness-utility tradeoff compared to alternatives like Individual Fairness or Differential Privacy.
为解决现有部分抽签机制在竞争性选拔中因微小分数变动导致选择概率剧烈波动的问题，本文提出以“平滑性”为设计原则，形式化为映射的Lipschitz条件。提出“截断线性抽签”机制，通过在上下阈值间线性分配选择概率，确保决策边界附近稳定性。理论分析证明其最坏情况遗憾接近最优平滑规则的下界，仅差一个与接受率相关的因子。在真实同行评审数据实验中，验证了现有机制在单分数扰动下高度不稳定，而该机制在平滑性与效用之间实现了优于其他方法的权衡。
FAME: Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Message-Level Log Anomaly Detection
Authors: Huanchi Wang, Zihang Huang, Yifang Tian, Kristina Dzeparoska, Hans-Arno Jacobsen, Alberto Leon-Garcia
First: 2026-05-21T17:34:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:34:53+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Production systems generate millions of log lines daily, yet most anomaly detectors operate at the session or window-level, flagging groups of lines rather than identifying the specific message responsible. This coarse granularity forces operators to inspect many routine lines per alert. Message-level detection offers finer granularity, but remains challenging. A single event template may correspond to both normal and anomalous messages, failures arise from heterogeneous subsystems, and line-level labeling at scale is impractical. Although large language models (LLMs) can reason over log semantics, applying them to every line is too costly for continuous monitoring. We present FAME (Failure-Aware Mixture-of-Experts), a label-efficient message-level mixture-of-experts framework that uses an LLM only once offline. We annotate at most K labeled lines per template to derive binary normal/anomaly indicators and representative examples. The LLM proposes a partition of templates into failure domains, and a certification step validates the proposal before training. FAME trains a lightweight router and domain experts that run on-premise and output anomaly predictions and failure-domain labels. On BGL, FAME achieves F1 = 98.16 at K = 100 reducing annotation effort by 76x and detects 86.3% of anomalies from unseen EventIDs. On Thunderbird, FAME reaches F1 = 99.95 with perfect recall.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FAME：面向消息级日志异常检测的故障感知专家混合模型
生产系统每日生成数百万条日志记录，但大多数异常检测器在会话或窗口级别运行，标记的是日志组而非具体异常消息。这种粗粒度迫使运维人员在每次告警中检查大量常规日志。消息级检测虽更精细，但挑战重重：单个事件模板可能对应正常或异常消息，故障源于异构子系统，大规模行级标注不切实际。尽管大语言模型（LLM）可理解日志语义，但对每行日志应用成本过高，难以满足持续监控需求。本文提出FAME（故障感知专家混合模型），一种标注效率高的消息级专家混合框架，仅在离线时使用一次LLM。我们为每个模板最多标注K条带标签日志行，以生成二元正常/异常指示及代表性示例。LLM提出模板的故障域划分方案，认证步骤在训练前验证该方案。FAME训练轻量级路由器和本地部署的领域专家，输出异常预测与故障域标签。在BGL数据集上，FAME在K=100时F1=98.16，标注工作量减少76倍，且能检测86.3%的未见EventID异常。在Thunderbird数据集上，FAME达到F1=99.95，召回率完美。
Summary / 总结
FAME addresses the challenge of message-level log anomaly detection by reducing reliance on extensive line-level labeling through a label-efficient mixture-of-experts framework. Motivated by the inefficiency of coarse-grained detection and the impracticality of annotating millions of log lines, FAME uses a large language model (LLM) offline to partition event templates into failure domains and generate representative examples, requiring only K labeled lines per template. The framework then trains lightweight on-premise components—a router and domain experts—to predict anomalies and failure domains. Experiments on BGL and Thunderbird show FAME achieves F1 scores of 98.16 and 99.95 respectively, with 76x reduction in annotation effort and high recall for unseen anomalies.
FAME旨在解决日志消息级异常检测中依赖昂贵逐行LLM推理的问题。其动机源于粗粒度检测效率低下和大规模标注不可行，因此FAME仅在离线阶段使用LLM，对每个事件模板最多标注K条样本，生成二分类异常指示和代表性示例。LLM提出模板划分至故障域的专家分配方案，经验证后训练轻量级本地路由器与领域专家，输出异常预测与故障域标签。在BGL和Thunderbird数据集上，FAME分别达到F1=98.16和99.95，标注工作量减少76倍，且对未见EventID仍保持高召回率。
DecQ: Detail-Condensing Queries for Enhanced Reconstruction and Generation in Representation Autoencoders
Authors: Tianhang Wang, Yitong Chen, Wei Song, Zuxuan Wu, Min Li, Jiaqi Wang
First: 2026-05-21T17:34:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:34:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation Autoencoders (RAEs) leverage frozen vision foundation models (VFMs) as tokenizer encoders, providing robust high-level representations that facilitate fast convergence and high-quality generation in latent diffusion models. However, freezing the VFM inherently constrains its spatial reconstruction capacity, limiting fine-grained generation and image editing; in contrast, incorporating reconstruction-oriented signals via fine-tuning disrupts the pretrained semantic space and degrades generative fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose DecQ, a simple yet effective framework for RAEs. Specifically, DecQ introduces lightweight detail-condensing queries that extract fine-grained information from intermediate VFM features through condenser modules. These queries are incorporated into the decoder to support reconstruction and are jointly generated with patch tokens during generative modeling. By aggregating information from both shallow and deep layers, DecQ effectively mitigates the reconstruction--generation trade-off, improving both reconstruction quality and generative performance. Our experiments demonstrate that: (1) with only 8 additional queries and 3.9% extra computation, DecQ improves reconstruction over the frozen DINOv2-based RAE, increasing PSNR from 19.13 dB to 22.76 dB; and (2) for generative modeling, DecQ achieves 3.3$\times$ faster convergence than RAE, attaining an FID of 1.41 without guidance and 1.05 with guidance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DecQ：用于表示自编码器中增强重建与生成的细节压缩查询
表示自编码器（RAEs）利用冻结的视觉基础模型（VFMs）作为分词器编码器，提供稳健的高层表示，有助于潜在扩散模型快速收敛和高质量生成。然而，冻结VFM会固有地限制其空间重建能力，限制精细生成和图像编辑；相反，通过微调引入重建导向信号会破坏预训练语义空间并降低生成保真度。为解决这一权衡，我们提出DecQ，一种简单而有效的RAEs框架。具体而言，DecQ引入轻量级细节压缩查询，通过压缩模块从VFM中间特征中提取细粒度信息。这些查询被整合到解码器中以支持重建，并在生成建模过程中与补丁标记联合生成。通过聚合浅层和深层的信息，DecQ有效缓解了重建与生成之间的权衡，提升了重建质量和生成性能。实验表明：（1）仅增加8个查询和3.9%的计算开销，DecQ在基于冻结DINOv2的RAE基础上提升了重建质量，PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB；（2）在生成建模中，DecQ比RAE快3.3倍，无引导时FID为1.41，有引导时为1.05。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between reconstruction fidelity and generative quality in representation autoencoders using frozen vision foundation models, the authors propose DecQ, a framework that introduces lightweight detail-condensing queries to extract fine-grained features from intermediate VFM layers via condenser modules. These queries are integrated into the decoder for reconstruction and jointly generated with patch tokens during generation, enabling the model to leverage both shallow and deep feature information. Experimental results show that DecQ improves reconstruction PSNR from 19.13 dB to 22.76 dB with only 8 additional queries and 3.9% extra computation, while also accelerating generative convergence by 3.3x and achieving FID scores of 1.41 and 1.05 without and with guidance, respectively.
为解决冻结视觉基础模型的表示自编码器在重建与生成之间存在的权衡问题，DecQ提出轻量级细节压缩查询，通过压缩模块从中间层特征中提取细粒度信息，并将其注入解码器用于重建，同时在生成过程中与补丁标记联合生成。实验表明，仅增加8个查询和3.9%计算开销，重建PSNR从19.13 dB提升至22.76 dB；生成方面收敛速度提升3.3倍，无引导FID达1.41，有引导FID达1.05。
SDPM: Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model for Continuous-Time Survival Analysis
Authors: Stanislav R. Kirpichenko, Andrei V. Konstantinov, Lev V. Utkin
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Survival analysis aims to estimate a time-to-event distribution from data with censored observations. Many existing methods either impose structural assumptions on the hazard function or discretize the time axis, which may limit flexibility and introduce approximation errors. We propose the Survival Diffusion Probabilistic Model (SDPM), a generative approach to continuous-time survival analysis. SDPM models the conditional distribution of the survival outcome, represented by the pair of observed time and censoring indicator, $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$, using a denoising diffusion model. Under the assumption of conditionally independent censoring, conditional samples generated by the model can be transformed into survival function estimates using the Kaplan-Meier estimator. This formulation avoids parametric assumptions on the event-time distribution and does not require a discretization of the output time space. The model operates in a transformed target space, using standardized log-times and a continuous Gaussian-mixture representation of the censoring indicator. We evaluate SDPM on ten real survival datasets and compare it with five strong baselines, including tree-based, boosting-based, and neural survival models. Results show that SDPM achieves competitive predictive performance across C-index, integrated time-dependent AUC, and integrated Brier score. A study on synthetic Cox-Weibull data demonstrates that SDPM can recover the shape of an underlying continuous survival distribution more accurately than a strong nonparametric baseline when sufficiently many samples are generated. An ablation study confirms the importance of the proposed target-space transformations, which improve event-rate calibration, reduce invalid generated times, and provide consistent gains in predictive discrimination. Codes implementing the proposed model are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SDPM：用于连续时间生存分析的生存扩散概率模型
生存分析旨在从具有删失观测的数据中估计事件发生时间的分布。许多现有方法要么对风险函数施加结构化假设，要么对时间轴进行离散化，这可能限制灵活性并引入近似误差。我们提出生存扩散概率模型（SDPM），一种用于连续时间生存分析的生成方法。SDPM 使用去噪扩散模型建模生存结果的条件分布（由观测时间与删失指示符组成的对 $\mathbb{P}(T,δ\mid \mathbf{x})$）。在条件独立删失的假设下，模型生成的条件样本可通过 Kaplan-Meier 估计器转换为生存函数估计。该方法避免了对事件时间分布的参数化假设，且无需对输出时间空间进行离散化。模型在变换后的目标空间中运行，使用标准化对数时间及连续高斯混合表示删失指示符。我们在十个真实生存数据集上评估 SDPM，并与五种强基线方法（包括基于树、提升和神经网络的生存模型）进行比较。结果表明，SDPM 在 C-index、时间依赖性 AUC 和 Brier 分数的综合指标上均表现优异。在合成 Cox-Weibull 数据上的研究表明，当生成足够多样本时，SDPM 比强非参数基线更准确地恢复底层连续生存分布的形状。消融研究证实了所提目标空间变换的重要性，这些变换可提升事件率校准、减少无效生成时间，并在预测区分度上持续带来改进。模型代码公开可用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations of existing survival analysis methods that rely on structural hazard assumptions or time discretization, the paper introduces SDPM, a denoising diffusion model that directly models the conditional distribution of survival outcomes (time and censoring indicator) without parametric constraints. By operating in a transformed target space using standardized log-times and a Gaussian-mixture censoring representation, SDPM avoids discretization and enables continuous-time inference. Evaluated on ten real datasets and compared against five strong baselines, SDPM achieves competitive performance across C-index, time-dependent AUC, and Brier score. Experiments on synthetic Cox-Weibull data show SDPM better recovers continuous survival distributions than nonparametric baselines with sufficient samples, and ablation studies confirm that target-space transformations improve calibration, reduce invalid times, and enhance predictive discrimination.
为克服现有生存分析方法对风险函数结构假设或时间离散化带来的限制，本文提出SDPM，一种基于去噪扩散的生成模型，用于估计生存结果的条件分布，无需参数假设或时间离散化。SDPM在标准化对数时间与高斯混合截断指示器的变换目标空间中操作，通过Kaplan-Meier估计器直接推断生存函数。在十组真实数据上与五种基线方法对比，SDPM在C指数、时变AUC和Brier分数上表现竞争力。在合成Cox-Weibull数据上，其在样本充足时比强非参数基线更准确恢复连续生存分布。消融实验验证了目标空间变换对校准、无效时间减少和预测区分度提升的关键作用。
MambaGaze: Bidirectional Mamba with Explicit Missing Data Modeling for Cognitive Load Assessment from Eye-Gaze Tracking Data
Authors: Amir Mousavi, Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Mimi Xie, Rocky Slavin, Leslie Neely, John Davis, John Quarles
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:41+00:00
Comments: Submitted to IEEE-EMBS International Conference on Biomedical and Health Informatics (BHI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time cognitive load assessment from eye-tracking signals could potentially enable adaptive human-centered-AI such as safety-critical applications such as driver vigilance monitoring or automated flight deck assistance, yet two challenges persist: handling frequent data missingness from blinks and tracking failures, and efficiently modeling long-range temporal dependencies. We propose MambaGaze, a framework that addresses these challenges through 1) XMD encoding, which augments raw features with observation masks and time-deltas to explicitly model data uncertainty, and 2) bidirectional Mamba-2, which captures temporal dependencies with linear computational complexity. Experiments on CLARE and CL-Drive datasets under leave-one-subject-out evaluation show that MambaGaze achieves 76.8% and 73.1% accuracy, respectively, outperforming CNN, Transformer, ResNet, and VGG baselines by 4-12 percentage points. Edge deployment benchmarks on NVIDIA Jetson platforms demonstrate real-time inference at 43-68 FPS with power consumption below 7.5W, confirming feasibility for wearable cognitive load monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MambaGaze：面向眼动追踪数据认知负荷评估的双向Mamba与显式缺失数据建模
从眼动信号实时评估认知负荷，有望实现如驾驶员警觉监控或自动飞行舱辅助等安全关键的人机协同AI系统，但面临两大挑战：因眨眼和跟踪失败导致的频繁数据缺失，以及高效建模长时序依赖关系。本文提出MambaGaze框架，通过1）XMD编码，将观测掩码与时间差附加至原始特征，显式建模数据不确定性；2）双向Mamba-2，以线性计算复杂度捕捉时序依赖。在CLARE与CL-Drive数据集上，采用留一受试者验证，MambaGaze分别达到76.8%和73.1%准确率，较CNN、Transformer、ResNet和VGG基线高出4-12个百分点。在NVIDIA Jetson平台边缘部署测试中，推理速度达43-68 FPS，功耗低于7.5W，证实其适用于可穿戴认知负荷监测。

MambaGaze introduces XMD encoding to explicitly model data uncertainty by augmenting raw features with observation masks and time-deltas, and employs bidirectional Mamba-2 to capture temporal patterns with linear computational complexity. CogAdapt: Transferring Clinical ECG Foundation Models to Wearable Cognitive Load Assessment via Lead Adaptation
Authors: Amir Mousavi, Mohammad Sadegh Sirjani, Erfan Nourbakhsh, Mimi Xie, Rocky Slavin, Leslie Neely, John Davis, John Quarles
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:35+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 7 figures. Submitted to IEEE-EMBS International Conference on Biomedical and Health Informatics (BHI 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time cognitive load assessment is essential for adaptive human-computer interaction but remains challenging due to limited labeled data and poor cross-subject generalization. Recent ECG foundation models pre-trained on millions of clinical recordings offer rich representations, but cannot be directly applied to wearable devices due to sensor configuration mismatch and task differences. In this paper, we propose CogAdapt, a framework that adapts clinical ECG foundation models to wearable cognitive load assessment. CogAdapt introduces LeadBridge, a learnable adapter that transforms 3-lead wearable signals into anatomically consistent 12-lead representations, and ProFine, a progressive fine-tuning strategy that gradually unfreezes encoder layers while preventing catastrophic forgetting. Evaluations on two public datasets (CLARE and CL-Drive) under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation show that CogAdapt substantially outperforms baselines trained from scratch, achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.626 and 0.768. These results demonstrate the promise of foundation model adaptation for subject-independent cognitive load assessment from wearable sensors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CogAdapt：通过导联适配将临床ECG基础模型迁移至可穿戴认知负荷评估

为解决可穿戴设备中实时认知负荷评估的挑战，本文提出CogAdapt框架，将临床ECG基础模型适配至可穿戴认知负荷任务。该框架包含LeadBridge模块，通过可学习适配器将3导联可穿戴ECG信号映射为12导联解剖学一致表示，并采用ProFine渐进式微调策略逐步解冻编码器层以避免灾难性遗忘。在CLARE和CL-Drive两个公开数据集上进行留一受试者交叉验证，CogAdapt显著优于从头训练基线模型，分别达到0.626和0.768的宏F1分数，验证了基础模型适配在可穿戴设备上实现跨受试者认知负荷评估的可行性。
Deep Reinforcement Learning for Flexible Job Shop Scheduling with Random Job Arrivals
Authors: Yu Tang, Muhammad Zakwan, Efe Balta, John Lygeros, Alisa Rupenyan
First: 2026-05-21T17:33:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:33:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Flexible Job Shop Scheduling Problem (FJSP) is the optimal allocation of a set of jobs to machines. Two primary challenges persist in FJSP: the unpredictable arrival of future jobs and the combinatorial complexity of the problem, rendering it intractable for conventional mixed-integer linear programming solvers. This paper proposes an event-based \gls{DRL} approach to solve FJSP with random job arrivals. Specifically, we employ the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm and use lightweight Multi-Layer Perceptrons to train the \gls{DRL} agent for minimizing the total completion time of all jobs. We design the state representation to be directly accessible from the environment, and limit the learning agent to selecting from among a set of well-established dispatching rules. Simulations show that our \gls{DRL} approach outperforms any of the individual dispatching rules on datasets with varying heterogeneity and job arrival rates. We benchmark our \gls{DRL} against an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solution and show that our method achieves good performance especially when the datasets are heterogeneous.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向随机作业到达的柔性作业车间调度的深度强化学习方法
柔性作业车间调度问题（FJSP）是将一组作业最优分配至机器的问题。FJSP存在两个主要挑战：未来作业的不可预测到达与问题的组合爆炸复杂性，使得传统混合整数线性规划求解器难以处理。本文提出一种基于事件的深度强化学习（DRL）方法，用于解决随机作业到达的FJSP。具体而言，我们采用近端策略优化算法，并使用轻量级多层感知机训练DRL智能体，以最小化所有作业的总完成时间。我们设计状态表示可直接从环境中获取，并限制学习智能体仅从一组成熟的调度规则中选择。仿真结果表明，我们的DRL方法在不同异质性和作业到达率的数据集上均优于任何单一调度规则。我们还将DRL方法与基于到达触发的混合整数线性规划解法进行对比，结果表明，当数据集异质性较高时，我们的方法表现尤为出色。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of unpredictable job arrivals and combinatorial complexity in Flexible Job Shop Scheduling, this paper proposes a deep reinforcement learning approach using Proximal Policy Optimization with lightweight MLPs to minimize total job completion time. The agent learns to select from a set of established dispatching rules using an environment-accessible state representation. Experimental results demonstrate that the DRL method consistently outperforms individual dispatching rules across varying job heterogeneity and arrival rates, and matches or exceeds the performance of an arrival-triggered mixed-integer linear programming solver, especially in heterogeneous scenarios.
为应对柔性作业车间调度中作业随机到达与组合爆炸的挑战，本文提出基于深度强化学习的解决方案，采用近端策略优化算法与轻量级多层感知机，以最小化总完工时间。智能体从环境直接获取状态表示，并在预设调度规则中选择动作。实验表明，该方法在不同作业到达率和数据异质性下均优于单个调度规则，且在异质数据集上表现尤为突出，可与基于事件触发的混合整数线性规划方法相媲美。
Reducing Political Manipulation with Consistency Training
Authors: Long Phan, Devin Kim, Alexander Pan, Alice Blair, Adam Khoja, Dan Hendrycks
First: 2026-05-21T17:32:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:32:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic political bias across a variety of sensitive contexts. We find that LLMs handle counterpart topics from opposing political sides asymmetrically. We refer to this phenomenon as covert political bias and identify 7 categories of techniques through which it operates. We propose two metrics for covert bias: Sentiment Consistency measures symmetry in rhetoric and framing across paired political prompts; Helpfulness Consistency measures symmetric depth and engagement. To reduce both types of covert bias, we introduce Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL training method with two complementary paradigms: Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training. We show that PCT preserves overall helpfulness, substantially reduces covert political bias, and generalizes to held-out benchmarks. We release our work at https://political-manipulation.ai
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过一致性训练减少政治操控
大型语言模型（LLMs）在多种敏感情境中表现出系统性的政治偏见。我们发现LLMs对来自对立政治立场的对应主题处理存在不对称性，我们将此现象称为隐性政治偏见，并识别出其运作的7类技术。我们提出两种衡量隐性偏见的指标：情感一致性衡量配对政治提示在修辞和框架上的对称性；有用性一致性衡量对称的深度和参与度。为减少这两种隐性偏见，我们引入了政治一致性训练（PCT），这是一种结合两种互补范式的强化学习训练方法：情感一致性训练和有用性一致性训练。我们证明PCT在保持整体有用性的同时，显著降低了隐性政治偏见，并能泛化到未见基准测试。我们的工作已发布于https://political-manipulation.ai
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the discovery that large language models exhibit asymmetric handling of politically opposing topics—a phenomenon termed covert political bias—the authors propose Political Consistency Training (PCT), an RL-based method combining Sentiment Consistency Training and Helpfulness Consistency Training to enforce symmetric rhetorical framing and response depth across political prompts. Experimental results demonstrate that PCT significantly reduces covert bias while preserving overall helpfulness and generalizing to unseen benchmarks.
鉴于大语言模型在处理对立政治话题时表现出系统性不对称——即隐蔽政治偏见——作者提出两个量化指标：情感一致性与帮助性一致性。他们设计了政治一致性训练（PCT），一种结合两种互补强化学习范式的训练方法，旨在强制政治对立提示在修辞与互动深度上保持对称。实验表明，PCT显著降低隐蔽政治偏见，同时保持整体帮助性，并能泛化至未见基准测试。
Understanding Data Temporality Impact on Large Language Models Pre-training
Authors: Pilchen Hippolyte, Fabre Romain, Signe Talla Franck, Perez Patrick, Grave Edouard
First: 2026-05-21T17:31:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:31:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are typically trained on shuffled corpora, yielding models whose knowledge is frozen at train time and whose temporal grounding remains poorly understood. In this work, we study the impact of pre-training dynamics on the acquisition of time-sensitive factual knowledge, focusing specifically on data ordering. Our main contributions are twofold. First, we introduce a comprehensive benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and an evaluation protocol that enables analysis of whether models correctly associate facts with their corresponding time periods. Second, we pretrain 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots and compare them against standard shuffled pre-training. Our results show that sequentially trained models match shuffled baselines on general language understanding and common knowledge while consistently exhibiting more up-to-date and temporally precise knowledge. Temporally ordered pre-training yields improved factual freshness, while shuffled pre-training peaks on older data, possibly due to increased factual repetition. These findings, along with the release of our code at https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos , checkpoints, and datasets at https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos provide a foundation for future research on continual learning for LLMs.

标题：理解数据时间性对大语言模型预训练的影响
大语言模型（LLMs）通常在打乱的数据语料上进行训练，导致模型知识在训练时即被冻结，其时间锚定能力也缺乏深入理解。本研究探讨预训练动态对时间敏感事实知识获取的影响，重点聚焦数据排序。主要贡献有两点：首先，我们构建了一个包含7000多个时间锚定问题的综合基准，并设计评估协议，用于分析模型是否能正确将事实与其对应的时间段关联；其次，我们在按时间顺序排列的Common Crawl快照上预训练6B参数模型，并与标准打乱预训练进行对比。结果表明，顺序训练模型在通用语言理解和常识方面与打乱基线相当，但始终展现出更新、更精确的时间知识。按时间顺序预训练可提升事实新鲜度，而打乱预训练则在较旧数据上表现更优，可能源于事实重复率更高。本研究结果及代码（https://github.com/kyutai-labs/kairos）、检查点和数据集（https://huggingface.co/collections/kyutai/kairos）为未来LLM持续学习研究奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how the temporal ordering of training data affects the factual knowledge acquisition of large language models during pre-training. Motivated by the observation that standard shuffled corpora lead to models with poorly understood temporal grounding, the authors introduce a benchmark of over 7,000 temporally grounded questions and train 6B-parameter models on temporally ordered Common Crawl snapshots, comparing them to shuffled baselines. Results show that sequentially trained models perform comparably on general language tasks but consistently demonstrate more up-to-date and temporally precise factual knowledge, whereas shuffled models peak on older data due to increased repetition.
该研究探讨了训练数据的时间顺序如何影响大语言模型在预训练阶段获取事实性知识的能力。鉴于现有模型通常在打乱的数据上训练，导致其时间锚定能力模糊，作者构建了包含7000多道时间相关问题的评测基准，并在时间有序的Common Crawl数据上预训练6B参数模型，与标准打乱训练模型进行对比。结果表明，顺序训练模型在通用语言理解上表现相当，但在事实更新性和时间准确性上显著优于打乱训练模型，而打乱训练模型则在旧事实表现更优，可能源于事实重复强化。这表明时间有序训练可提升事实新鲜度而不牺牲通用能力。
Synthetic Data Alone is Enough? Rethinking Data Scarcity in Pediatric Rare Disease Recognition
Authors: Ganlin Feng, Yuxi Long, Erin Lou, Lianghong Chen, Zihao Jing, Pingzhao Hu, Wei Xu
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-21T17:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:28:11+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 CV4CHL workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Children with rare genetic diseases often exhibit distinctive facial phenotypes, yet developing computer vision systems for early diagnosis remains challenging due to extreme data scarcity, privacy constraints, and limited data sharing in pediatric settings. These challenges not only hinder automated diagnosis but also restrict the availability of visual resources for clinical genetic counseling. While prior work has shown that synthetic data can augment real datasets and preserve phenotype-level semantics, it remains unclear whether synthetic data alone is sufficient for learning in ultra-low-resource pediatric settings. In this work, we study the synthetic-only regime for pediatric rare disease recognition. Under a controlled experimental setup, models are trained exclusively on phenotype-aware synthetic facial images at increasing scales. We find that synthetic-only training achieves performance comparable to real-data-only baselines at sufficient scale across multiple backbones, suggesting that high-fidelity synthetic data can approximate clinically meaningful distributions. These findings together further enable the use of synthetic pediatric facial images as privacy-preserving resources for genetic education and counseling, supporting clinician training and patient communication. Our results highlight the potential of computer vision to improve data efficiency and expand accessible visual tools in children's healthcare.

标题：仅靠合成数据就足够了吗？重新思考儿科罕见病识别中的数据稀缺问题
患有罕见遗传病的儿童常表现出独特的面部表型，但由于数据极度稀缺、隐私限制及儿科领域数据共享有限，开发用于早期诊断的计算机视觉系统仍具挑战性。这些挑战不仅阻碍自动化诊断，也限制了临床遗传咨询中视觉资源的可用性。尽管已有研究表明合成数据可增强真实数据集并保留表型层面语义，但尚不清楚合成数据是否足以在极端低资源的儿科环境中实现学习。本研究探讨了仅使用合成数据的儿科罕见病识别方案。在受控实验设置下，模型仅在不同规模的表型感知合成面部图像上训练。我们发现，在足够规模下，仅合成数据训练的性能可与仅真实数据训练的基线相当，表明高保真合成数据可近似临床有意义的分布。这些发现进一步支持将合成儿科面部图像作为隐私保护的遗传教育与咨询资源，助力临床医生培训和患者沟通。我们的结果凸显了计算机视觉在提升数据效率、扩展儿童医疗中可及视觉工具方面的潜力。

Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation
Authors: Samson Gourevitch, Yazid Janati, Dario Shariatian, Umut Simsekli, Eric Moulines, Eric P. Xing, Alain Durmus
First: 2026-05-21T17:27:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:27:19+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor. We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：均匀扩散模型再探：留一法去噪器与吸收态重述
离散扩散模型常通过干净数据预测进行训练，但预测可用于定义反向动力学的不同方式。在掩码扩散模型（MDM）中，这些选择基本一致，而在均匀扩散模型（UDM）中则不然。我们证明，UDM的标准插值桥参数化并非由去噪后验优化，而是由留一法后验优化，该后验预测每个干净标记时不使用其自身的噪声观测。这揭示了插值ELBO与常用交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配。我们刻画了留一法目标，并推导出去噪器、留一法后验与分数之间的精确转换。这些转换使我们能够分离参数化与训练目标。我们的结果还通过有指导的预测-校正采样器和基于留一法预测的改进温度采样，在无需额外训练的情况下提升推理性能。我们进一步引入了均匀扩散的吸收态重述，该重述保留了UDM的联合分布，同时将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，具有更简单的去噪后验、携带式去掩码和自然的重掩码机制。在语言建模中，留一法参数化持续提升UDM生成效果，而吸收态构造可匹配或超越掩码扩散。这些结果表明，掩码与均匀扩散之间的经验差距，更多由参数化与采样设计驱动，而非边际分布本身的选择。代码与模型见 https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm。
Summary / 总结
This paper revisits uniform diffusion models by identifying a mismatch between their standard plug-in ELBO objective and the true denoising posterior, which is better captured by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each token without using its own noisy observation. The authors derive exact mathematical conversions between denoisers, leave-one-out posteriors, and score functions, enabling decoupling of parameterization from training objectives. They introduce an absorbing-state reformulation that preserves the UDM joint distribution while simplifying sampling via masked-diffusion-like operations, leading to improved inference without extra training. Experiments on language modeling show that leave-one-out parameterizations consistently enhance UDM generation, and the absorbing-state approach matches or outperforms masked diffusion, suggesting that performance gaps stem more from sampling and parameterization than from the underlying diffusion structure.
为解决标准UDM参数化与去噪目标不匹配的问题，本文重新审视了均匀扩散模型，提出一种“留一法”去噪器，即在预测每个干净token时不使用其自身的噪声观测，发现插值ELBO并非最优。作者推导出去噪器、留一后验与分数之间的精确转换，实现参数化与训练目标的解耦。实验表明，留一法参数化显著提升UDM生成效果，而引入的吸收态重写结构——将UDM分解为类似掩码扩散的采样步骤，具有更简单的后验、自然的解掩码机制——在语言建模任务中表现与掩码扩散相当或更优，表明性能差距主要源于采样与参数化设计，而非边际分布本身。
SONIC: Supersizing Motion Tracking for Natural Humanoid Whole-Body Control
Authors: Zhengyi Luo, Ye Yuan, Tingwu Wang, Chenran Li, Fernando Castañeda, Sirui Chen, Zi-Ang Cao, Jiefeng Li, David Minor, Qingwei Ben, Jinhyung Park, David Sami, Zi Wang, Xingye Da, Runyu Ding, Cyrus Hogg, Lina Song, Edy Lim, Eugene Jeong, Tairan He, Haoru Xue, Wenli Xiao, Simon Yuen, Jan Kautz, Yan Chang, Umar Iqbal, Linxi "Jim" Fan, Yuke Zhu
First: 2025-11-11T04:37:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:26:49+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://nvlabs.github.io/SONIC/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite the rise of billion-parameter foundation models trained across thousands of GPUs, similar scaling gains have not been shown for humanoid control. Current neural controllers for humanoids remain modest in size, target a limited set of behaviors, and are trained on a handful of GPUs. We show that scaling model capacity, data, and compute yields a generalist humanoid controller capable of natural, robust whole-body movements. We position motion tracking as a scalable task for humanoid control, leveraging dense supervision from diverse motion-capture data to acquire human motion priors without manual reward engineering. We build a foundation model for motion tracking by scaling along three axes: network size (1.2M to 42M parameters), dataset volume (100M+ frames from 700 hours of motion capture), and compute (21k GPU hours). Beyond demonstrating the benefits of scale, we further show downstream utility through: (1) a real-time kinematic planner bridging motion tracking to tasks such as navigation, enabling natural and interactive control, and (2) a unified token space supporting VR teleoperation and vision-language-action (VLA) models with a single policy. Through this interface, we demonstrate autonomous VLA-driven whole-body loco-manipulation requiring coordinated hand and foot placement. Scaling motion tracking exhibits favorable properties: performance improves steadily with compute and data diversity, and learned policies generalize to unseen motions, establishing motion tracking at scale as a practical foundation for humanoid control.
