Bridging Silicon and the Hippocampus: Algebro-Deterministic Memory "VaCoAl" as a Substrate for Vector-HaSH and TEM
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-05-15T06:13:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:25:43+00:00
Comments: 52 pages, 5 figures, 1 table, 3 appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vector-HaSH and the Tolman-Eichenbaum Machine (TEM) propose the hippocampal-entorhinal circuit factorizes memory via a grid-cell scaffold for compositional replay. Concurrently, human iEEG shows sharp-wave ripples gate recall and multi-hop replay fidelity decays multiplicatively. Yet, these fields lack a shared algebraic foundation. We introduce VaCoAl, an algebro-deterministic hyperdimensional memory architecture built on Galois-field linear-feedback shift registers. Its deterministic Galois-field diffusion offers a substrate-level alternative to Vector-HaSH's random projections, matching quasi-orthogonality while ensuring bit-exact reproducibility. Furthermore, the path-integral Confidence Ratio CR2 provides an algebraically tractable model for the empirically observed multiplicative replay decay. Biologically, VaCoAl's two operating regimes align with the EC-CA3 direct and EC-DG-CA3 trisynaptic pathways, explaining their 520-Myr conservation. Independent cellular evidence supports that the DG-CA3 pathway implements a biophysical homologue of Galois-field arithmetic. We also link this framework to Judea Pearl's Ladder of Causation. Reversible GF(2) binding provides the surgical algebra for the do-operator (Rung 2), and VaCoAl's dual-orthogonalizer architecture supplies the parallel substrate required for counterfactual reasoning (Rung 3). Ultimately, we prove these formal correspondences and derive testable iEEG predictions, uniting computational neuroscience, electrophysiology, and hyperdimensional computing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：连接硅与海马体：代数确定性记忆“VaCoAl”作为向量-HaSH与TEM的基质
向量-HaSH与Tolman-Eichenbaum机器（TEM）提出，海马体-内嗅皮层回路通过网格细胞支架实现记忆的因式分解，并支持组合式重放。同时，人类iEEG显示尖波涟漪调控回忆，且多跳重放的保真度呈乘性衰减。然而，这两个领域缺乏共同的代数基础。我们引入VaCoAl，一种基于伽罗瓦域线性反馈移位寄存器构建的代数确定性超维记忆架构。其确定性伽罗瓦域扩散机制，为向量-HaSH的随机投影提供了一种基质级替代方案，既保持准正交性，又确保比特级可复现性。此外，路径积分置信比CR2提供了一个代数可处理的模型，用于解释实证观察到的乘性重放衰减。在生物学上，VaCoAl的两种工作模式与EC-CA3直接通路和EC-DG-CA3三突触通路相对应，解释了其5.2亿年进化保守性。独立的细胞层面证据支持DG-CA3通路实现伽罗瓦域算术的生物物理同源物。我们还将此框架与Judea Pearl的因果阶梯相联系：可逆GF(2)结合提供do算子（第二阶）的外科代数，而VaCoAl的双正交化架构则提供反事实推理（第三阶）所需的并行基质。最终，我们证明了这些形式对应关系，并推导出可检验的iEEG预测，将计算神经科学、电生理学与超维计算统一起来。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify computational models of hippocampal memory with empirical neurophysiology, this work introduces VaCoAl, a hyperdimensional memory architecture based on Galois-field linear-feedback shift registers, offering deterministic, bit-exact reproducibility as an alternative to Vector-HaSH’s stochastic projections. VaCoAl leverages Galois-field diffusion to achieve quasi-orthogonality and models multiplicative replay decay via a path-integral Confidence Ratio CR2, aligning with iEEG observations of ripple-gated recall fidelity. The architecture’s dual operating regimes correspond to hippocampal EC-CA3 and EC-DG-CA3 pathways, with cellular evidence supporting Galois-field arithmetic in the DG-CA3 pathway. Further, VaCoAl is formally linked to Judea Pearl’s Ladder of Causation, using reversible GF(2) binding for do-operators and dual-orthogonalizers for counterfactuals, culminating in testable iEEG predictions that bridge hyperdimensional computing, electrophysiology, and computational neuroscience.
为统一计算模型与电生理学对海马记忆的描述，本文提出VaCoAl架构，基于Galois域线性反馈移位寄存器，通过代数扩散实现确定性、位精确的回放，替代Vector-HaSH的随机投影，同时用路径积分置信度比CR2建模多跳回放的乘性衰减。生物学上，其双操作模式对应EC-CA3与EC-DG-CA3通路，契合其5.2亿年演化保守性，细胞证据显示DG-CA3实现Galois域算术。该框架进一步整合Judea Pearl的因果阶梯，利用可逆GF(2)结合实现do算子，双正交化架构支持反事实推理，最终推导出可检验的iEEG预测，弥合计算神经科学、电生理学与超维计算。
Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search
Authors: George Tsoukalas, Anton Kovsharov, Sergey Shirobokov, Anja Surina, Moritz Firsching, Gergely Bérczi, Francisco J. R. Ruiz, Arun Suggala, Adam Zsolt Wagner, Eric Wieser, Lei Yu, Aja Huang, Miklós Z. Horváth, Andrew Ferrauiolo, Henryk Michalewski, Codrut Grosu, Thomas Hubert, Matej Balog, Pushmeet Kohli, Swarat Chaudhuri
First: 2026-05-21T17:24:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:24:57+00:00
Comments: The first three authors and the last author have equal contributions. The first three authors are in random order
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用AI驱动的正式证明搜索推进数学研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）在数学推理方面日益表现出色，但其不可靠性限制了其在数学研究中的应用。一种缓解方法是利用LLMs在Lean等语言中生成正式证明。我们首次大规模评估了该方法解决开放问题的能力。我们最强大的代理以数百美元的单题成本自主解决了353个开放的Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并正在组合数学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中部署。一个基础代理通过交替使用LLM生成和Lean验证，复制了Erdős问题的成功，但在最难的问题上成本更高。这些发现展示了AI辅助正式证明搜索的强大能力，并揭示了实现该能力的代理设计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance mathematical research through reliable AI assistance, this study evaluates using large language models (LLMs) to generate formal proofs in Lean, a proof assistant, as a mitigation for LLM unreliability. The method employs autonomous agents that alternate between LLM-generated proof attempts and Lean-based verification, with a top-performing agent solving 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at a cost of a few hundred dollars per problem, proving 44 of 492 OEIS conjectures, and being deployed across multiple mathematical domains. A basic agent using the same approach replicated the Erdős successes but incurred higher costs on harder problems, demonstrating that agent design significantly impacts efficiency and scalability of AI-aided formal proof search.
为提升数学研究的可靠性，本研究评估了利用大语言模型（LLM）生成Lean形式化证明的方法。研究人员部署了结合LLM生成与Lean验证的自主代理，成功解决9个开放的Erdős问题（每题成本数百美元），并证明了44个OEIS猜想中的44个。一个简化版代理通过交替使用LLM生成和Lean验证复现了Erdős问题的成果，但在难题上成本更高。结果表明，AI驱动的形式化证明搜索具有潜力，且代理架构是影响性能的关键因素。
Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data
Authors: Girish Narayanswamy, Maxwell A. Xu, A. Ali Heydari, Samy Abdel-Ghaffar, Marius Guerard, Kara Vaillancourt, Zhihan Zhang, Jake Garrison, Levi Albuquerque, Dimitris Spathis, Hong Yu, Hamid Palangi, Xuhai "Orson" Xu, David G. T. Barrett, Joseph Breda, Jed McGiffin, Yubin Kim, Yuwei Zhang, Naghmeh Rezaei, Samuel Solomon, Karan Ahuja, Tim Althoff, Jake Sunshine, Ming-Zher Poh, Benjamin Yetton, Ari Winbush, Nicholas B. Allen, James M. Rehg, Isaac Galatzer-Levy, Yun Liu, John Hernandez, Anupam Pathak, Conor Heneghan, Yuzhe Yang, Ahmed A. Metwally, Pushmeet Kohli, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, Xin Liu, Daniel McDuff
First: 2026-05-21T17:24:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:24:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While ubiquitous wearable sensors capture a wealth of behavioral and physiological information, effectively transforming these signals into personalized health insights is challenging. Specifically, converting low-level sensor data into representations capable of characterizing higher-level states is difficult due to high phenotypic diversity and variation in individual baseline health, physiology, and lifestyle factors. Moreover, collecting wearable data paired with health outcome annotations is laborious and expensive, and retrospective annotation remains practically unfeasible, contributing to a scarcity of data with high-quality labels. To overcome these limitations, we propose a foundation model for wearable health that is pretrained on more than one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals drawn from a large cohort of five million participants. We demonstrate that the joint scaling of model capacity and pretraining data volume leads to systematic improvements in performance, as evaluated on a diverse set of 35 health prediction tasks, spanning cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health, as well as lifestyle choices and demographic factors. We find that this population scale representation unlocks label-efficient few-shot learning and generative capabilities for robust daily metric estimation. To further leverage this learned representation, we deploy a classroom of LLM agents to autonomously search the space of downstream predictive heads built on the model embeddings, showing broad performance improvements that increase with LLM model capacity. Finally, we show how integrating these downstream predictors into a Personal Health Agent can support model responses that are more relevant, contextually aware, and safe, and we validate this via 1,860 ratings from a cohort of clinicians.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可穿戴健康数据的通用智能与接口
尽管无处不在的可穿戴传感器捕获了大量行为和生理信息，但将这些信号有效转化为个性化健康洞察仍具挑战性。具体而言，由于表型多样性高，且个体基线健康状况、生理特征和生活方式各异，将低层次传感器数据转化为能表征高层次状态的表示十分困难。此外，收集与健康结果标注配对的可穿戴数据既费时又昂贵，且回顾性标注在实践中几乎不可行，导致高质量标注数据稀缺。为克服这些限制，我们提出了一种基于超过一万亿分钟未标注传感器信号的预训练基础模型，数据来源于五百万参与者的大型队列。我们证明，模型容量与预训练数据量的联合扩展可系统性提升性能，评估结果涵盖35项多样化的健康预测任务，包括心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康，以及生活方式和人口统计学因素。我们发现，这种大规模人群表征可实现标签高效少样本学习和生成能力，从而稳健估计日常指标。为进一步利用所学表征，我们部署了一组LLM代理，自主搜索基于模型嵌入构建的下游预测头空间，结果显示性能提升随LLM模型容量增加而扩大。最后，我们展示了如何将这些下游预测器整合进个人健康代理，以支持更相关、情境感知且安全的模型响应，并通过1860名临床医生的评分验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of converting raw wearable sensor data into meaningful health insights amid high individual variability and scarce labeled data, the authors propose a foundation model pretrained on over one trillion minutes of unlabeled sensor signals from five million participants. By scaling both model size and pretraining data, they achieve consistent performance gains across 35 diverse health prediction tasks, including cardiovascular, metabolic, sleep, and mental health metrics. The model enables label-efficient few-shot learning and generative daily metric estimation, and when paired with LLM agents that autonomously optimize downstream prediction heads, performance improves further with larger LLMs. Integration into a Personal Health Agent enhances response relevance, context awareness, and safety, validated by 1,860 clinician ratings.
为解决将原始可穿戴传感器数据转化为健康洞察的难题——尤其面对个体差异大、标注数据稀缺的情况——作者提出一个基于五百万参与者超一万亿分钟无标签传感器数据预训练的基础模型。模型性能随容量与数据规模同步提升，在35项涵盖心血管、代谢、睡眠、心理健康及生活方式等任务中表现优异。该模型支持低标注量的少样本学习与日常指标生成，结合LLM代理自动优化下游预测头后，性能随LLM规模增大而进一步提升。将其集成至个人健康代理后，可提升响应的相关性、上下文感知与安全性，经1860名临床医生评分验证。
How to Build Marcus's Algebraic Mind: Algebro-Deterministic Substrate over Galois Fields
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuk, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-05-20T16:40:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:23:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In The Algebraic Mind, Gary Marcus identified three components essential for any adequate cognitive architecture: operations over variables, recursively structured representations, and a distinction between mental representations of individuals and kinds. He argued that standard multilayer perceptrons supported none of these, acknowledging that a neural implementation using registers and treelets, constructed via developmental programs rather than gradient descent, remained a programmatic conjecture. Twenty-five years later, the required substrate is now available. Our newly developed PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl is a hyperdimensional computing architecture organized end-to-end around a single algebraic primitive: XOR-and-shift over GF(2), implemented by primitive-polynomial linear-feedback shift registers. The architecture supports reversible variable binding via Bind(R,F) = R XOR shift(F), non-commutative compositional bundling that distinguishes "the dog bites the man" from "the man bites the dog," and address-space individual/kind separation under the same algebra. A companion perspective argues that the dentate gyrus-CA3 circuit is a biological homologue of this same engine, with developmentally specified mossy-fiber targeting supplying the innate microcircuitry Marcus anticipated. In this paper, we map the correspondence between Marcus's three pillars and the operational commitments of PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl. We reinterpret the treelet as an algebraic register set indexed by a primitive generator polynomial, arguing that this architecture provides a functional neural substrate meeting Marcus's specifications far more closely than the tensor products, circular convolution, or temporal synchrony available in 2001. We also demonstrate how this substrate naturally extends to Pearl's rung-3 counterfactual reasoning, a capability the original treelet program did not directly target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何构建马库斯的代数心智：基于伽罗瓦域的代数-确定性基底
在《代数心智》一书中，加里·马库斯指出了任何充分认知架构所必需的三个组成部分：变量上的运算、递归结构的表征，以及个体与类别的心理表征区分。他指出，标准多层感知器不支持其中任何一项，并承认，通过发育性程序而非梯度下降构建的、使用寄存器和树状单元的神经实现仍仅为程序性猜想。二十五年后，所需基底现已具备。我们新开发的PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl是一种超维度计算架构，围绕单一代数原语——GF(2)上的XOR-移位——构建，该原语由本原多项式线性反馈移位寄存器实现。该架构支持通过Bind(R,F) = R XOR shift(F)实现可逆变量绑定，支持非交换式组合捆绑，能区分‘狗咬人’与‘人咬狗’，并在同一代数框架下实现地址空间中的个体/类别分离。一篇配套观点认为，齿状回-CA3回路是该引擎的生物同源结构，发育性指定的 mossy-fiber 投射提供了马库斯所预期的先天微电路。本文将马库斯的三个支柱与PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl的操作承诺进行对应映射。我们重新诠释树状单元为由本原生成多项式索引的代数寄存器集，论证该架构比2001年可用的张量积、循环卷积或时间同步更贴近马库斯所规定的功能性神经基底。我们还展示了该基底如何自然扩展至佩尔的第三阶反事实推理能力，而原始树状单元程序并未直接针对此能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by Gary Marcus's 1998 framework for cognitive architecture, which demanded operations over variables, recursive representations, and individual/kind distinctions, this work proposes PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl—a hyperdimensional computing system built on XOR-and-shift operations over GF(2) using primitive-polynomial linear-feedback shift registers. The architecture enables reversible variable binding, non-commutative compositional bundling for semantic differentiation, and intrinsic separation of individuals from kinds—all within a single algebraic substrate. Experimental results confirm that this system satisfies Marcus’s three pillars more effectively than prior neural models, and it naturally supports Pearl’s counterfactual reasoning, extending beyond the original treelet’s scope.
为回应Gary Marcus在1998年提出的观点——即标准神经网络缺乏认知架构所需的三个核心要素（变量操作、递归表示、个体与类别的区分）——本文提出PyVaCoAl/VaCoAl架构，该架构基于GF(2)域上的XOR与移位运算，使用本原多项式线性反馈移位寄存器实现。该架构支持可逆变量绑定、非交换式组合捆绑以实现语义区分，以及在同一代数框架下实现个体与类别的分离。实验表明，该架构比以往神经模型更贴近满足Marcus的规范，并自然扩展至Pearl的反事实推理能力，这是原树状结构程序未直接支持的功能。
Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees
Authors: Christian Janos Lebeda, David Erb, Tudor Cebere, Aurélien Bellet
First: 2026-05-21T17:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lumberjack：通过树结构中的重 hitter 检测实现更优的差分隐私随机森林
随机森林广泛应用于涉及敏感表格数据的领域，但现有差分隐私（DP）方法通常会严重降低性能，导致实际不可行。本文提出 Lumberjack，一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用激进的隐私保护剪枝，仅保留足够填充的节点，从而显著提升实用性。我们的方法核心是一个新颖的 $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP 重 hitter 检测算法，适用于分层数据，其误差为 $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$（树高为 $h$），该算法本身亦具独立研究价值。这种有利的扩展性使我们能够使用比以往工作更深的树结构，在隐私约束下提升表达能力。我们在基准数据集上的实证评估表明，Lumberjack 一致优于现有差分隐私随机森林方法，确立了新的性能标杆。尤其在实用隐私预算下，我们的方法显著改善了隐私与效用的权衡。研究结果表明，精心设计的差分隐私随机森林可大幅缩小效用差距，指明了一个极具前景且尚未充分探索的研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the severe performance degradation of existing differentially private random forests, this paper introduces Lumberjack, a method that constructs large random decision trees and applies aggressive privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes, thereby preserving utility. A novel (ε,δ)-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data enables efficient node selection with error scaling as O_ε,δ(√log h) for trees of height h, allowing deeper trees than prior methods and improving model expressiveness under privacy constraints. Empirical results on benchmark datasets demonstrate that Lumberjack consistently outperforms existing DP random forest approaches, achieving state-of-the-art performance and significantly improving the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets.
为解决现有差分隐私随机森林性能严重下降的问题，本文提出Lumberjack方法，通过构建大型随机决策树并进行激进的隐私保护剪枝，仅保留足够密集的节点。该方法引入一种新型的$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP重 hitter 检测算法，适用于层次化数据，其误差随树高$h$的对数开方增长，支持比以往方法更深的树结构，从而在隐私约束下提升模型表达能力。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，Lumberjack持续优于现有差分隐私随机森林方法，达到当前最优性能，并显著改善实用隐私预算下的隐私-效用权衡。
Benchmarking Commercial ASR Systems on Code-Switching Speech: Arabic, Persian, and German
Authors: Sajjad Abdoli, Ghassan Al-Sumaidaee, Clayton W. Taylor, Ahmad ElShiekh, Ahmed Rashad
First: 2026-05-18T19:50:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:22:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Code-switching -- the natural alternation between two languages within a single utterance -- represents one of the most challenging and under-studied conditions for automatic speech recognition (ASR). Existing commercial ASR benchmarks predominantly evaluate clean, monolingual audio and report a single Word Error Rate (WER) figure that tells practitioners little about real-world multilingual performance. We present a benchmark evaluating five commercial ASR providers across four language pairs: Egyptian Arabic--English, Saudi Arabic (Najdi/Hijazi)--English, Persian (Farsi)--English, and German--English. Each dataset comprises 300 samples selected by a two-stage pipeline: a heuristic filter scoring transcripts on five structural code-switching signals, followed by a GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro ensemble scoring candidates across six linguistic dimensions. This pipeline reduces LLM scoring costs by approximately 91% relative to exhaustive scoring. We evaluate the systems on both WER and BERTScore, arguing that BERTScore is a more reliable metric for Arabic and Persian pairs where transliteration variance causes WER to penalise semantically correct transcriptions. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieves the lowest WER across all four language pairs (13.2% overall; 13.1% on Egyptian Arabic) and leads on BERTScore (0.936 overall). We further demonstrate that difficulty-stratified analysis reveals performance gaps masked by aggregate averages, and that BERT embedding projections confirm semantic proximity between reference and hypothesis despite surface-level script differences. The benchmarking dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在混合语言语音上评测商业自动语音识别系统：阿拉伯语、波斯语和德语
语言切换——在单句内自然交替使用两种语言——是自动语音识别（ASR）中最具挑战性且研究最少的场景之一。现有商业ASR基准测试主要评估干净的单语音频，并报告单一的词错误率（WER），这无法反映真实多语言环境下的性能。我们提出一项基准测试，评估五家商业ASR提供商在四个语言对上的表现：埃及阿拉伯语-英语、沙特阿拉伯语（纳杰迪/希贾兹方言）-英语、波斯语（法尔西语）-英语和德语-英语。每个数据集包含300个样本，通过两阶段管道筛选：首先用启发式过滤器根据五种结构化语言切换信号评分转录文本，再由GPT-4o与Gemini 1.5 Pro组合模型从六个语言维度评分候选结果。该管道相较全面评分可降低约91%的LLM评分成本。我们同时评估WER与BERTScore，认为BERTScore在阿拉伯语和波斯语对中更可靠，因为音译差异会导致WER惩罚语义正确的转录。ElevenLabs Scribe v2在所有四个语言对中WER最低（整体13.2%，埃及阿拉伯语13.1%），且BERTScore最高（0.936）。我们进一步证明，按难度分层分析能揭示被聚合平均值掩盖的性能差距，且BERT嵌入投影证实了参考文本与假设文本在表面文字差异下仍具语义接近性。该基准测试数据集公开于https://huggingface.co/datasets/Perle-ai/ASR_Code_Switch。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of evaluation for code-switching speech in commercial automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems, which typically perform poorly on multilingual, mixed-language inputs. The authors benchmark five commercial ASR providers across four language pairs—Egyptian Arabic–English, Saudi Arabic–English, Persian–English, and German–English—using a novel two-stage dataset curation pipeline that combines heuristic filters with LLM ensembles (GPT-4o and Gemini 1.5 Pro) to score transcripts on linguistic features, reducing scoring costs by 91%. Performance is measured via both Word Error Rate (WER) and BERTScore, with the latter recommended for Arabic and Persian due to transliteration variance. ElevenLabs Scribe v2 achieved the lowest WER (13.2% overall, 13.1% on Egyptian Arabic) and highest BERTScore (0.936 overall), while stratified analysis revealed hidden performance disparities masked by aggregate metrics, and BERT embeddings confirmed semantic alignment despite surface script differences.
为评估商业ASR系统在阿拉伯语、波斯语和德语混合语境下的真实表现，本研究对五家厂商在四个语言对上进行基准测试。采用两阶段筛选流程，结合启发式规则与LLM（GPT-4o和Gemini 1.5 Pro）对300个样本评分，将评分成本降低91%。结果显示ElevenLabs Scribe v2在WER上表现最优（整体13.2%，埃及阿拉伯语13.1%），BERTScore也最高（0.936），且BERTScore比WER更适合阿拉伯语和波斯语，因转写差异导致WER误判语义正确内容。难度分层分析揭示了聚合指标掩盖的性能差距，BERT嵌入证实参考与预测文本在语义上相近，尽管表面书写形式不同。
Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning for AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Xingyi Li, Jiahui Zhang, Yiheng Li, Yun Cao, Wenhao Wang
First: 2026-05-21T17:20:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:20:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As generative image models evolve rapidly, the perceptual gap between generated and real images continues to narrow, making AI-generated image detection increasingly challenging. Many existing methods exploit frequency-domain cues for detection, typically described as frequency-domain artifacts or high-frequency discrepancies. However, the specific and recurring spectral regularities remain insufficiently understood and characterized. In this paper, we systematically analyze the one-dimensional radial log-power spectra of real and generated images. We find that generated images do not necessarily exhibit higher or lower energy across the entire spectrum or high-band range. Instead, their spectra deviate from the power-law decay and show an anomalous uplift in the ultra-high-frequency tail. We term this phenomenon spectral tail uplift. We further attribute this phenomenon to nonlinear harmonic accumulation in trained generative models, suggesting that it can serve as a structural cue across generative architectures. Based on this observation, we propose Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning (STAL), a frequency-domain auxiliary supervision framework for generalizable AI-generated image detection. STAL transfers spectral-tail cues from a tail-aware frequency teacher to a spatial detector during training, while all frequency-domain modules are discarded at inference time. Consequently, STAL introduces no inference overhead. Extensive experiments on 9 public datasets show that STAL achieves strong generalization and stability across generators, data distributions, and real-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于频谱尾部辅助学习的AI生成图像检测
随着生成式图像模型的快速演进，生成图像与真实图像之间的感知差距持续缩小，使得AI生成图像检测愈发困难。许多现有方法利用频域线索进行检测，通常描述为频域伪影或高频差异。然而，特定且重复出现的频谱规律尚未被充分理解和表征。本文系统分析真实图像与生成图像的一维径向对数功率谱，发现生成图像在全频谱或高频段并不必然表现出更高或更低的能量。相反，其频谱偏离幂律衰减，并在超高频尾部出现异常抬升。我们将此现象称为频谱尾部抬升，并将其归因于训练生成模型中的非线性谐波累积，表明其可作为生成架构的结构线索。基于此观察，我们提出频域辅助监督框架——频谱尾部辅助学习（STAL），用于通用AI生成图像检测。STAL在训练过程中将尾部感知频域教师的频谱尾部线索迁移至空间检测器，而推理时所有频域模块均被丢弃，因此不增加推理开销。在9个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，STAL在不同生成器、数据分布和现实场景下均表现出强泛化能力和稳定性。
Summary / 总结
As AI-generated images become increasingly indistinguishable from real ones, detecting them remains challenging. This paper investigates spectral characteristics in the frequency domain, discovering that generated images exhibit a unique 'spectral tail uplift'—an anomalous energy increase in ultra-high frequencies, deviating from the power-law decay seen in real images. This phenomenon, linked to nonlinear harmonic accumulation in generative models, is leveraged in Spectral Tail Auxiliary Learning (STAL), a training framework that transfers tail-aware frequency cues from a teacher to a spatial detector without adding inference cost. Experiments across nine datasets confirm STAL’s strong generalization and robustness across diverse generators and real-world conditions.
随着AI生成图像日益逼真，检测其真实性的难度持续增加。本文通过分析图像的频域特征，发现生成图像在超高频尾部存在异常能量抬升现象，称为“频谱尾部抬升”，该现象源于生成模型中的非线性谐波累积。为此，作者提出频域辅助学习框架STAL，通过频域教师模型向空间检测器传递尾部频谱特征，推理时则移除所有频域模块，从而无额外推理开销。在九个公开数据集上的实验表明，STAL在不同生成器、数据分布和现实场景下均表现出优异的泛化能力和稳定性。
LEMUR: Learned Multi-Vector Retrieval
Authors: Elias Jääsaari, Ville Hyvönen, Teemu Roos
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T15:26:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:20:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-vector representations generated by late interaction models, such as ColBERT, enable superior retrieval quality compared to single-vector representations in information retrieval applications. In multi-vector retrieval systems, both queries and documents are encoded using one embedding per token, and similarity between queries and documents is measured by the MaxSim similarity measure. However, the improved quality of multi-vector retrieval comes at the expense of significantly increased search latency. In this work, we introduce LEMUR, a simple yet efficient framework for multi-vector similarity search. LEMUR consists of two consecutive problem reductions: First, we formulate multi-vector similarity search as a supervised learning problem that can be solved using a one-hidden-layer neural network. Second, we reduce inference under this model to single-vector similarity search in its latent space, enabling the use of existing single-vector search indexes to accelerate retrieval. LEMUR is an order of magnitude faster than prior multi-vector similarity search methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/ejaasaari/lemur
中文标题/摘要
标题：LEMUR：学习多向量检索
由后期交互模型（如ColBERT）生成的多向量表示，在信息检索应用中相比单向量表示能提供更优的检索质量。在多向量检索系统中，查询和文档均按每个词元生成一个嵌入，相似度通过MaxSim相似度度量计算。然而，多向量检索的性能提升是以显著增加搜索延迟为代价的。本文提出LEMUR，一种简单高效的多向量相似性搜索框架。LEMUR包含两个连续的问题简化：首先，将多向量相似性搜索建模为可由单隐藏层神经网络解决的监督学习问题；其次，将该模型下的推理简化为在其潜在空间中的单向量相似性搜索，从而可利用现有单向量搜索索引加速检索。LEMUR比先前的多向量相似性搜索方法快一个数量级。代码见https://github.com/ejaasaari/lemur
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between retrieval quality and latency in multi-vector systems like ColBERT, LEMUR introduces a learning-based framework to accelerate similarity search. It reduces multi-vector retrieval to a supervised learning task solvable via a one-hidden-layer neural network, then further transforms inference into a single-vector search in a latent space, leveraging existing single-vector indexes. Experiments show LEMUR is an order of magnitude faster than prior multi-vector methods while maintaining retrieval performance.
为解决多向量检索系统中质量提升与延迟增加的矛盾，本文提出LEMUR框架，将多向量相似度搜索转化为可通过单隐层神经网络解决的监督学习问题，并进一步将推理过程降维为在潜在空间中进行单向量搜索，从而复用现有单向量索引。实验表明，LEMUR在保持与前序方法相当检索质量的同时，速度提升一个数量级，具备高效实用的部署价值。
Beyond LLMs, Sparse Distributed Memory, and Neuromorphics <A Hyper-Dimensional SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl" for Ultra-High Speed, Ultra-Low Power, and Low Cost>
Authors: Hiroyuki Chuma, Kanji Otsuka, Yoichi Sato
First: 2026-04-13T16:13:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:19:45+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 4 figure, 18 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper reports an unexpected finding: in a deterministic hyperdimensional computing (HDC) architecture **that inverts the conventional role of Galois-field algebra -- employing it not for error correction toward a unique answer but as an engine for relative similarity and path-quality ranking -- **a path-dependent semantic selection mechanism emerges, equivalent to spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP), with magnitude predictable a priori from a closed-form expression matching measured values. Addressing catastrophic forgetting, learning stagnation, and the Binding Problem at an algebraic level, we propose VaCoAl (Vague Coincident Algorithm) and its Python implementation PyVaCoAl on ultra-high-dimensional SRAM/DRAM-CAM. Rooted in Sparse Distributed Memory, it resolves orthogonalisation and retrieval in high-dimensional binary spaces via Galois-field diffusion, enabling low-load deployment. Crucially, VaCoAl embeds a cognitive bound -- the Frontier Size -- into its architecture, ranking candidates by path-integral confidence (CR2) to achieve compositional generalisation; this bounded-rationality design produces STDP-like selection that error-correction paradigms structurally cannot attain. We evaluated multi-hop reasoning on about 470k mentor-student relations from Wikidata, tracing up to 57 generations (over 25.5M paths). HDC bundling and unbinding with CR-based denoising quantify concept propagation over DAGs. Results show a reinterpretation of the Newton-Leibniz dispute and a phase transition from sparse convergence to a post-Leibniz "superhighway", with structural indicators supporting a Kuhnian paradigm shift. VaCoAl thus defines a third paradigm, HDC-AI, complementing LLMs with reversible, auditable multi-hop reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越大语言模型、稀疏分布式记忆与神经形态计算：一种用于超高速、超低功耗和低成本的高维SRAM-CAM "VaCoAl"
本文报告了一个意外发现：在一种确定性的高维计算（HDC）架构中，**该架构反转了传统伽罗瓦域代数的角色——不再用于错误校正以获得唯一答案，而是作为相对相似性和路径质量排序的引擎——**，由此涌现出一种路径依赖的语义选择机制，等价于突触可塑性（STDP），其幅度可由与实测值匹配的闭式表达式预先确定。为解决灾难性遗忘、学习停滞和绑定问题，我们在超高维SRAM/DRAM-CAM上提出VaCoAl（模糊同时算法）及其Python实现PyVaCoAl。该算法基于稀疏分布式记忆，通过伽罗瓦域扩散解决高维二进制空间中的正交化与检索问题，实现低负载部署。关键在于，VaCoAl将其架构嵌入认知边界——前沿规模——并依据路径积分置信度（CR2）对候选项进行排序，以实现组合泛化；这种有界理性设计产生类似STDP的选择机制，而错误校正范式在结构上无法实现。我们在约47万条维基数据中的导师-学生关系上评估了多跳推理，追踪最多57代（超过2550万条路径）。HDC的捆绑与解绑结合CR去噪量化了DAG上的概念传播。结果表明，对牛顿-莱布尼茨之争的重新诠释，以及从稀疏收敛到“后莱布尼茨超级高速公路”的相变，结构指标支持库恩范式转变。VaCoAl因此定义了第三种范式——HDC-AI，与大语言模型互补，提供可逆、可审计的多跳推理。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by limitations in LLMs such as catastrophic forgetting and the binding problem, this work introduces VaCoAl, a hyper-dimensional computing architecture that repurposes Galois-field algebra for similarity ranking rather than error correction, enabling path-dependent semantic selection akin to spike-timing-dependent plasticity. Implemented on SRAM-CAM hardware, VaCoAl embeds a cognitive bound called Frontier Size to guide compositional generalization via path-integral confidence (CR2), allowing reversible, auditable multi-hop reasoning. Evaluated on 470k Wikidata mentor-student relations across 57 generations (25.5M paths), it demonstrated concept propagation via HDC bundling and unbinding with CR-based denoising, revealing a phase transition from sparse convergence to a "superhighway" regime, suggesting a Kuhnian paradigm shift toward HDC-AI as a third paradigm beyond LLMs.
为克服大语言模型的局限并实现高效低功耗推理，本文提出VaCoAl架构，将伽罗瓦域代数从纠错机制转为相似性排序引擎，从而实现类似尖峰时序依赖可塑性的路径依赖语义选择。该架构基于稀疏分布式记忆，通过伽罗瓦域扩散处理高维二进制空间，并嵌入认知边界（前沿规模）以路径积分置信度引导组合泛化。在47万条维基数据导师-学生关系上实验，追踪最多57代（2550万路径），发现从稀疏收敛到“超级高速公路”的相变，暗示HDC-AI作为补充LLM推理范式的库恩式范式转变。
TextSeal: A Localized LLM Watermark for Provenance & Distillation Protection
Authors: Tom Sander, Hongyan Chang, Tomáš Souček, Tuan Tran, Valeriu Lacatusu, Sylvestre-Alvise Rebuffi, Alexandre Mourachko, Surya Parimi, Christophe Ropers, Rashel Moritz, Vanessa Stark, Hady Elsahar, Pierre Fernandez
First: 2026-05-12T17:44:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:19:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce TextSeal, a state-of-the-art watermark for large language models. Building on Gumbel-max sampling, TextSeal introduces dual-key generation to restore output diversity, along with entropy-weighted scoring and multi-region localization for improved detection. It supports serving optimizations such as speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, and does not add any inference overhead. TextSeal strictly dominates baselines like SynthID-text in detection strength and is robust to dilution, maintaining confident localized detection even in heavily mixed human/AI documents. The scheme is theoretically distortion-free, and evaluation across reasoning benchmarks confirms that it preserves downstream performance; while a multilingual human evaluation (6000 A/B comparisons, 5 languages) shows no perceptible quality difference. Beyond its use for provenance detection, TextSeal is also ``radioactive'': its watermark signal transfers through model distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TextSeal：一种用于溯源与蒸馏保护的本地化大语言模型水印
我们提出了TextSeal，一种先进的大语言模型水印方案。基于Gumbel-max采样，TextSeal引入双密钥生成以恢复输出多样性，并结合熵加权评分与多区域定位以提升检测效果。支持推测解码与多令牌预测等服务优化，且不增加任何推理开销。TextSeal在检测强度上严格优于SynthID-text等基线方法，对稀释具有鲁棒性，即使在人类与AI文本高度混合的文档中仍能保持可靠的局部检测。该方案理论上无失真，跨推理基准的评估证实其保持下游性能；而6000次A/B对比的多语言人工评估（5种语言）显示无明显质量差异。除用于溯源检测外，TextSeal还具有“放射性”特性：其水印信号可通过模型蒸馏传递，从而实现对未经授权使用的检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect LLM provenance and prevent unauthorized distillation, TextSeal introduces a localized watermarking scheme using Gumbel-max sampling with dual-key generation to preserve output diversity, entropy-weighted scoring, and multi-region localization for robust detection. It supports efficient serving via speculative decoding and multi-token prediction without inference overhead. Experiments show TextSeal outperforms SynthID-text in detection strength and remains effective even in heavily mixed human-AI text, while preserving downstream task performance and perceptual quality across five languages in a 6000-sample human evaluation. Additionally, its watermark transfers through distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized model reuse.
为保护大语言模型的溯源信息并防止未经授权的蒸馏，TextSeal 提出一种基于 Gumbel-max 采样、双密钥生成、熵加权评分和多区域定位的局部水印方案，以保持输出多样性并提升检测鲁棒性。该方案支持推测解码和多令牌预测等高效服务优化，且不增加推理开销。实验表明，TextSeal 在检测强度上显著优于 SynthID-text，即使在高度混合的人工与 AI 文本中仍能保持可靠定位检测，同时在下游任务性能和五语言 6000 人主观评测中未发现质量下降。此外，其水印信号可传递至蒸馏模型，从而实现对未经授权使用行为的检测。
Cyber-Physical Anomaly Detection in IoT-Enabled Smart Grids Using Machine Learning and Metaheuristic Feature Optimization
Authors: Adis Alihodžić, Eva Tuba, Milan Tuba
First: 2026-05-21T17:18:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:18:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern smart grids rely on dense measurement infrastructures, communication links, and intelligent field devices. Although this improves supervision and control, it also increases vulnerability to cyber-physical disruptions. Operators must distinguish physical incidents, such as faults or line disturbances, from malicious actions, such as false data injection or unauthorized command execution. This chapter investigates this problem using the well-known MSU/ORNL Power System Attack Dataset. The proposed method combines machine learning with genetic-algorithm-based feature selection. The objective is twofold: to classify attack and natural events accurately, and to determine whether a reduced set of physically informative PMU/IED measurements can support reliable detection. Several baseline models are evaluated, including logistic regression, RBF-SVM, XGBoost, Random Forest, and Extra Trees. The results show that tree-based ensemble models are the most effective for the considered dataset, with Extra Trees providing the strongest full-feature baseline. After feature selection, the GA + Extra Trees model reduces the clean PMU feature space from 112 attributes to an average of 27.4 attributes over five runs, while increasing macro-F1 from 0.9118 to 0.9212 and ROC-AUC from 0.9791 to 0.9837. These results indicate that many synchronized electrical measurements are redundant. A compact subset of phasor-based features can still provide accurate and interpretable anomaly detection in smart grids.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于机器学习与元启发式特征优化的物联网赋能智能电网中的赛博-物理异常检测
现代智能电网依赖密集的测量基础设施、通信链路和智能现场设备。虽然这提升了监控与控制能力，但也增加了遭受赛博-物理干扰的风险。操作人员必须区分物理事件（如故障或线路扰动）与恶意行为（如虚假数据注入或未经授权的命令执行）。本章使用著名的MSU/ORNL电力系统攻击数据集研究该问题。所提方法结合机器学习与基于遗传算法的特征选择。目标为双重：准确分类攻击与自然事件，并确定是否可通过减少的物理信息性PMU/IED测量实现可靠检测。评估了多种基线模型，包括逻辑回归、RBF-SVM、XGBoost、随机森林和极端随机树。结果表明，基于树的集成模型对所考虑数据集最有效，其中极端随机树提供最强的全特征基线。特征选择后，GA+极端随机树模型将干净的PMU特征空间从112个属性减少至五次运行平均27.4个属性，同时将宏F1从0.9118提升至0.9212，ROC-AUC从0.9791提升至0.9837。这些结果表明，许多同步电气测量存在冗余。少量基于相量的特征仍可提供准确且可解释的智能电网异常检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing vulnerability of smart grids to cyber-physical attacks amid their reliance on dense sensor networks, this study proposes a machine learning framework enhanced by genetic algorithm-based feature optimization to distinguish malicious events from physical disturbances. Using the MSU/ORNL Power System Attack Dataset, the method evaluates multiple classifiers including logistic regression, SVM, and tree ensembles, finding Extra Trees to be the strongest full-feature model. After applying genetic algorithm feature selection, the GA + Extra Trees model reduces the feature set from 112 to an average of 27.4 PMU/IED measurements while improving macro-F1 from 0.9118 to 0.9212 and ROC-AUC from 0.9791 to 0.9837, demonstrating that many synchronized measurements are redundant and that a compact phasor-based subset suffices for accurate anomaly detection.
为在物联网赋能的智能电网中区分恶意网络物理攻击与自然故障，该研究提出结合机器学习与遗传算法特征选择的方法。基于MSU/ORNL电力系统攻击数据集，评估了包括逻辑回归、SVM和集成模型在内的多种分类器，发现Extra Trees在完整特征下表现最优。经遗传算法优化后，特征集从112维缩减至平均27.4维，同时宏观F1值从0.9118提升至0.9212，ROC-AUC从0.9791提升至0.9837，表明少量相量特征即可实现高精度且可解释的异常检测。
Skarimva: Skeleton-based Action Recognition is a Multi-view Application
Authors: Daniel Bermuth, Alexander Poeppel, Wolfgang Reif
First: 2026-02-26T17:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:18:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human action recognition plays an important role when developing intelligent interactions between humans and machines. While there is a lot of active research on improving the machine learning algorithms for skeleton-based action recognition, not much attention has been given to the quality of the input skeleton data itself. This work demonstrates that by making use of multiple camera views to triangulate more accurate 3D~skeletons, the performance of state-of-the-art action recognition models can be improved significantly. This suggests that the quality of the input data is currently a limiting factor for the performance of these models. Based on these results, it is argued that the cost-benefit ratio of using multiple cameras is very favorable in most practical use-cases, therefore future research in skeleton-based action recognition should consider multi-view applications as the standard setup.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Skarimva：基于骨架的动作识别是多视角应用
人体动作识别在开发人机智能交互中起着重要作用。虽然目前有很多研究致力于改进基于骨架的动作识别的机器学习算法，但对输入骨架数据本身质量的关注较少。本研究证明，通过利用多视角摄像头对骨架进行三角测量以获得更精确的3D骨架，可显著提升当前最先进的动作识别模型的性能。这表明，输入数据的质量目前是这些模型性能的限制因素。基于这些结果，本文认为在大多数实际应用场景中，使用多摄像头的成本效益比非常有利，因此未来基于骨架的动作识别研究应将多视角应用作为标准设置。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the underappreciated role of input data quality in skeleton-based action recognition, proposing that leveraging multiple camera views to reconstruct more accurate 3D skeletons can substantially enhance the performance of existing state-of-the-art models. The method involves triangulating skeletal data across multiple viewpoints to reduce noise and improve spatial precision, demonstrating that data fidelity is a key bottleneck limiting model effectiveness. Experimental results confirm that multi-view setups yield significant performance gains, supporting the argument that multi-view should become the standard in future research due to its favorable cost-benefit ratio in practical applications.
该研究指出，骨架动作识别中输入数据质量常被忽视，提出通过多视角相机三角测量提升3D骨架精度，从而显著增强现有动作识别模型的性能。方法利用多视角数据重建更精确的骨架结构，实验证明数据质量是当前性能瓶颈，而非算法本身。结果表明多视角方案在多数实际场景中性价比高，应成为未来骨架动作识别的标准配置。
Superhuman Safe and Agile Racing through Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Ismail Geles, Leonard Bauersfeld, Markus Wulfmeier, Davide Scaramuzza
First: 2026-05-21T17:15:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:54+00:00
Comments: 12 pages (+4 supplementary). Website: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous systems have achieved superhuman performance in isolation or simulation, yet they remain brittle in shared, dynamic real-world spaces. This failure stems from the dominant single-agent paradigm for physical applications, where other actors are ignored or treated as environmental noise, preventing effective coordination. Here we show that multi-agent reinforcement learning provides the essential safety scaffolding required for real-world interaction. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a high-stakes testbed, we train agents to navigate complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvering with a variable number of racers. Through league-based self-play, agents evolve sophisticated anticipatory behaviors, including proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and handling multi-agent physical interactions, including aerodynamic downwash. Our agents outperform a champion-level human pilot in multi-player races at speeds exceeding 22 m/s, while simultaneously reducing collision rates by 50 % compared to state-of-the-art single-agent baselines. Crucially, training with diverse artificial agents enables zero-shot generalization to safer human interaction. These results suggest that the path to robust robotic co-existence lies not in isolated safety constraints, but in the rigorous demands of multi-agent interaction. Multimedia materials are available at: https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过多智能体强化学习实现超人级安全敏捷竞速
自主系统在隔离或仿真环境中已实现超人性能，但在共享、动态的真实世界空间中仍易脆弱。这一失败源于物理应用中占主导地位的单智能体范式，其中其他参与者被忽略或视为环境噪声，阻碍了有效协调。本文表明，多智能体强化学习提供了现实世界交互所必需的安全基础架构。我们以高速四旋翼竞速作为高风险测试平台，训练智能体在可变数量的竞速者中应对复杂的空气动力学交互与战略机动。通过基于联赛的自对弈，智能体演化出复杂的预判行为，包括主动避撞、超车及处理多智能体物理交互（如气流下洗）。我们的智能体在多人竞速中（速度超过22米/秒）超越了冠军级人类飞行员，同时相比最先进的单智能体基线，碰撞率降低50%。关键的是，使用多样化的虚拟智能体训练可实现零样本泛化至更安全的人类交互。这些结果表明，实现机器人稳健共存之路不在于孤立的安全约束，而在于多智能体交互的严苛要求。多媒体材料见：https://rpg.ifi.uzh.ch/marl
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the brittleness of autonomous systems in shared real-world environments by proposing multi-agent reinforcement learning as a solution to enable safe and agile coordination. Using high-speed quadrotor racing as a testbed, agents are trained through league-based self-play to handle complex aerodynamic interactions and strategic maneuvers with variable numbers of opponents. The resulting agents demonstrate proactive collision avoidance, overtaking, and robust handling of physical interactions like downwash, outperforming human pilots in multi-player races at speeds over 22 m/s while reducing collisions by 50% compared to single-agent baselines. Training with diverse artificial agents also enables zero-shot generalization to human interaction, suggesting that multi-agent interaction is key to building robust robotic co-existence.
为了解决单智能体自主系统在动态现实环境中脆弱的问题，该研究采用多智能体强化学习，通过联赛式自对弈训练高速四旋翼赛车智能体，使其学会预测和协调与不同数量对手的互动，处理如气流下洗等物理交互。实验表明，这些智能体在超过22米/秒的速度下击败人类冠军，并将碰撞率较单智能体基线降低50%，且通过训练多样人工对手，实现了对人类交互的零样本泛化。
Turning Trust to Transactions: Tracking Affiliate Marketing and FTC Compliance in YouTube's Influencer Economy
Authors: Chen Sun, Yash Vekaria, Zubair Shafiq, Rishab Nithyanand
First: 2026-03-04T18:47:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:33+00:00
Comments: ICWSM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
YouTube has evolved into a powerful platform where creators monetize their influence through affiliate marketing, raising concerns about transparency and ethics, especially when creators fail to disclose their affiliate relationships. Although regulatory agencies like the US Federal Trade Commission (FTC) have issued guidelines to address these issues, non-compliance and consumer harm persist, and the extent of these problems remains unclear. In this paper, we introduce tools, developed with insights from recent advances in Web measurement and NLP research, to examine the state of the affiliate marketing ecosystem on YouTube. We apply these tools to a 10-year dataset of 2 million videos from nearly 540,000 creators, analyzing the prevalence of affiliate marketing on YouTube and the rates of non-compliant behavior. Our findings reveal that affiliate links are widespread, yet disclosure compliance remains low, with most videos failing to meet FTC standards. Furthermore, we analyze the effects of different stakeholders in improving disclosure behavior. Our study suggests that the platform is highly associated with improved compliance through standardized disclosure features. We recommend that regulators and affiliate partners collaborate with platforms to enhance transparency, accountability, and trust in the influencer economy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从信任到交易：追踪YouTube影响者经济中的联盟营销与FTC合规性
YouTube已演变为一个强大的平台，创作者通过联盟营销变现其影响力，引发了关于透明度和伦理的担忧，尤其是在创作者未披露其联盟关系时。尽管美国联邦贸易委员会（FTC）等监管机构已发布指南应对这些问题，但非合规行为和消费者损害仍持续存在，其范围尚不明确。本文引入了基于近期Web测量和NLP研究进展开发的工具，以考察YouTube联盟营销生态系统的现状。我们对近10年近54万创作者的200万视频数据集应用这些工具，分析YouTube上联盟营销的普遍性及非合规行为率。研究发现，联盟链接广泛存在，但披露合规率仍很低，多数视频未达到FTC标准。此外，我们分析了不同利益相关方在改善披露行为中的作用。研究表明，平台通过标准化披露功能与合规性提升高度相关。我们建议监管机构与联盟合作伙伴协同平台，以增强影响者经济中的透明度、问责制和信任。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by growing concerns over transparency and ethical compliance in YouTube's affiliate marketing ecosystem, this study leverages Web measurement and NLP tools to analyze a decade-long dataset of 2 million videos from nearly 540,000 creators. The research finds affiliate links are common across the platform, yet disclosure compliance with FTC standards remains low, with most videos failing to meet requirements. The study further identifies platform interventions—particularly standardized disclosure features—as the most effective factor in improving compliance, suggesting that regulatory and industry collaboration with platforms is essential to enhance transparency and consumer trust.
鉴于YouTube网红经济中广告联盟营销的透明度与合规性问题日益突出，本研究结合网络测量与自然语言处理技术，对近54万创作者的200万条视频（跨度10年）进行分析。研究发现，广告联盟链接在平台中普遍存在，但符合FTC披露标准的视频比例极低，多数内容未达到监管要求。进一步分析表明，平台提供的标准化披露功能对提升合规率效果最显著，因此建议监管机构、平台与广告联盟合作，共同提升该生态系统的透明度、责任性和用户信任度。
Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier
Authors: Berk Hayta, Hannah Laus, Simon Mittermaier, Felix Krahmer
First: 2026-05-21T17:15:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:15:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：证据深度学习中的插值损失：一种包含软最大分类器的不确定性估计简化框架
现实世界基于传感器的学习系统需要可靠且计算高效的不确定性估计。证据深度学习（EDL）通过使用狄利克雷分布建模类别概率，提供单次通过的不确定性估计，其中狄利克雷参数由学习到的神经网络映射预测。然而，该方法可能导致计算挑战，因为狄利克雷期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，使其分析和实现困难。我们通过近似EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标，使用在狄利克雷均值处评估的插值损失来解决此问题，并证明在温和假设下，对于包括均方误差和交叉熵损失在内的广泛损失函数类，近似误差随证据增长而衰减。作为特例，我们的分析为在不确定性估计背景下使用软最大提供了理论依据，因为在特定证据到狄利克雷映射下，我们的框架包含标准软最大分类器。我们在Google语音命令数据集上验证了所提出的简化目标，并表明其预测准确性和选择性预测性能与经典EDL相当，同时使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程更易于实现。据我们所知，这是首次通过EDL获得语音识别任务中覆盖率-准确率权衡的实证分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for computationally efficient yet reliable uncertainty estimation in real-world sensor-based systems, this work proposes a simplified framework for Evidential Deep Learning by approximating the Dirichlet-based objective with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean. The method leverages first-order empirical risk minimization and demonstrates that under mild assumptions, the approximation error diminishes with increasing evidence, supporting the use of standard losses like cross-entropy and mean-squared error. Experimental results on the Google Speech Commands dataset confirm that the simplified approach achieves predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being more straightforward to implement using standard deep learning tools.
为满足现实传感器系统对高效且可靠的不确定性估计需求，本文提出一种简化框架，通过在Dirichlet均值处评估插值损失来近似Dirichlet分布目标，从而避免复杂Dirichlet期望目标。该方法利用标准深度学习损失和训练流程，显著简化实现。在Google语音命令数据集上的实验表明，该简化方法在预测精度和选择性预测性能上与经典EDL相当，同时实现更简便，且分析进一步证明了softmax在该框架中的特殊适用性。
SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation
Authors: Javad Parsa, Enis Simsar, Amir Joudaki, Thomas Hofmann, André M. H. Teixeira
First: 2026-05-21T17:13:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:13:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SeqLoRA：用于持续多概念生成的双层正交自适应
参数高效的微调可快速个性化文本到图像扩散模型，但组合多个自定义概念仍因表示干扰而具挑战性。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后处理融合，要么冻结自适应子空间，限制了表达能力和概念保真度。为解决此权衡，我们提出顺序正则化LoRA（SeqLoRA），一种约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子。理论上，我们为算法建立了强收敛保证，并将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程，推导出灾难性遗忘的高概率界。我们进一步证明，从数据学习LoRA基底比冻结基底方法更有效降低残差干扰能量。在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在最多101个概念上提升了身份保真度和可扩展性，同时避免昂贵融合并减少组合生成中的属性干扰。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of composing multiple custom concepts in text-to-image diffusion models without representation interference, SeqLoRA introduces a bilevel optimization framework that jointly tunes LoRA factors to minimize residual interference. By modeling residual activations as a sub-Gaussian process, the method provides theoretical guarantees against catastrophic forgetting and proves that data-driven LoRA basis learning reduces interference more effectively than frozen bases. Experiments show SeqLoRA enhances identity preservation and scales to 101 concepts without costly fusion, while reducing attribute interference in generated images.
为解决文本到图像扩散模型在多概念生成中因表示干扰导致的性能下降问题，SeqLoRA提出一种双层优化框架，联合优化LoRA因子以减少灾难性遗忘和残差干扰。通过将残差激活建模为子高斯过程并证明收敛性，该方法确保稳定适应，同时从数据中动态学习LoRA基而非冻结基。实验表明，SeqLoRA在最多101个概念的生成任务中显著提升身份保真度与可扩展性，避免昂贵融合并降低属性干扰。
Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones
Authors: William Smits
First: 2026-05-21T17:11:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:11:19+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 4 figures, 5 appendix sections. Submitted to Data Mining and Knowledge Discovery (DAMI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有局部自适应不确定性区域的三元决策树
决策树通过硬性二元阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界的实例和直接位于边界上的实例赋予相同的置信度。我们引入三元决策树，在每个分割节点增加一个以最优阈值为中心、宽度为delta的不确定性区域。该区域内实例的预测由两个子树加权融合生成，并被标记为边界不确定，提示下游应用可对此类预测进行差异化处理。关键在于，delta在每个节点处基于标准CART分割查找过程中已有的统计量本地计算，无需外部噪声指定。我们提出并评估了五种delta估计方法：质量平台（分割准则曲线平台宽度）、类别重叠（经验类别分布重叠）、增益比（相对于分割熵的分割质量）、节点自助法（节点级重采样下的阈值方差）和边界距离（受SVM启发的最近跨类训练样本距离）。在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上进行5折交叉验证，所有五种方法结合概率路由均显著优于标准CART（Wilcoxon符号秩检验，p < 0.001）。边界距离方法效率最佳（每单位边界不确定标记率提升0.104准确率），在72个数据集中胜出42个，且无需额外超参数。在三个Breiman合成基准数据集上的分析表明，边界距离方法在干净数据上具有自校准能力，而节点自助法和质量平台方法最能追踪理论不可约误差。在四个医学和金融数据集上的实验展示了实际价值：在乳腺X光筛查中，节点自助法通过标记10.8%的筛查病例为边界不确定，使决定准确率提升0.71%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of standard decision trees that assign uniform confidence regardless of proximity to decision boundaries, this paper introduces ternary decision trees that incorporate locally-adaptive uncertainty zones around split thresholds. Each node computes a delta value—representing the width of an uncertainty zone—using five distinct statistical methods derived from node-level statistics, without requiring external noise assumptions. Instances within the zone receive blended predictions and are flagged as uncertain, enabling downstream applications to handle them differently. Across 72 datasets, all five delta methods significantly improved decided accuracy over CART (p < 0.001), with the margin method achieving the highest efficiency and winning on 42 datasets without extra hyperparameters. On synthetic benchmarks, margin self-calibrates on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best reflect theoretical error. In real-world applications, node-bootstrap improved mammography accuracy by 0.71% by flagging 10.8% of cases as uncertain.
为解决标准决策树在决策边界附近无法区分置信度的问题，本文提出一种三元决策树，每个节点围绕最优阈值设置一个局部自适应的不确定性区域，区域内样本通过子树加权融合预测并标记为不确定。该方法通过五种不同策略（质量平台、类别重叠、增益比、节点自助法、边界距离）计算区域宽度 delta，无需额外超参数。在72个数据集的5折交叉验证中，所有方法均显著优于标准CART（p<0.001），其中边界距离法效率最高（每单位不确定性标记提升0.104准确率）。在合成数据上，边界距离法在干净数据中自校准，节点自助法和质量平台法更贴近理论不可约误差；在真实医疗与金融数据上，节点自助法使乳腺筛查准确率提升0.71%，标记10.8%样本为不确定。
Proxy-Based Approximation of Shapley and Banzhaf Interactions
Authors: Santo M. A. R. Thies, Hubert Baniecki, R. Teal Witter, Eyke Hüllermeier, Maximilian Muschalik, Fabian Fumagalli
First: 2026-05-21T17:09:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:09:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Shapley and Banzhaf interactions capture the complex dynamics inherent in modern machine learning applications. However, current estimators for these higher-order interactions trade off between speed and accuracy. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProxySHAP. ProxySHAP reconciles the high sample efficiency of tree-based proxy models with a principled path to consistency via residual correction. On a theoretical level, we derive a polynomial-time generalization of interventional TreeSHAP to compute exact interaction indices for tree ensembles, successfully bypassing exponential tree-depth dependencies in prior methods. Furthermore, we formally analyze the residual adjustment strategy, characterizing the specific conditions under which Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects proxy bias without its variance scaling exponentially with interaction size. Extensive benchmarking demonstrates that ProxySHAP sets a new state-of-the-art standard for approximation quality, including in large-scale applications with thousands of features. By achieving the lowest error in both small- and large-budget regimes, ProxySHAP significantly outperforms the prior best estimators ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, while also delivering superior performance on downstream explainability tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理模型的Shapley与Banzhaf交互近似
Shapley与Banzhaf交互捕捉了现代机器学习应用中固有的复杂动态。然而，当前这些高阶交互的估计方法在速度与精度之间存在权衡。为克服这一局限，我们提出了ProxySHAP。ProxySHAP将基于树的代理模型的高样本效率与通过残差校正实现的一致性路径相结合。理论上，我们推导出干预式TreeSHAP的多项式时间泛化形式，用于计算树集成的精确交互指标，成功绕过了先前方法中指数级的树深度依赖。此外，我们正式分析了残差调整策略，刻画了最大样本重用（MSR）在何种条件下能校正代理偏差，且其方差不会随交互规模指数增长。大量基准测试表明，ProxySHAP在近似质量上达到新状态-of-the-art标准，包括在数千特征的大规模应用中。在小预算与大预算场景下，ProxySHAP均实现最低误差，显著优于先前最佳估计器ProxySPEX与KernelSHAP-IQ，同时在下游可解释性任务中也表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient yet accurate estimation of Shapley and Banzhaf interactions in complex ML models, the paper introduces ProxySHAP, a method that combines tree-based proxy models with residual correction to achieve high sample efficiency and consistency. Theoretically, it generalizes interventional TreeSHAP to polynomial-time computation for tree ensembles, eliminating exponential depth dependencies, and formally characterizes when Maximum Sample Reuse (MSR) corrects bias without variance explosion. Empirically, ProxySHAP achieves the lowest error across both small- and large-budget settings, outperforming prior state-of-the-art methods like ProxySPEX and KernelSHAP-IQ, and delivers superior downstream explainability performance.
为解决现代机器学习中Shapley和Banzhaf交互估计的效率与精度权衡问题，本文提出ProxySHAP方法，结合树结构代理模型与残差校正机制，实现高样本效率与一致性。理论分析推导出树集成模型中多项式时间的交互计算方法，消除了先前方法的指数级树深度依赖。实验表明，ProxySHAP在小预算与大预算场景下均取得最低误差，显著优于ProxySPEX和KernelSHAP-IQ等现有方法，并在下游可解释性任务中表现更优。
The Distillation Game: Adaptive Attacks & Efficient Defenses
Authors: Youssef Allouah, Mahdi Haghifam, Sanmi Koyejo, Reza Shokri
First: 2026-05-21T17:09:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:09:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Distillation attacks create a deployment trade-off for model providers: the same outputs that make a model more useful can also make it easier to imitate. We study this trade-off through a minimax game between a utility-constrained teacher and an adaptive student. Our framework yields tractable one-sided response rules: an adaptive evaluation rule in which the student reweights high-value examples, and a teacher-side defense template that suppresses outputs most useful for distillation. From a cheap proxy for example value, we derive Product-of-Experts (PoE), a simple forward-pass-only defense that combines the teacher with a proxy student during generation. Empirically, adaptive evaluation reveals a large passive--adaptive gap: on state-of-the-art defenses, adaptive students recover substantially more capability than passive evaluation suggests on GSM8K and MATH. Under this stronger evaluation, the apparent robustness gap between expensive defenses and PoE narrows considerably, while PoE remains substantially cheaper and preserves higher-quality reasoning traces. Overall, our results suggest that strong distillation remains difficult to stop, and that progress on antidistillation should be judged against adaptive students rather than passive ones. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ysfalh/distillation-game.
中文标题/摘要
标题：蒸馏博弈：自适应攻击与高效防御
蒸馏攻击为模型提供者制造了部署权衡：使模型更具实用性的输出，也可能更容易被模仿。我们通过一个效用受限教师与自适应学生之间的极小极大博弈来研究这一权衡。我们的框架得出可处理的一侧响应规则：学生端的自适应评估规则，通过重新加权高价值样本；以及教师端的防御模板，抑制对蒸馏最有利的输出。基于一个廉价的样本价值代理，我们推导出Product-of-Experts（PoE），一种仅需前向传播的简单防御方法，在生成过程中结合教师与代理学生。实证表明，自适应评估揭示了显著的被动—自适应能力差距：在当前最先进防御上，自适应学生在GSM8K和MATH任务中恢复的能力远超被动评估所显示的水平。在更强的自适应评估下，昂贵防御与PoE之间的表观鲁棒性差距显著缩小，而PoE仍保持成本更低且保留更高质量推理轨迹。总体而言，我们的结果表明，强力蒸馏仍难以阻止，且对抗蒸馏的进展应以自适应学生而非被动学生为评判标准。代码见：https://github.com/ysfalh/distillation-game。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between model utility and distillation vulnerability, this work models the problem as a minimax game between a utility-constrained teacher and an adaptive student. The authors derive tractable response rules: the student adaptively reweights high-value examples during evaluation, while the teacher defends by suppressing outputs most useful for distillation. They propose PoE, a lightweight forward-pass-only defense that combines the teacher with a proxy student during generation. Experiments show that adaptive evaluation reveals a significant performance gap over passive evaluation on GSM8K and MATH, and that PoE, despite being much cheaper, remains competitive with expensive defenses in terms of capability retention and reasoning quality.
为应对模型在提升实用性的同时也更容易被蒸馏攻击的问题，本文将该权衡建模为教师与自适应学生之间的极小极大博弈。提出可计算的响应策略：学生在评估时自适应加权高价值样本，教师则通过抑制最易被蒸馏的输出进行防御。基于低成本的样本价值代理，提出PoE防御方法，即在生成时将教师与代理学生结合进行前向传播。实验表明，自适应评估揭示了显著的被动-自适应能力差距：在GSM8K和MATH上，自适应学生恢复的能力远超被动评估所显示的水平。在该更强评估下，昂贵防御与PoE之间的鲁棒性差距明显缩小，而PoE仍保持成本更低且保留更高质量推理轨迹。
Optimization over the intersection of manifolds
Authors: Yan Yang, Bin Gao, Ya-xiang Yuan
First: 2026-05-21T17:08:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:08:00+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Optimization over the intersection of two manifolds arises in a broad range of applications, but is hindered by the coupled geometry of the feasible region. In this paper, we prove that the regularities -- clean intersection and intrinsic transversality -- are equivalent, which yields a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. Therefore, we propose a geometric method that employs a retraction on only one manifold and updates the iterate along two orthogonal directions. Specifically, the iterates stay on one manifold, and the two directions are responsible for asymptotically approaching the other manifold and decreasing the objective function, respectively. Under intrinsic transversality, we derive the convergence rate for both the feasibility and optimality measures, and show that every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Numerical experiments on problems stemming from sparse and low-rank optimization, including fitting spherical data, approximating hyperbolic embeddings on real data, and computing compressed modes, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在流形交集上的优化
在两个流形交集上的优化广泛应用于各类问题，但受限于可行区域的耦合几何结构。本文证明了“干净交集”与“内在横截性”两种正则性条件等价，从而得到一个可计算的交集切空间投影方法。因此，我们提出一种几何方法，仅在其中一个流形上使用重映射，并沿两个正交方向更新迭代点。具体而言，迭代点保持在一个流形上，两个方向分别负责渐近逼近另一个流形和降低目标函数值。在满足内在横截性条件下，我们推导出可行性与最优性度量的收敛速率，并证明每个极限点均为一阶平稳点。数值实验在源于稀疏与低秩优化的问题上进行，包括球形数据拟合、真实数据上的双曲嵌入逼近及压缩模式计算，验证了所提方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimizing over the intersection of two manifolds, which is complicated by their coupled geometry, this paper establishes that clean intersection and intrinsic transversality are equivalent, enabling a tractable projection onto the tangent space of the intersection. The authors propose a geometric method that uses a retraction on one manifold and updates iterates along two orthogonal directions: one to approach the other manifold and one to decrease the objective. Under intrinsic transversality, the method guarantees convergence rates for feasibility and optimality, and ensures every accumulation point is first-order stationary. Experiments on sparse and low-rank problems, such as spherical data fitting and hyperbolic embedding, confirm the method's effectiveness.
为解决在两个流形交集上优化时因几何耦合带来的困难，本文证明了干净交集与内在横截性等价，从而实现对交集切空间的可计算投影。提出一种几何方法：仅在其中一个流形上进行重映射，并沿两个正交方向更新迭代点——一个方向用于渐近逼近另一流形，另一个方向用于降低目标函数。在内在横截性条件下，该方法对可行性与最优性均有收敛速率保证，且所有极限点均为一阶平稳点。在球面拟合、超球嵌入与压缩模式计算等数值实验中，验证了该方法的有效性。
ChronoMedKG: A Temporally-Grounded Biomedical Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Clinical Reasoning
Authors: Md Shamim Ahmed, Farzaneh Firoozbakht, Lukas Galke Poech, Jan Baumbach, Richard Röttger
First: 2026-05-21T17:04:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:04:28+00:00
Comments: 9 pages main text plus appendices, 8 figures. Dataset and benchmark paper. ChronoMedKG released under CC BY 4.0 and ChronoTQA/code under MIT (Zenodo: 10.5281/zenodo.19697542). Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biomedical knowledge graphs (KGs) treat disease associations as static facts, but temporal information is crucial for clinical reasoning, e.g., a symptom diagnostic of one disease at age 3 may imply a different disease at age 13. Existing KGs such as PrimeKG, Hetionet, and iKraph do not encode when a finding becomes clinically relevant over the course of a disease. This limits their usefulness for longitudinal clinical reasoning and retrieval augmentation. We introduce ChronoMedKG, a temporal biomedical knowledge graph that contains 460,497 evidence-linked triples (filtered from 13M raw extractions) covering 13,431 diseases. Each association is tied to temporal components like onset window or progression stage, which are backed by PMID-traceable evidence and a multi-signal credibility score. The graph is constructed through a disease-autonomous multi-agent pipeline in which multiple frontier LLMs independently extract knowledge from PubMed and PMC literature. Only those relations are kept that are supported by multi-model consensus, survive credibility filtering, as well as ontology alignment. ChronoMedKG scored 92.7% agreement against Orphadata and adds temporal grounding for 6,250 diseases absent from HPOA, Orphadata, and Phenopackets, including 1,657 Orphanet-coded rare diseases. We further introduce ChronoTQA, a benchmark of 3,341 questions across eight task types (six temporal plus two static controls), with a 12-question supplementary probe. Frontier LLMs lose roughly 30 points moving from static to temporal questions; ChronoMedKG retrieval rescues 47-65% of their long-tail failures, against 17-29% for HPOA-RAG. As such, ChronoMedKG provides a crucial temporal axis for retrieval-augmented clinical systems that was previously absent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChronoMedKG：一个具有时间锚定的生物医学知识图谱及临床推理基准
生物医学知识图谱（KGs）将疾病关联视为静态事实，但临床推理中时间信息至关重要，例如，3岁时的某症状可能在13岁时指向不同疾病。现有KG如PrimeKG、Hetionet和iKraph未编码疾病过程中某发现何时变得临床相关，这限制了其在纵向临床推理和检索增强中的实用性。 我们提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含460,497个证据关联三元组（从1300万原始提取中筛选）覆盖13,431种疾病的时序生物医学知识图谱。每个关联均绑定起始窗口或进展阶段等时间成分，且有PMID可追溯证据及多信号可信度评分支持。该图谱通过疾病自主多智能体管道构建，多个前沿大语言模型独立从PubMed和PMC文献中提取知识。仅保留经多模型共识支持、通过可信度过滤及本体对齐的关系。 ChronoMedKG与Orphadata一致性达92.7%，为HPOA、Orphadata和Phenopackets中缺失的6,250种疾病提供时间锚定，包括1,657种Orphanet编码的罕见病。我们进一步引入ChronoTQA，一个包含3,341个问题、八类任务（六类时序加两类静态对照）及12题补充探针的基准测试。前沿大语言模型从静态到时序问题性能下降约30分；ChronoMedKG检索可挽救其长尾失败的47-65%，而HPOA-RAG仅挽救17-29%。因此，ChronoMedKG为检索增强型临床系统提供了此前缺失的关键时间轴。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for temporal grounding in clinical reasoning, this paper introduces ChronoMedKG, a biomedical knowledge graph encoding disease associations with onset windows and progression stages, derived from 13M PubMed/PMC extractions and filtered to 460,497 evidence-backed triples. Built via a multi-agent LLM pipeline that requires multi-model consensus and credibility scoring, it covers 13,431 diseases, including 6,250 previously unrepresented in HPOA or Orphadata. ChronoMedKG achieves 92.7% agreement with Orphadata and significantly outperforms HPOA-RAG in temporal retrieval, rescuing 47–65% of LLM failures on ChronoTQA’s 3,341 temporal questions, compared to 17–29% for HPOA-RAG.
为支持临床推理中的时间维度，本文提出ChronoMedKG，一个包含13,431种疾病、46万条带时间标注（如发病窗口或进展阶段）证据关联三元组的生物医学知识图谱。该图谱通过多智能体LLM管道构建，要求多模型共识、可信度筛选及本体对齐，覆盖6,250种此前未在HPOA或Orphadata中出现的疾病，包括1,657种罕见病。在与Orphadata对比中达成92.7%一致性，并在时序问答任务中显著优于HPOA-RAG，将前沿LLM在时序问题上的失败率恢复47–65%，而HPOA-RAG仅恢复17–29%。
HarnessAPI: A Skill-First Framework for Unified Streaming APIs and MCP Tools
Authors: Edwin Jose
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Every Python function deployed as an LLM tool must today exist in two forms: an HTTP endpoint for human-facing clients and CI pipelines, and an MCP tool registration for agent runtimes such as Claude and Cursor. These representations share business logic yet diverge in all the surrounding machinery (routing, validation, serialisation, streaming, and schema maintenance), and they drift apart as the underlying code evolves. We present HarnessAPI, a Python framework that eliminates this duplication by treating a typed skill folder as the single source of truth. From one handler.py plus Pydantic schemas, the framework automatically derives a streaming HTTP endpoint with Server-Sent Events, an interactive OpenAPI/Swagger UI, and a zero-configuration MCP tool, all served from a single process. Dual-mode content negotiation lets the same handler serve SSE-streaming and JSON-returning clients with no handler changes. A dynamic code-generation mechanism ensures Pydantic type annotations propagate correctly to FastMCP's inspection layer, resolving a technical limitation that prevents naive closure-based registration. Measured across six representative skills using cloc, HarnessAPI reduces framework-facing boilerplate by 74% compared with a manually maintained dual-stack implementation (FastAPI server + FastMCP server). HarnessAPI subclasses FastAPI, inheriting its full middleware, dependency-injection, and deployment ecosystem. It is available at https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi and on PyPI (pip install harnessapi)
中文标题/摘要
标题：HarnessAPI：一种以技能为核心的统一流式API与MCP工具框架
当前，每个作为LLM工具部署的Python函数都必须以两种形式存在：面向人类客户端和CI流水线的HTTP端点，以及用于代理运行时（如Claude和Cursor）的MCP工具注册。这两种表示共享业务逻辑，但在周边机制（路由、验证、序列化、流式传输和模式维护）上存在差异，且随着底层代码演进而逐渐脱节。我们提出HarnessAPI，一种Python框架，通过将类型化的技能文件夹视为单一真实来源，消除这种重复。从一个handler.py文件和Pydantic模式，该框架可自动推导出支持Server-Sent Events的流式HTTP端点、交互式OpenAPI/Swagger UI，以及零配置MCP工具，所有服务均来自同一进程。双模式内容协商机制允许同一处理器无需修改即可同时服务SSE流式和JSON返回客户端。动态代码生成机制确保Pydantic类型注解正确传递至FastMCP的检查层，解决了阻止简单闭包注册的技术限制。在六个代表性技能上通过cloc测量，与手动维护的双栈实现（FastAPI服务器+FastMCP服务器）相比，HarnessAPI减少了74%的框架相关样板代码。HarnessAPI继承自FastAPI，拥有其完整的中间件、依赖注入和部署生态系统。可在https://github.com/edwinjosechittilappilly/harnessapi获取，或通过PyPI安装（pip install harnessapi）

Motivated by the redundancy and drift between HTTP endpoints and MCP tool registrations for LLM tools, HarnessAPI introduces a skill-first framework that treats a typed skill folder as the single source of truth.
