Experimental results across six skills show a 74% reduction in boilerplate compared to manually maintained dual-stack implementations using FastAPI and FastMCP.
为解决LLM工具在HTTP端点与MCP工具注册间存在的冗余与发散问题，HarnessAPI提出以技能文件夹为单一真实来源的框架。它从单个handler.py和Pydantic模式自动生成支持SSE流式传输的HTTP端点、OpenAPI界面及零配置MCP工具，统一由一个进程提供服务。通过双模式内容协商，同一处理器可同时支持流式与JSON客户端，无需修改代码；动态代码生成机制确保Pydantic注解正确传递至FastMCP的检查层。在六个代表性技能上的实验表明，与手动维护的双栈方案（FastAPI + FastMCP）相比，框架层面的样板代码减少了74%。
Beyond Acoustic Emotion Recognition: Multimodal Pathos Analysis in Political Speech Using LLM-Based and Acoustic Emotion Models
Authors: Juergen Dietrich
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:37+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, as operationalised by the TRUST multi-agent large language model (LLM) pipeline. Using a Bundestag plenary speech by Felix Banaszak (51 segments, 245 s) as a case study, we compare three analysis modalities: (1) emotion2vec_plus_large, an acoustic speech emotion recognition (SER) model whose continuous Arousal and Valence values are derived via post-hoc Russell Circumplex projection; (2) Gemini 2.5 Flash, an LLM analysing the full speech audio together with its transcript in an open-ended, context-aware fashion; and (3) TRUST-Pathos scores from a three-advocate LLM supervisor ensemble. Spearman rank correlations reveal that Gemini Valence correlates strongly with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), whereas emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). We further demonstrate, via a systematic quality evaluation of the Berlin Database of Emotional Speech (EMO-DB) using Gemini in an open-ended annotation paradigm, that standard SER benchmark corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category incompatibility. Our results suggest that LLM-based multimodal analysis captures semantically defined political emotion substantially better than acoustic models alone, while acoustic features remain informative for low-level Arousal estimation. Future work will extend this approach to video-based analysis incorporating facial expression and gaze.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越声学情绪识别：基于LLM与声学情绪模型的政治演讲多模态情感分析
我们探讨声学情绪识别模型是否可作为政治演讲分析中‘Pathos’维度的代理，该维度由TRUST多智能体大语言模型（LLM）管道定义。以联邦议院议员Felix Banaszak的全体会议演讲（51个片段，245秒）为案例，我们比较三种分析模式：(1) emotion2vec_plus_large，一种声学语音情绪识别（SER）模型，其连续的唤醒度与效价值通过事后Russell环形空间投影获得；(2) Gemini 2.5 Flash，一种在开放且上下文感知模式下同时分析完整语音音频及其转录文本的LLM；(3) 由三位LLM监督者组成的TRUST-Pathos评分集合。Spearman秩相关分析显示，Gemini效价与TRUST-Pathos高度相关（rho = +0.664, p < 0.001），而emotion2vec效价无显著相关（rho = +0.097, p = 0.499）。我们进一步通过Gemini在开放标注范式下对柏林情绪语音数据库（EMO-DB）的系统质量评估表明，标准SER基准语料库存在表演性语音、文化偏见及类别不兼容问题。结果表明，基于LLM的多模态分析在捕捉语义定义的政治情绪方面显著优于声学模型，而声学特征仍对低级唤醒度估计具有信息价值。未来工作将扩展此方法至视频分析，整合面部表情与注视方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better capture the Pathos dimension in political speech analysis, this study evaluates whether acoustic emotion recognition models can serve as proxies for LLM-derived emotional assessments. Using a Bundestag speech as a case study, it compares three modalities: an acoustic SER model (emotion2vec_plus_large) projecting arousal and valence via Russell Circumplex, an LLM (Gemini 2.5 Flash) analyzing audio and transcript together, and a TRUST-LLM ensemble for Pathos scoring. Results show Gemini Valence strongly correlates with TRUST-Pathos (rho = +0.664, p < 0.001), while emotion2vec Valence does not (rho = +0.097, p = 0.499). Further evaluation of EMO-DB reveals acoustic corpora suffer from acted speech, cultural bias, and category mismatch. The study concludes that LLM-based multimodal analysis better captures semantic political emotion than acoustic models alone, though acoustic features remain useful for low-level arousal estimation.
为评估政治演讲中的Pathos维度，本研究对比了三种分析方法：声学情绪识别模型（emotion2vec_plus_large）、多模态大语言模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash）和基于TRUST的大语言模型集成。结果显示，Gemini的情绪价值得分与TRUST-Pathos高度相关（rho = +0.664，p < 0.001），而声学模型的情绪价值相关性极弱（rho = +0.097，p = 0.499）。对EMO-DB语料库的进一步评估表明，标准声学情绪数据集存在表演性语音、文化偏见和类别不兼容问题。研究结论认为，基于大语言模型的多模态分析更准确捕捉语义化的政治情绪，而声学特征仍对低层唤醒度估计具有参考价值。
Post-Training is About States, Not Tokens: A State Distribution View of SFT, RL, and On-Policy Distillation
Authors: Dong Nie
First: 2026-05-21T17:03:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:03:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model post-training methods such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning (RL), and distillation are often analyzed through their loss functions: maximum likelihood, policy gradients, forward KL, reverse KL, or related objective-level variants. We study a complementary factor: the state distribution on which supervision is applied. For an autoregressive policy, a state is a prompt plus generated prefix. SFT trains on fixed dataset states, while RL and on-policy distillation (OPD) train on states induced by the current learner. We formalize post-training as state-distribution shaping and run a controlled smallscale study using Qwen3-0.6B-Base on GSM8K, with TruthfulQA and MMLU as retention evaluations. Our results show three phenomena. First, a mild SFT run improves GSM8K with little forgetting, while a stress SFT run causes substantial retention loss. Second, OPD from a degraded SFT teacher surpasses that teacher on GSM8K, TruthfulQA, and MMLU, despite using the teacher as its only supervision source. Third, a lightweight on-policy RL run improves GSM8K while preserving retention. These results support a state-centric view of post-training: the source and locality of training states can be as important as the form of the supervision signal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后训练关注状态而非标记：SFT、RL与在线蒸馏的状态分布视角
监督微调（SFT）、强化学习（RL）和蒸馏等大语言模型后训练方法常通过其损失函数（如最大似然、策略梯度、前向KL、反向KL或相关目标变体）进行分析。我们研究一个互补因素：监督所应用的状态分布。对于自回归策略，状态由提示与生成前缀组成。SFT在固定数据集状态上训练，而RL和在线蒸馏（OPD）则在当前学习者诱导的状态上训练。我们将后训练形式化为状态分布塑造，并在GSM8K数据集上使用Qwen3-0.6B-Base进行受控小规模研究，以TruthfulQA和MMLU作为保留能力评估。结果呈现三种现象：第一，轻度SFT训练可提升GSM8K且遗忘少，而高强度SFT训练则导致显著保留能力损失；第二，从退化SFT教师模型衍生的OPD在GSM8K、TruthfulQA和MMLU上表现超越该教师，尽管仅使用教师作为监督源；第三，轻量级在线RL训练可提升GSM8K同时保留能力。这些结果支持后训练的状态中心视角：训练状态的来源与局部性可能与监督信号的形式同等重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that post-training methods for large language models are often evaluated through loss functions alone, this study investigates how the distribution of training states—comprising prompts and generated prefixes—affects performance. It compares supervised fine-tuning (SFT), reinforcement learning (RL), and on-policy distillation (OPD) by analyzing their state distributions: SFT uses fixed states from a dataset, while RL and OPD use states dynamically generated by the learner. Experiments on Qwen3-0.6B-Base show that mild SFT improves GSM8K with minimal retention loss, while aggressive SFT causes significant forgetting. Surprisingly, OPD from a degraded SFT teacher outperforms the teacher on multiple benchmarks, and a lightweight RL run enhances GSM8K without harming retention. These findings suggest that the origin and locality of training states matter as much as the supervision signal itself.
该研究旨在探讨大语言模型后训练方法中，训练状态分布（由提示与生成前缀组成）对性能的影响，而非仅关注损失函数。作者将后训练形式化为状态分布塑造，并在Qwen3-0.6B-Base上进行控制实验，以GSM8K为主任务，TruthfulQA和MMLU评估保留能力。结果表明：轻度SFT可提升GSM8K且遗忘少，而高强度SFT导致显著遗忘；令人意外的是，从退化SFT教师处进行的在线蒸馏反而在GSM8K、TruthfulQA和MMLU上表现更优；轻量级在线强化学习亦能提升GSM8K且保留能力不受损。这些结果支持状态中心视角：训练状态的来源与局部性与监督信号形式同等重要。
LLM Readiness Harness: Evaluation, Observability, and CI Gates for LLM/RAG Applications
Authors: Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano
First: 2026-03-28T18:03:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T17:00:55+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures, 15 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a readiness harness for LLM and RAG applications that turns evaluation into a deployment decision workflow. The system combines automated benchmarks, OpenTelemetry observability, and CI quality gates under a minimal API contract, then aggregates workflow success, policy compliance, groundedness, retrieval hit rate, cost, and p95 latency into scenario-weighted readiness scores with Pareto frontiers. We evaluate the harness on ticket-routing workflows and BEIR grounding tasks (SciFact and FiQA) with full Azure matrix coverage (162/162 valid cells across datasets, scenarios, retrieval depths, seeds, and models). Results show that readiness is not a single metric: on FiQA under sla-first at k=5, gpt-4.1-mini leads in readiness and faithfulness, while gpt-5.2 pays a substantial latency cost; on SciFact, models are closer in quality but still separable operationally. Ticket-routing regression gates consistently reject unsafe prompt variants, demonstrating that the harness can block risky releases instead of merely reporting offline scores. The result is a reproducible, operationally grounded framework for deciding whether an LLM or RAG system is ready to ship.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型就绪性工具：面向LLM/RAG应用的评估、可观测性与CI门禁
我们提出了一种面向LLM和RAG应用的就绪性工具，将评估转化为部署决策流程。该系统在最小API契约下整合自动化基准测试、OpenTelemetry可观测性与CI质量门禁，并将工作流成功率、策略合规性、依据性、检索命中率、成本及p95延迟聚合为加权就绪性得分，辅以帕累托前沿。我们在工单路由流程与BEIR依据性任务（SciFact和FiQA）上评估该工具，覆盖Azure矩阵全部162个有效单元（涵盖数据集、场景、检索深度、种子与模型）。结果表明，就绪性并非单一指标：在FiQA的sla-first场景下，k=5时gpt-4.1-mini在就绪性与忠实性上领先，而gpt-5.2则付出显著延迟代价；在SciFact中，模型质量接近但操作上仍可区分。工单路由回归门禁持续拒绝不安全提示变体，证明该工具可阻止高风险发布，而非仅报告离线分数。最终成果是一个可复现、具备操作基础的框架，用于判断LLM或RAG系统是否具备发布条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, automated deployment decisions for LLM and RAG systems, the authors introduce a readiness harness that integrates automated benchmarks, OpenTelemetry observability, and CI gates under a minimal API contract. The system computes scenario-weighted readiness scores by aggregating metrics like policy compliance, groundedness, retrieval hit rate, cost, and latency, using Pareto frontiers to balance trade-offs. Evaluated on ticket-routing and BEIR grounding tasks (SciFact and FiQA) across full Azure matrix coverage, the harness revealed that readiness is multi-dimensional: on FiQA under SLA-first at k=5, gpt-4.1-mini outperformed gpt-5.2 in readiness and faithfulness despite higher latency; on SciFact, models were more comparable but still operationally distinguishable. The system also consistently blocked unsafe prompt variants in ticket-routing regression gates, proving its ability to prevent risky releases.
为解决大语言模型和RAG系统部署决策缺乏自动化与可靠性的问题，作者提出了一种“就绪性工具包”，整合自动化基准测试、OpenTelemetry可观测性与CI质量门禁，通过最小API契约实现统一接口。系统通过聚合政策合规性、事实依据度、检索命中率、成本与P95延迟等指标，结合帕累托前沿计算场景加权就绪分数。在票务路由工作流与BEIR接地任务（SciFact和FiQA）上，全Azure矩阵覆盖（162/162有效单元）下，结果显示就绪性非单一指标：在FiQA SLA优先k=5场景中，gpt-4.1-mini在就绪性与忠实度上领先，而gpt-5.2虽性能相近但延迟代价显著；在SciFact中模型质量接近但操作上仍可区分。回归门禁持续拦截危险提示变体，证明该工具包可阻止高风险发布而非仅报告离线分数。
Representation over Routing: Overcoming Surrogate Hacking in Multi-Timescale PPO
Authors: Jing Sun
First: 2026-04-15T06:03:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:58:14+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Temporal credit assignment in reinforcement learning has long been a central challenge. Inspired by the multi-timescale encoding of the dopamine system in neurobiology, recent research has sought to introduce multiple discount factors into Actor-Critic architectures, such as Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), to balance short-term responses with long-term planning. However, this paper reveals that blindly fusing multi-timescale signals in complex delayed-reward tasks can lead to severe algorithmic pathologies. We systematically demonstrate that exposing a temporal attention routing mechanism to policy gradients results in surrogate objective hacking, while adopting gradient-free uncertainty weighting triggers irreversible myopic degeneration, a phenomenon we term the Paradox of Temporal Uncertainty. To address these issues, we propose a Target Decoupling architecture: on the Critic side, we retain multi-timescale predictions to enforce auxiliary representation learning, while on the Actor side, we strictly isolate short-term signals and update the policy based solely on long-term advantages. Rigorous empirical evaluations across multiple independent random seeds in the LunarLander-v2 environment demonstrate that our proposed architecture achieves statistically significant performance improvements. Without relying on hyperparameter hacking, it consistently surpasses the ''Environment Solved'' threshold with minimal variance, completely eliminates policy collapse, and escapes the hovering local optima that trap single-timescale baselines. The source code to reproduce our experiments is publicly available at https://github.com/ben-dlwlrma/Representation-Over-Routing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：表示优于路由：克服多时间尺度PPO中的代理攻击
强化学习中的时间信用分配长期以来一直是核心挑战。受神经生物学中多时间尺度多巴胺系统的启发，近期研究试图在Actor-Critic架构（如近端策略优化PPO）中引入多个折扣因子，以平衡短期响应与长期规划。然而，本文揭示，在复杂的延迟奖励任务中盲目融合多时间尺度信号会导致严重的算法病态。我们系统性地证明，将时间注意力路由机制暴露于策略梯度会导致代理目标攻击，而采用无梯度的不确定性加权则会引发不可逆的近视退化，我们称之为“时间不确定性悖论”。为解决这些问题，我们提出目标解耦架构：在Critic端保留多时间尺度预测以强化辅助表示学习；在Actor端严格隔离短期信号，仅基于长期优势更新策略。在LunarLander-v2环境中，通过多个独立随机种子的严格实证评估表明，所提架构实现统计显著的性能提升。无需依赖超参数调优，它始终以最小方差超越“环境已解决”阈值，完全消除策略崩溃，并摆脱单时间尺度基线陷入的悬停局部最优。实验代码公开于https://github.com/ben-dlwlrma/Representation-Over-Routing。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to resolve temporal credit assignment in reinforcement learning, this paper investigates multi-timescale PPO architectures inspired by dopamine’s biological encoding. It identifies critical failures when fusing multi-timescale signals, including surrogate objective hacking via temporal attention routing and myopic degeneration from gradient-free uncertainty weighting. To remedy these, the authors propose Target Decoupling: the Critic retains multi-timescale predictions for representation learning, while the Actor isolates short-term signals and updates solely on long-term advantages. Experiments across multiple random seeds in LunarLander-v2 show statistically significant performance gains, consistent surpassing the 'Environment Solved' threshold with low variance, eliminating policy collapse, and avoiding local optima that trap single-timescale baselines.
为解决强化学习中的时序信用分配问题，本文研究受多尺度多巴胺系统启发的多时间尺度PPO架构，发现当路由机制暴露于策略梯度或采用无梯度不确定性加权时，会引发代理目标篡改和近视退化等严重问题。为此，作者提出目标解耦架构：评论家保留多时间尺度信号以促进表征学习，而行动者仅依据长期优势更新策略。在LunarLander-v2环境中多随机种子实验表明，该架构显著提升性能，稳定超越‘环境已解决’阈值，消除策略崩溃，避免单时间尺度基线陷入的局部最优。
Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning
Authors: Adrien Weihs, Hayden Schaeffer
First: 2026-05-21T16:57:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:57:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多神经算子在多任务学习中实现近最优速率
我们研究了在共享多任务设置下学习算子集合的逼近与统计复杂性，重点聚焦于多神经算子（MNO）架构。对于广泛类别的利普希茨多重算子映射，我们推导出逼近与统计泛化能力的近最优上界。在下界方面，我们建立了参数复杂性的诅咒并证明了相应的最小最大速率。这些结果共同表明，跨任务共享表示不会增加整体成本：多任务算子学习遵循与单算子学习相同的缩放规律。我们还比较了MNO与基于拼接任务输入的DeepONet多任务扩展版本，从最坏情况逼近复杂度视角看，两种架构满足几乎相同的渐近速率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the theoretical efficiency of multi-task learning with neural operators, this work analyzes the approximation and statistical complexity of learning multiple operators sharing representations. It introduces the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture and derives near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and generalization for broad classes of Lipschitz operator maps. Complementing these with minimax lower bounds, the study demonstrates that shared representations across tasks do not incur additional computational cost, matching the scaling laws of single-task learning. Experimental comparisons with a concatenated-input DeepONet variant reveal that both architectures achieve asymptotically identical worst-case approximation rates.
为理解多任务算子学习的理论效率，本文研究了共享表示下Multiple Neural Operators（MNO）的逼近与统计复杂性。针对广泛类的Lipschitz多算子映射，推导出逼近与泛化误差的近最优上界，并证明了参数复杂性带来的下界，揭示了参数诅咒。结果表明，跨任务共享表示不会增加整体成本，多任务学习的标度规律与单任务学习一致。与基于拼接输入的多任务DeepONet扩展相比，二者在最坏情况下的逼近复杂性渐近率几乎相同。
The Value of Covariance Matching in Gaussian DDPMs and the Lanczos Sampler
Authors: Md Sahil Akhtar, Aymane El Gadarri, Vivek F. Farias, Adam D. Jozefiak
First: 2026-05-21T16:57:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:57:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central error measure in Gaussian DDPMs is the path-space KL divergence between the exact reverse chain and the learned Gaussian reverse process. This quantity is especially relevant for procedures such as classifier guidance, which perturb the entire reverse trajectory rather than only the terminal sample. Prior analyses show that standard isotropic reverse covariances suffer an unavoidable $Ω(1/T)$ path-KL error as the number of denoising steps $T$ grows. We show that matching the full posterior covariance breaks this barrier, yielding an order-wise improvement that reduces the path KL to $O(1/T^2)$. To make full covariance matching practical, we introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method for sampling from the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products, which are available through Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense covariance storage and auxiliary covariance models. We prove that LGS approximation error decays exponentially in the number of Lanczos steps, where each Lanczos step requires a single Jacobian-vector product. Empirically, using only just three such steps improves sample quality over strong diagonal-covariance baselines, including OCM-DDPM, across standard image benchmarks. This identifies full covariance matching as both theoretically valuable and practically accessible for fast DDPM sampling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协方差匹配在高斯DDPM及Lanczos采样器中的价值
高斯DDPM中的核心误差度量是精确逆链与学习到的高斯逆过程之间的路径空间KL散度。该量对分类器引导等扰动整个逆轨迹而非仅终端样本的程序尤其重要。先前分析表明，标准各向同性逆协方差在去噪步数T增大时不可避免地产生Ω(1/T)的路径KL误差。我们证明，匹配完整后验协方差可突破此限制，实现阶数级改进，将路径KL降至O(1/T²)。为使完整协方差匹配实用，我们引入Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），这是一种无需训练、无需矩阵存储的方法，仅通过后验均值的雅可比-向量积即可计算最优逆协方差下的采样，避免密集协方差存储及辅助协方差模型。我们证明LGS近似误差随Lanczos步数呈指数衰减，每步仅需一次雅可比-向量积。实验证明，仅用三步Lanczos即可在标准图像基准上超越包括OCM-DDPM在内的强对角协方差基线，提升样本质量。这表明完整协方差匹配在理论与实践中均具有价值且易于快速DDPM采样实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve reverse sampling in Gaussian DDPMs, especially for trajectory-based methods like classifier guidance, this work demonstrates that matching the full posterior covariance rather than using isotropic covariances reduces path-space KL divergence from Ω(1/T) to O(1/T²). To enable practical implementation, the authors introduce the Lanczos Gaussian sampler (LGS), a training-free, matrix-free method that approximates the optimal reverse covariance using only covariance-vector products derived from Jacobian-vector products of the posterior mean. LGS avoids dense storage and auxiliary models, and its error decays exponentially with the number of Lanczos steps. Empirical results show that just three Lanczos steps significantly outperform diagonal-covariance baselines like OCM-DDPM on standard image benchmarks, confirming both the theoretical benefit and practical feasibility of full covariance matching for efficient DDPM sampling.
为提升高斯DDPM在如分类器引导等扰动场景下的采样效率，本文研究反向过程中的协方差匹配。研究表明，匹配完整后验协方差可将路径空间KL散度从Ω(1/T)降低至O(1/T²)，突破先前理论瓶颈。为实现实用化，作者提出Lanczos高斯采样器（LGS），一种无需训练、无需存储稠密协方差矩阵的方法，仅通过后验均值的雅可比-向量积即可计算协方差-向量积。实验表明，仅用三步Lanczos迭代即可显著优于如OCM-DDPM等对角协方差基线，在标准图像数据集上提升采样质量，验证了完整协方差匹配的理论价值与工程可行性。
Spectral Dynamics in Deep Networks: Feature Learning, Outlier Escape, and Learning Rate Transfer
Authors: Clarissa Lauditi, Cengiz Pehlevan, Blake Bordelon
First: 2026-05-08T15:28:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:56:03+00:00
Comments: Updating related works + discussion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the evolution of hidden-weight spectra in wide neural networks trained by (stochastic) gradient descent. We develop a two-level dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) that jointly tracks bulk and outlier spectral dynamics for spiked ensembles whose spike directions remain statistically dependent on the random bulk. We apply this framework to two settings: (1) infinite-width nonlinear networks in mean-field/$μ$P scaling and (2) deep linear networks in the proportional high-dimensional limit, where width, input dimension, and sample size diverge with fixed ratios. Our theory predicts how outliers evolve with training time, width, output scale, and initialization variance. In deep linear networks, $μ$P yields width-consistent outlier dynamics and hyperparameter transfer, including width-stable growth of the leading NTK mode toward the edge of stability (EoS). In contrast, NTK parameterization exhibits strongly width-dependent outlier dynamics, despite converging to a stable large-width limit. We show that this bulk+outlier picture is descriptive of simple tasks with small output channels, but that tasks involving large numbers of outputs (ImageNet classification or GPT language modeling) are better described by a restructuring of the spectral bulk. We develop a toy model with extensive output channels that recapitulates this phenomenon and show that edge of the spectrum still converges for sufficiently wide networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度网络中的谱动力学：特征学习、异常值逃逸与学习率迁移
我们研究了在（随机）梯度下降训练下宽神经网络中隐藏权重谱的演化。我们开发了一种两级动力学平均场理论（DMFT），联合追踪尖峰集合中尖峰方向与随机主体部分统计相关时的总体与异常值谱动力学。我们将该框架应用于两种情形：（1）在平均场/μP缩放下的无限宽度非线性网络；（2）在比例高维极限下的深度线性网络，其中宽度、输入维度和样本量以固定比例发散。我们的理论预测了异常值如何随训练时间、宽度、输出尺度和初始化方差演化。在深度线性网络中，μP产生与宽度一致的异常值动力学和超参数迁移，包括主导NTK模式向稳定性边缘（EoS）的宽度稳定增长。相比之下，NTK参数化虽收敛至大宽度稳定极限，但表现出强烈的宽度依赖性异常值动力学。我们表明，该总体+异常值图景适用于输出通道数小的简单任务，但涉及大量输出（如ImageNet分类或GPT语言建模）的任务更适合用谱总体重构描述。我们构建了一个具有大量输出通道的玩具模型，再现了这一现象，并证明在足够宽的网络中，谱边缘仍会收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding how spectral properties of hidden weights evolve during training in deep neural networks, the authors develop a two-level dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) to jointly track bulk and outlier spectral dynamics in spiked ensembles. Applied to infinite-width nonlinear networks under mean-field/$μ$P scaling and deep linear networks in proportional high-dimensional limits, the theory predicts outlier behavior as a function of training time, width, output scale, and initialization variance. Results show that $μ$P scaling yields width-consistent outlier dynamics and stable hyperparameter transfer, including controlled growth of the leading NTK mode toward the edge of stability, while NTK parameterization exhibits strong width dependence despite converging to a stable limit. The bulk+outlier model works well for small-output tasks but fails for large-output tasks like ImageNet or GPT, where spectral restructuring dominates; a toy model with many outputs confirms that spectral edges still converge for sufficiently wide networks.
为理解神经网络训练过程中隐藏权重谱的演化，作者构建了两级动力学平均场理论，联合追踪宽神经网络中本征谱与异常值谱的动态。该理论应用于无限宽非线性网络（μP缩放）和深度线性网络（比例高维极限），预测异常值随训练时间、宽度、输出尺度和初始化方差的变化。结果表明，在μP缩放下，深度线性网络表现出宽度一致的异常值动态和超参数迁移，主导NTK模式稳定增长至稳定性边缘；而NTK参数化虽收敛于大宽度稳定极限，但异常值动态强烈依赖宽度。该本征+异常值模型适用于小输出通道任务，但对ImageNet或GPT等大输出任务需重构本征谱；一个具有大量输出通道的玩具模型验证了即使在大宽度下，谱边缘仍可收敛。
N3P: Accelerated Automated Parking via a Learning-Based Naturalistic Three-Stage Scheme
Authors: Yifan Xue, Toktam Mohammadnejad, Faizan M Tariq, Sangjae Bae, David Isele, Yosuke Sakamoto, Nadia Figueroa, Jovin D'sa
First: 2026-05-21T16:56:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:56:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE Intelligent Transportation Systems Conference (ITSC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous parking requires efficient path planning that ensures kinematic feasibility and collision avoidance in constrained environments. Hybrid A* is widely used but computationally expensive, while reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack reliability and often struggle with long-horizon geometric constraints, leading to suboptimal trajectories. We present N3P, a fast learning-based three-stage framework for automated parking. By introducing an intermediate preparatory pose and using a learning module to predict it, N3P decomposes the maneuver into simpler subproblems, thereby reducing computational complexity and accelerating path generation. We validate the framework by integrating it with Hybrid A* algorithms. Experiments in perpendicular and parallel parking scenarios show that N3P-enhanced Hybrid A* speeds up planning by more than 80%. It also outperforms RL baselines in success rate and trajectory quality, producing shorter trajectories with fewer gear changes, while achieving comparable or lower planning time in most cases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：N3P：基于学习的自然三阶段方案加速自动泊车
自动驾驶泊车需要高效路径规划，以确保在受限环境中运动可行性与避障。混合A*算法虽常用但计算成本高，而强化学习方法可靠性不足，常难以处理长时域几何约束，导致轨迹次优。本文提出N3P，一种快速学习型三阶段框架用于自动泊车。通过引入中间预备姿态并利用学习模块预测该姿态，N3P将操作分解为更简单的子问题，从而降低计算复杂度并加速路径生成。我们通过与混合A*算法集成验证该框架。在垂直和平行泊车场景实验表明，N3P增强的混合A*规划速度提升超80%。其成功率与轨迹质量优于强化学习基线，生成更短轨迹、换挡次数更少，且在多数情况下规划时间相当或更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of Hybrid A* and the unreliability of reinforcement learning in geometrically constrained parking tasks, N3P introduces a learning-based three-stage framework that decomposes parking into simpler subproblems via an intermediate preparatory pose predicted by a neural module. Integrated with Hybrid A* in experiments across perpendicular and parallel parking scenarios, N3P accelerates path planning by over 80% and outperforms RL baselines in success rate and trajectory quality, generating shorter paths with fewer gear shifts while maintaining or reducing planning time.
为解决混合A*算法计算效率低和强化学习在几何约束环境下可靠性差的问题，N3P提出一种基于学习的三阶段框架，通过神经模块预测中间预备姿态，将停车任务分解为更简单的子问题。在垂直与平行停车场景实验中，与混合A*集成后，N3P使路径规划提速超80%，相比强化学习基线方法成功率更高、轨迹更优，路径更短、换挡次数更少，且多数情况下规划时间相当或更低。
Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems via Decentralized Memory
Authors: Guangya Hao, Yunbo Long, Zhuokai Zhao
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-evolving multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising route to LLM agents that continually improve from experience, with persistent memory at their foundation. However, existing designs almost exclusively adopt a centralized repository shared across agents, incurring communication and coordination overhead, raising privacy concerns, and collapsing agent diversity. We propose DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework in which each agent maintains its own dual-pool memory -- an exploitation pool of consolidated past trajectories and an exploration pool of LLM-generated candidates for unseen contexts. The two pools are reweighted online based on stage-wise feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge. Theoretically, we prove that this design guarantees global reachability of the solution space and achieves $O(\log T)$ cumulative regret, matching the stochastic bandit lower bound up to constants. In practice, across three MAS frameworks (AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet), three Qwen3 backbones (4B/8B/14B), two Gemma4 backbones (E2B/E4B) and five benchmarks spanning math, code, QA, and embodied tasks, DecentMem improves average accuracy by up to 23.8% over the strongest centralized memory baseline and by up to 52.5% over the no-memory baseline, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过去中心化记忆实现自演化的多智能体系统
自演化的多智能体系统（MAS）已成为一种有前景的路径，使大语言模型（LLM）代理能够从经验中持续改进，其基础是持久记忆。然而，现有设计几乎都采用跨代理共享的集中式存储库，导致通信与协调开销、隐私担忧，并削弱代理多样性。我们提出DecentMem，一种去中心化记忆框架，每个代理维护自己的双池记忆——一个包含整合历史轨迹的利用池，以及一个包含LLM生成的、用于未见情境候选方案的探索池。两个池根据LLM作为裁判提供的阶段性反馈在线重新加权。理论上，我们证明该设计保证解空间的全局可达性，并实现$O(\log T)$累积遗憾，达到随机多臂老虎机下界（常数因子内）。实践中，在三个MAS框架（AutoGen、DyLAN、AgentNet）、三个Qwen3主干（4B/8B/14B）、两个Gemma4主干（E2B/E4B）及涵盖数学、代码、问答和具身任务的五个基准测试中，DecentMem相较最强集中式记忆基线平均准确率提升最高达23.8%，相较无记忆基线提升最高达52.5%，同时降低token使用量最高达49%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, privacy-preserving, and diverse self-evolving multi-agent systems, the paper introduces DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework where each agent maintains two memory pools—exploitation and exploration—and reweights them dynamically using LLM feedback. Theoretical analysis confirms that this design ensures global solution space coverage and achieves near-optimal regret bounds. Experimentally, DecentMem outperforms centralized memory baselines by up to 23.8% in accuracy across diverse agent frameworks and LLM backbones, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.
为实现可扩展、隐私保护且保持多样性的自进化多智能体系统，作者提出DecentMem框架，每个智能体独立维护两个记忆池——一个用于整合过往经验，一个用于生成新上下文候选方案，并由LLM作为裁判在线调整权重。理论上，该设计保证解空间全局可达性，累积遗憾为O(log T)，达到随机多臂老虎机下界。实验表明，在多个智能体框架和LLM模型上，DecentMem相较集中式记忆基线准确率提升最高23.8%，相较无记忆基线提升最高52.5%，同时节省49%的token用量。
Can AI Make Conflicts Worse? An Alignment Failure in LLM Deployment Across Conflict Contexts
Authors: Andrii Kryshtal
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:39+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 8 pages, 2 figures. Code and evaluation framework: https://github.com/akryshtal/conflict-sensitivity-eval-bloom
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
AI models are already deployed in societies affected by armed conflict, and journalists, humanitarian workers, governments and ordinary citizens rely on them for information or for their work processes. No established practice exists for checking whether their outputs can make those conflicts worse. We tested nine model configurations from four providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, xAI) on 90 multi-turn scenarios designed to surface misaligned behaviour in conflict contexts: false equivalence between documented atrocities, denial of genocide, and failure to recognise ethnic slurs, among others. When such outputs feed into journalism, humanitarian reporting, or public debate, they can deepen divisions in fragile societies. Failure rates span 6\% to 47\% between the best and worst performing models, which makes model choice a safety question in its own right and when users pushed for ``balance'' in cases where international courts have already assigned responsibility, five of nine configurations failed 80 to 100 percent of the time. We release the first evaluation framework for this domain and propose adding it to alignment evaluation portfolios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能会让冲突更严重吗？在冲突背景下大语言模型部署中的对齐失败
人工智能模型已在受武装冲突影响的社会中部署，记者、人道主义工作者、政府和普通民众依赖它们获取信息或完成工作。目前尚无既定实践用于检查其输出是否会加剧冲突。我们对四家供应商（OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、xAI）的九种模型配置，在90个设计用于揭示冲突背景下对齐失败行为的多轮场景中进行了测试：包括对已记录暴行的虚假等同、否认种族灭绝、未能识别种族侮辱等。当此类输出进入新闻报道、人道主义报道或公共辩论时，可能加深脆弱社会的分裂。模型表现最差者失败率达47%，最佳者为6%，这使得模型选择本身即成为安全问题；当用户要求在国际法庭已明确责任的案例中保持“平衡”时，九种配置中有五种失败率高达80%至100%。我们发布了该领域的首个评估框架，并建议将其纳入对齐评估体系。

Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification
Authors: Mahdi Nasermoghadasi
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:27+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).
中文标题/摘要
标题：阅读任务失败的激活分析：对GPT-2 Small在间接宾语识别任务中稀疏特征的审计
我们报告了一项小型、可复现的审计，研究GPT-2 Small在间接宾语识别（IOI）任务中失败与成功试次上，稀疏自动编码器（SAE）特征的激活差异。在300个提示下，GPT-2 Small准确率为79.7%；Bloom（2024）第8层残差流SAE的24,576个特征中，146个通过Holm校正显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量（|Cohen's d| > 0.8）。最强相关特征17,491（d=+2.93，Neuronpedia标签‘加密密钥’）仅在提示中转移对象为‘钥匙’时激活，此时GPT-2 Small失败率达93.3%，而其他七种对象仅7.5%（Fisher精确p = 8.79 x 10^-33）。我们对该相关性进行了三项机制性检验：(i) 因果消融：在45个‘钥匙’提示的所有token位置中置零该特征，未恢复准确率（6.7% → 4.4%）；该特征为相关性而非充分原因。(ii) 表示基线：对原始768维残差流进行逻辑回归，ROC AUC为0.929，与前100个SAE特征（0.927）相当；SAE基底提升可解释性，而非预测能力。(iii) 随机种子鲁棒性检验：五次随机种子下‘钥匙’子集失败率稳定在75.0–93.3%（行为效应真实），但该特征仅在5次运行中的1次为最大|d|特征。因此，方法贡献在于审计流程（低成本、模型无关、揭示命名相关特征），而非发现的单个特征。我们公开代码、300提示语语料库、300x24,576激活矩阵、消融与基线脚本及图表。完整流程可在笔记本电脑（Apple M3 Max，无独立GPU）上运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding why GPT-2 small fails on indirect object identification tasks, the study audits sparse-autoencoder features in layer 8 to identify which activate differently on failure versus success. Using 300 prompts, 146 features passed a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 showed large effect sizes; feature 17,491 ('cryptographic keys') was the strongest correlate, activating only when the prompt referenced 'the keys,' correlating with 93.3% failure rate. Three controls tested its causal role: ablating it reduced accuracy slightly but not enough to restore performance, logistic regression on raw activations matched SAE performance, and the feature was not consistently top-ranked across seeds. The key contribution is the audit pipeline, not the feature itself, as it is cheap, model-agnostic, and reveals named correlates.
为理解GPT-2 small在间接宾语识别任务中的失败原因，研究审计了第8层残差流中的稀疏自动编码器特征，识别出在失败与成功试次中激活模式不同的特征。在300个提示下，146个特征通过Holm校正显著性阈值，105个具有大效应量；特征17,491（标签为'加密密钥'）是最强相关特征，仅在提示提及'钥匙'时激活，对应93.3%的失败率。三项控制验证其因果作用：将其置零仅小幅降低准确率，原始残差流的逻辑回归与SAE特征表现相当，且该特征在不同随机种子中并非始终最显著。研究核心贡献是审计流程本身，而非特定特征，因其廉价、模型无关且能揭示可解释的相关特征。
WorldKV: Efficient World Memory with World Retrieval and Compression
Authors: Jung Yi, Minjae Kim, Paul Hyunbin Cho, Wooseok Jang, Sangdoo Yun, Seungryong Kim
First: 2026-05-21T16:55:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:55:04+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive video diffusion models have enabled real-time, action-conditioned world generation. However, sustaining a persistent world, where revisiting a previously seen viewpoint yields consistent content, remains an open problem. Full KV-cache attention preserves this consistency but breaks real-time constraints: memory footprint and attention cost grow linearly with rollout length. Sliding window inference restores throughput but discards long-term consistency. We propose WorldKV, a training-free framework with two components: World Retrieval and World Compression. World Retrieval stores evicted KV-cache chunks in GPU/CPU memory and selectively retrieves scene-relevant chunks via camera/ action correspondence, inserting them back into the native attention window without re-encoding. World Compression prunes redundant tokens within each chunk via key-key similarity to an anchor frame, halving per-chunk storage to fit 2x more history under a fixed budget. On Matrix-Game-2.0 and LingBot- World-Fast, WorldKV matches or exceeds full-KV memory fidelity at roughly 2x the throughput, and is competitive with memory-trained baselines without any fine-tuning. Project Page: https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
中文标题/摘要
标题：WorldKV：基于世界检索与压缩的高效世界记忆
自回归视频扩散模型已实现实时、动作条件下的世界生成。然而，维持一个持久的世界——即重新访问先前视角时内容保持一致——仍是一个开放性问题。完整的KV缓存注意力可保持一致性，但会破坏实时性：内存占用和注意力开销随展开长度线性增长。滑动窗口推理恢复吞吐量，但牺牲了长期一致性。我们提出WorldKV，一个无需训练的框架，包含两个组件：世界检索与世界压缩。世界检索将被逐出的KV缓存块存储于GPU/CPU内存中，并通过相机/动作对应关系选择性检索场景相关块，将其重新插入原注意力窗口而不需重新编码。世界压缩通过键-键相似度对每个块内冗余token进行剪枝，以锚定帧为基准，将每块存储量减半，在固定预算下可容纳两倍历史记录。在Matrix-Game-2.0和LingBot-World-Fast上，WorldKV在约两倍吞吐量下达到或超越完整KV内存的保真度，并在无需微调的情况下与记忆训练基线相当。项目页面：https://cvlab-kaist.github.io/WorldKV/
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of maintaining consistent world generation in real-time autoregressive video diffusion models while avoiding memory and computational bottlenecks, WorldKV introduces a training-free framework combining World Retrieval and World Compression. World Retrieval stores evicted KV-cache chunks in external memory and selectively retrieves relevant chunks based on camera and action correspondence, reintegrating them into the attention window without re-encoding. World Compression reduces per-chunk storage by pruning redundant tokens using key-key similarity to an anchor frame, effectively doubling the history capacity under fixed memory budgets. Experiments on Matrix-Game-2.0 and LingBot-World-Fast show that WorldKV achieves or surpasses full-KV memory fidelity at approximately twice the throughput, while remaining competitive with memory-trained baselines without requiring fine-tuning.
为解决自回归视频扩散模型在实时生成中维持世界状态一致性的同时避免内存与计算瓶颈的问题，WorldKV 提出了一种无需训练的框架，包含世界检索与世界压缩两个模块。世界检索将被驱逐的KV缓存块存储于外部内存，并根据相机与动作对应关系选择性地重新插入相关块，保持长期一致性而无需重编码。世界压缩通过以锚帧为基准的键-键相似度剪枝每个块内冗余token，将每块存储开销减半，从而在固定内存预算下翻倍历史容量。在Matrix-Game-2.0与LingBot-World-Fast上的实验表明，WorldKV在约两倍吞吐量下实现或超越全KV缓存的保真度，并在无需微调的情况下与内存训练基线方法表现相当。
Live Music Diffusion Models: Efficient Fine-Tuning and Post-Training of Interactive Diffusion Music Generators
Authors: Zachary Novack, Stephen Brade, Haven Kim, Hugo Flores García, Nithya Shikarpur, Chinmay Talegaonkar, Suwan Kim, Valerie K. Chen, Julian McAuley, Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick, Cheng-Zhi Anna Huang
First: 2026-05-21T16:54:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:54:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Interactive streaming music generation promises the use of generative models for live performance and co-creation that is impossible with offline models. However, SOTA models exist in the discrete-AR regime, requiring industrial levels of compute for both training and inference. In this work, we investigate whether audio diffusion models, with their wide support in the open-source community but non-streaming bidirectional nature, can be repurposed efficiently into interactive models accessible on consumer hardware. By taking a critical look at the modern pipeline for block-wise outpainting diffusion, we identify critical inefficiencies during inference that result in strictly worse computational efficiency than their discrete-AR counterparts. We propose Live Music Diffusion Models (LMDMs), a simple modification of the generative diffusion process that recovers, and then outperforms, the inference complexity of the discrete Live Music Models (LMMs) through block-wise KV Caching. Unlike LMMs, LMDMs further enable stable post-training alignment through our novel ARC-Forcing paradigm, reducing error accumulation without any explicit RL or reward models. We demonstrate the application of LMDMs in a number of creative domains, including text-conditioned generation, sketch-based music synthesis, and jamming. We finally show how LMDMs can be used as a generative instrument in a real artist-AI collaboration, utilizing LMDMs as a "generative delay" to transform musicians' improvisation live for variable timbral effects while running locally on a consumer gaming laptop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：实时音乐扩散模型：交互式扩散音乐生成器的高效微调与后训练
交互式流媒体音乐生成有望利用生成模型实现现场表演与共创，这是离线模型无法实现的。然而，当前最先进的模型存在于离散自回归框架中，训练和推理均需工业级算力。本文探讨音频扩散模型是否能高效改造为适用于消费级硬件的交互式模型。通过审视现代块级外推扩散的流程，我们发现推理阶段存在关键低效环节，导致其计算效率劣于离散自回归模型。我们提出实时音乐扩散模型（LMDMs），通过块级KV缓存对生成扩散过程进行简单修改，不仅恢复了离散实时音乐模型（LMMs）的推理复杂度，且表现更优。与LMMs不同，LMDMs还通过我们提出的ARC-强制范式实现稳定后训练对齐，无需显式强化学习或奖励模型即可减少误差累积。我们展示了LMDMs在多个创意领域的应用，包括文本条件生成、草图音乐合成与即兴演奏。最后，我们演示了LMDMs如何作为生成乐器用于真实艺术家与AI协作，通过本地运行于消费级游戏笔记本电脑，将音乐家即兴演奏实时转化为可变音色效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, real-time music generation on consumer hardware, this work explores whether audio diffusion models—despite their non-streaming bidirectional nature—can be adapted into interactive systems. The authors identify inefficiencies in standard diffusion inference pipelines and propose Live Music Diffusion Models (LMDMs), which modify the diffusion process to use block-wise KV Caching, achieving inference efficiency comparable to or better than discrete autoregressive models. LMDMs further enable stable post-training alignment via ARC-Forcing, eliminating error accumulation without reinforcement learning. Experiments show LMDMs successfully support text-conditioned generation, sketch-based synthesis, and live jamming, and are demonstrated in a real artist-AI collaboration where they function as a generative delay on a gaming laptop, producing variable timbral effects in real time.
为实现消费级硬件上的实时音乐生成，该研究探讨音频扩散模型能否被高效改造为交互式系统。作者发现标准扩散推理流程存在效率瓶颈，提出Live Music Diffusion Models（LMDMs），通过块级KV缓存优化扩散过程，其推理效率可媲美甚至超越离散自回归模型。LMDMs还引入ARC-Forcing机制，在无需强化学习的前提下实现稳定后训练对齐。实验表明LMDMs支持文本引导生成、草图音乐合成与即兴伴奏，并在真实艺术家与AI协作中作为生成延迟模块运行于游戏笔记本上，实时产生多变音色效果。
Findings of the Counter Turing Test: AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Rajarshi Roy, Nasrin Imanpour, Ashhar Aziz, Shashwat Bajpai, Gurpreet Singh, Shwetangshu Biswas, Kapil Wanaskar, Parth Patwa, Subhankar Ghosh, Shreyas Dixit, Nilesh Ranjan Pal, Vipula Rawte, Ritvik Garimella, Amitava Das, Amit Sheth, Vasu Sharma, Aishwarya Naresh Reganti, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha
Venue: AAAI 2025
First: 2026-05-20T06:32:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:53:24+00:00
Comments: Defactify4 @AAAI 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancements in generative AI technologies, such as Stable Diffusion, DALL-E, and Midjourney, have significantly transformed the creation of synthetic visual content. While these models enable innovation across industries, they also pose serious challenges, including misinformation, disinformation, and biased content generation. The increasing realism of AI-generated images makes their detection a pressing concern for researchers, policymakers, and industry stakeholders. In this paper, we present the findings of the Defactify 4.0 workshop, which introduced the Counter Turing Test (CT2) for AI-Generated Image Detection. The competition consisted of two key tasks: (1) binary classification of images as either AI-generated or real and (2) identification of the specific generative model responsible for an AI-generated image. To facilitate this, we developed the MS COCOAI dataset, consisting of 50,000 synthetic images from multiple generative models alongside real-world images from the MS COCO dataset. Participants employed diverse detection strategies, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs), Vision Transformers (ViTs), frequency-based analysis, contrastive learning, and multimodal techniques. The results demonstrated that while AI-generated images can be detected with high accuracy (F1-score > 0.83), identifying the exact model used remains significantly more challenging (highest F1-score: 0.4986). These findings highlight the need for improved model fingerprinting, adversarial robustness, and real-time detection mechanisms.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗图灵测试：AI生成图像检测研究结果
随着Stable Diffusion、DALL-E和Midjourney等生成式AI技术的快速发展，合成视觉内容的创作方式已发生显著变革。尽管这些模型推动了各行业的创新，但也带来了虚假信息、误导性内容和偏见生成等严峻挑战。AI生成图像日益逼真，使其检测成为研究人员、政策制定者和行业利益相关者亟需关注的问题。 本文呈现了Defactify 4.0研讨会的成果，该研讨会引入了对抗图灵测试（CT2）用于AI生成图像检测。竞赛包含两项核心任务：（1）将图像二分类为AI生成或真实图像；（2）识别AI生成图像所使用的具体生成模型。为支持该研究，我们构建了MS COCOAI数据集，包含50,000张来自多种生成模型的合成图像，以及来自MS COCO真实世界图像。 参赛者采用了多种检测策略，包括卷积神经网络（CNN）、视觉Transformer（ViT）、频域分析、对比学习和多模态技术。结果显示，AI生成图像的检测准确率较高（F1分数>0.83），但精确识别所用模型仍极具挑战（最高F1分数：0.4986）。这些发现凸显了改进模型指纹识别、对抗鲁棒性和实时检测机制的必要性。

Driven by the proliferation of realistic AI-generated images from models like Stable Diffusion and DALL-E, this study addresses the urgent need to detect synthetic visuals amid growing concerns over misinformation. The Counter Turing Test (CT2) competition, hosted at AAAI 2025, evaluated two tasks: binary classification of AI vs. real images and model identification. Using the MS COCOAI dataset of 50,000 synthetic and real images, participants applied CNNs, Vision Transformers, frequency analysis, and multimodal methods. 鉴于AI生成图像日益逼真及其带来的社会风险，本研究提出了反图灵测试（CT2）以评估检测能力。基于包含5万张合成与真实图像的MS COCOAI数据集，参赛者采用CNN、ViT、频域分析及多模态方法进行AI生成图像二分类及模型溯源。结果显示，图像真假分类准确率较高（F1>0.83），但模型识别难度大（F1=0.4986），凸显了模型指纹识别与鲁棒检测机制亟待提升。
Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative
Authors: Jorge Fandinno, Yuliya Lierler, Torsten Schaub
First: 2026-05-21T16:52:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:52:18+00:00
Comments: To appear in Theory and Practice of Logic Programming
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：参数化模块化答案集程序的声明式表达
本文探讨一阶答案集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，引入一种名为参数化模块逻辑程序的新形式化方法，允许定义带参数和内涵性语句的子程序。我们展示该形式化方法如何捕捉具有集体控制的clingo程序语义，该特性支持子程序的结构化与实例化。我们为模块化ASP提供理论基础，说明其实用性，并与传统非模块化ASP建立联系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to structure and reuse logic programs in answer set programming, this paper introduces parametric modular logic programs, a formalism that enables defining subprograms with parameters and intensional statements. The method extends traditional ASP by allowing modular decomposition and instantiation through parameterized components, capturing the semantics of clingo’s collective control feature. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach successfully formalizes modular reasoning while preserving the expressive power of non-modular ASP, enabling clearer program organization and reuse without sacrificing computational adequacy.
为解决答案集编程中逻辑程序结构化与复用的需求，本文提出参数化模块化逻辑程序形式化方法，支持子程序带参数定义与内涵语句。该方法通过扩展一阶ASP，实现子程序的模块化组合与实例化，捕捉clingo集体控制机制的语义。实验表明，该方法有效支持模块化推理，保持传统ASP的表达能力，同时实现程序结构化而不损失声明式语义。
AnyMo: Geometry-Aware Setup-Agnostic Modeling of Human Motion in the Wild
Authors: Baiyu Chen, Zechen Li, Wilson Wongso, Lihuan Li, Xiachong Lin, Hao Xue, Benjamin Tag, Flora Salim
First: 2026-05-21T16:52:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:52:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
As wearable and mobile devices become increasingly embedded in daily life, they offer a practical way to continuously sense human motion in the wild. But inertial signals are highly dependent on the sensing setup, including body location, mounting position, sensor orientation, device hardware, and sampling protocol. This setup dependence makes it difficult to learn motion representations that transfer across devices and datasets, and limits the broader use of wearable IMUs beyond closed-set recognition. We introduce AnyMo, a geometry-aware framework for setup-agnostic human motion modeling. AnyMo uses physics-grounded IMU simulation over dense body-surface placements to generate diverse and plausible synthetic signals, pre-trains a graph encoder from paired synthetic placement views and masked partial observations, tokenizes multi-position IMU into full-body motion tokens, and aligns these tokens with an LLM for motion-language understanding. We evaluate AnyMo on three complementary tasks: zero-shot activity recognition across 14 unseen downstream datasets, cross-modal retrieval, and wearable IMU motion captioning, where it improves average Accuracy/F1/R@2 by 11.7\%/11.6\%/22.6\% on HAR, increases zero-shot IMU-to-text and text-to-IMU retrieval MRR by 15.9\% and 28.6\%, respectively, and improves zero-shot captioning BERT-F1 by 18.8\%. These results support AnyMo as a generalist model for wearable motion understanding in the wild. Project page: https://baiyuchen.com/project/AnyMo.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AnyMo：面向野外人体运动建模的几何感知、设置无关框架
随着可穿戴和移动设备日益融入日常生活，它们为在野外持续感知人体运动提供了实用途径。但惯性信号高度依赖传感设置，包括身体位置、安装位置、传感器朝向、设备硬件及采样协议。这种设置依赖性使得学习可跨设备和数据集迁移的运动表征变得困难，也限制了可穿戴IMU在闭集识别之外的广泛应用。我们提出AnyMo，一种面向野外人体运动建模的几何感知、设置无关框架。AnyMo通过密集体表位置上的物理基础IMU仿真生成多样且合理的合成信号，从配对的合成放置视角和掩码部分观测中预训练图编码器，将多位置IMU令牌化为全身运动令牌，并与LLM对齐以实现运动-语言理解。我们在三个互补任务上评估AnyMo：跨14个未见下游数据集的零样本活动识别、跨模态检索及可穿戴IMU运动描述生成，其中在HAR任务中平均提升Accuracy/F1/R@2分别为11.7%/11.6%/22.6%，零样本IMU到文本及文本到IMU检索MRR分别提升15.9%和28.6%，零样本描述生成BERT-F1提升18.8%。这些结果支持AnyMo作为野外可穿戴运动理解的通用模型。项目页面：https://baiyuchen.com/project/AnyMo。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of inconsistent inertial sensor setups hindering cross-device motion modeling, AnyMo introduces a geometry-aware framework that simulates diverse IMU signals across dense body placements using physics-grounded methods. It pre-trains a graph encoder on synthetic data with masked observations, tokenizes multi-position IMU signals into full-body motion tokens, and aligns them with an LLM for motion-language understanding. Evaluated across zero-shot activity recognition, cross-modal retrieval, and motion captioning, AnyMo achieves significant gains: 11.7% accuracy, 11.6% F1, and 22.6% R@2 improvements on HAR; 15.9% and 28.6% MRR boosts in IMU-to-text and text-to-IMU retrieval; and 18.8% BERT-F1 gain in captioning, demonstrating its effectiveness as a generalist motion understanding model in real-world settings.
为解决惯性传感器安装方式差异导致的跨设备运动建模困难，AnyMo提出一种几何感知框架，通过物理驱动的密集体表IMU信号仿真生成多样且合理的合成数据。该框架先在合成数据上预训练图编码器，对多位置IMU信号进行分词并转化为全身运动token，再与大语言模型对齐以实现运动-语言理解。在零样本活动识别、跨模态检索和IMU运动描述三项任务中，AnyMo表现显著：在HAR任务中准确率、F1、R@2分别提升11.7%、11.6%、22.6%；IMU到文本与文本到IMU检索的MRR分别提升15.9%和28.6%；零样本描述任务BERT-F1提升18.8%，验证了其在野外穿戴设备运动理解中的泛化能力。
Insights Generator: Systematic Corpus-Level Trace Diagnostics for LLM Agents
Authors: Akshay Manglik, Apaar Shanker, Kaustubh Deshpande, Jason Qin, Yash Maurya, Veronica Chatrath, Vijay S. Kalmath, Levi Lentz, Yuan, Xue
First: 2026-05-20T16:13:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:51:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diagnosing failures in LLM agents remains largely manual. Practitioners inspect a small subset of execution traces, form ad-hoc hypotheses, and iterate. This process misses patterns that only emerge across trace populations and does not scale to production corpora where individual traces span tens of thousands of tokens. We formalize the problem of corpus-level trace diagnostics. Given a corpus of execution traces, the goal is to produce grounded natural-language insights that characterize systematic behavioral patterns across trace groups, each linked to supporting evidence. We present the Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system that answers diagnostic questions by proposing and testing hypotheses across the trace corpus to produce an evidence-backed insights report. We evaluate IG across qualitative and objective dimensions, spanning rubric-based report assessment and downstream performance improvements achieved by implementing IG insights. Human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4pp over the unmodified baseline scaffold, and coding agents leveraging IG-derived insights show consistent and stable gains. Across benchmarks, IG's scout-investigator architecture produces findings comparable in detection coverage to competing approaches, while domain experts rated IG reports as leading depth and evidence quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：洞察生成器：面向大语言模型代理的系统性语料级执行轨迹诊断
诊断大语言模型代理的故障目前仍主要依赖人工。从业者仅检查少量执行轨迹，形成临时假设并反复迭代，此过程无法捕捉仅在轨迹群体中显现的模式，也难以扩展至生产语料，其中单条轨迹可达数万token。我们形式化了语料级轨迹诊断问题：给定执行轨迹语料，目标是生成基于证据的自然语言洞察，刻画各轨迹组间的系统性行为模式。我们提出了洞察生成器（IG），一种多智能体系统，通过在轨迹语料中提出并验证假设，生成证据支持的洞察报告。我们在定性与客观维度评估IG，涵盖基于评分标准的报告评估及实施IG洞察后下游性能提升。使用IG报告的人类专家，其框架性能较未修改基线提升30.4pp；利用IG洞察的编码代理表现稳定且持续提升。在多个基准测试中，IG的侦察-调查架构检测覆盖率与竞品相当，而领域专家评价IG报告在深度与证据质量方面领先。
