Motivated by the inefficiency and scalability limitations of manual LLM agent failure diagnosis, the paper introduces Insights Generator (IG), a multi-agent system designed to automatically generate evidence-backed natural-language insights from large corpora of execution traces. IG systematically identifies and validates behavioral patterns across trace groups, producing diagnostic reports that link findings to supporting evidence. Experimental results show that human experts using IG reports improve scaffold performance by 30.4 percentage points over the baseline, while coding agents using IG insights achieve consistent and stable gains. IG’s scout-investigator architecture matches or exceeds competing methods in detection coverage and is rated superior by domain experts in report depth and evidence quality.
为解决人工诊断LLM代理失败效率低、难以扩展的问题，本文提出Insights Generator（IG），一个自动分析大规模执行轨迹语料库、生成系统性行为模式诊断报告的多智能体系统。IG通过提出并验证假设，生成带有证据支持的自然语言洞察报告。实验表明，使用IG报告的人类专家在scaffold性能上比基线提升30.4个百分点，基于IG洞察的编码代理表现稳定且持续提升。在多个基准测试中，IG的scout-investigator架构在检测覆盖率上与竞品相当，且专家评价其报告在深度和证据质量上表现最佳。
AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments
Authors: Sid-ali Temkit
First: 2026-05-21T16:51:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:51:04+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 14 figures, 6 tables. Single author. Code, data (75,898 deduplicated API responses), and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/chutapp/amel
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations. Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17). Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AMEL：累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响
大型语言模型常被用作自动化评估器，用于审查代码、内容审核或评分，通常有大量项目通过同一对话。我们探讨先前对话历史的极性是否会影响后续判断，这一效应称为累积消息效应对大语言模型判断的影响（AMEL）。通过对4家供应商（OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型）的11个模型进行75,898次API调用，我们展示了相同测试项目在孤立或跟随充满主要正面或负面评价的历史后的情况。模型会趋向于对话的主流极性（d = -0.17，p < 10^-46）。该效应主要集中在模型基线时真正不确定的项目上（高熵项目d = -0.34，与基线确定性项目d = -0.15相比）。偏见不随上下文长度增加：5轮先前对话与50轮产生相同偏移（Spearman |r| < 0.01；OLS斜率p = 0.80）。同时存在负面偏向不对称性：每项配对比较中，负面历史诱导的偏见是正面历史的1.62倍（t = 13.46，p < 10^-39，n = 2,481）。扩展规模有助于但无法完全解决该问题（Anthropic：Haiku -0.22至Opus -0.17；OpenAI：Nano -0.34至GPT-5.2 -0.17）。三个后续研究缩小了机制范围。标记概率分布连续变化，而非阈值触发。负面偏向不对称性同时包含标记级和语义成分，尽管在当前样本量下归因平衡仍属探索性。位置无关紧要：50轮历史中任何位置的5轮偏倚对话均产生相同偏移。评估流程中最简单的解决方案是每项独立上下文；若必须批处理，平衡历史有助于缓解问题。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether prior conversation history biases large language models' judgments, a phenomenon termed the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Using 75,898 API calls across 11 models from four providers, identical test items were presented either in isolation or after histories saturated with positive or negative evaluations. Results show models consistently shift toward the prevailing polarity in history (effect size d = -0.17, p < 10^-46), with stronger bias observed on uncertain items (d = -0.34) than deterministic ones (d = -0.15). The bias does not increase with context length (5 vs. 50 turns yield similar shifts) and is asymmetric: negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive ones. Scaling models does not eliminate the effect. Follow-up analyses reveal continuous token probability shifts, a negativity asymmetry with both token and semantic components, and that position in history does not matter. The simplest mitigation is resetting context per item or balancing history when batching.
本研究探讨先前对话历史是否会影响大语言模型的判断，这一现象被称为累积消息效应（AMEL）。通过75,898次API调用，覆盖11个来自四大厂商的模型，相同测试项在孤立呈现或跟随大量正面或负面评价历史后进行测试。结果表明，模型会持续向对话中主导极性倾斜（效应量d = -0.17，p < 10^-46），在基线不确定性高的项目上偏差更显著（d = -0.34）。偏差与上下文长度无关（5轮与50轮效果相同），负面历史诱导的偏差是正面历史的1.62倍。模型规模增大虽能缓解但无法根除该效应，其机制表现为令牌概率分布的连续变化而非阈值切换。历史中位置不影响偏差，最简单解决方案是每个项目重置上下文或在批处理时平衡历史内容。
Pelican-Unify 1.0: A Unified Embodied Intelligence Model for Understanding, Reasoning, Imagination and Action
Authors: Yi Zhang, Yinda Chen, Che Liu, Zeyuan Ding, Jin Xu, Shilong Zou, Junwei Liao, Jiayu Hu, Xiancong Ren, Xiaopeng Zhang, Yechi Liu, Haoyuan Shi, Zecong Tang, Haosong Sun, Renwen Cui, Kuishu Wu, Wenhai Liu, Yang Xu, Yingji Zhang, Yidong Wang, Senkang Hu, Jinpeng Lu, Nga Teng Chan, Yechen Wu, Zeting Liu, Xianzhou Hou, Yong Dai, Jian Tang, Xiaozhu Ju
First: 2026-05-14T17:50:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:50:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Pelican-Unify 1.0, the first embodied foundation model trained according to the principle of unification. Pelican-Unify 1.0 uses a single VLM as a unified understanding module, mapping scenes, instructions, visual contexts, and action histories into a shared semantic space. The same VLM also serves as a unified reasoning module, autoregressively producing task-, action-, and future-oriented chains of thought in a single forward pass and projecting the final hidden state into a dense latent variable. A Unified Future Generator (UFG) then conditions on this latent variable and jointly generates future videos and future actions through two modality-specific output heads within the same denoising process. The language, video, and action losses are all backpropagated into the shared representation, enabling the model to jointly optimize understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action during training, rather than training three isolated expert systems. Experiments demonstrate that unification does not imply compromise. With a single checkpoint, Pelican-Unify 1.0 achieves strong performance across all three capabilities: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, the best among comparable-scale models; 66.03 on WorldArena, ranking first; and 93.5 on RoboTwin, the second-best average among compared action methods. These results show that the unified paradigm succeeds in preserving specialist strength while bringing understanding, reasoning, imagination, and action into one model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鹈鹕统一1.0：一种统一具身智能模型，用于理解、推理、想象与行动
我们提出了鹈鹕统一1.0，这是首个依据统一原则训练的具身基础模型。鹈鹕统一1.0使用单一视觉语言模型（VLM）作为统一理解模块，将场景、指令、视觉上下文和行动历史映射到共享语义空间。同一VLM同时作为统一推理模块，在单次前向传播中自回归生成任务导向、行动导向和未来导向的思维链，并将最终隐藏状态投影为稠密潜在变量。随后，统一未来生成器（UFG）基于该潜在变量，在同一去噪过程中通过两个模态特定输出头联合生成未来视频与未来行动。语言、视频与行动损失均反向传播至共享表示，使模型在训练中联合优化理解、推理、想象与行动，而非训练三个独立的专家系统。 实验表明，统一并不意味着妥协。单个检查点下，鹈鹕统一1.0在三项能力上均取得优异表现：在八个VLM基准测试中得分64.7，优于同类规模模型；在WorldArena中得分66.03，排名第一；在RoboTwin中得分93.5，为对比行动方法中的第二佳平均分。这些结果表明，统一范式成功保留了专家能力，同时将理解、推理、想象与行动整合于单一模型中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training separate systems for understanding, reasoning, and action, Pelican-Unify 1.0 introduces a unified embodied intelligence model that leverages a single vision-language model (VLM) as both an understanding and reasoning module, mapping multimodal inputs into a shared semantic space and generating future-oriented chains of thought. A Unified Future Generator then jointly produces future video and action sequences conditioned on the VLM’s latent output, with all modalities jointly optimized via shared backpropagation. Experimental results show that this unified approach achieves top-tier performance: 64.7 on eight VLM benchmarks, 66.03 on WorldArena, and 93.5 on RoboTwin, demonstrating that unification does not sacrifice specialization and enables strong cross-capability performance.
为解决传统方法中理解、推理与行动需分别训练多个专家模型的低效问题，Pelican-Unify 1.0 提出统一的具身智能框架：单个视觉语言模型同时承担理解与推理功能，将场景、指令、视觉上下文及动作历史映射至共享语义空间，并自回归生成任务导向、动作导向及未来导向的思维链；随后，统一未来生成器基于推理输出的稠密潜在变量，通过双模态输出头在单次去噪过程中联合生成未来视频与动作。语言、视频与动作损失均反向传播至共享表征，实现四类能力的联合优化。实验表明，该统一架构未牺牲专业能力，单模型在VLM基准上达64.7分（同类模型最优）、WorldArena上达66.03分（排名第一）、RoboTwin上达93.5分（排名第二），证明统一范式可同时保持专家级表现并实现多能力融合。
Abstraction for Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Clarisse Wibault, Alexander Goldie, Antonio Villares, Maike Osborne, Jakob Foerster
First: 2026-05-21T16:50:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:50:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) often exhibit significant redundancy due to symmetries and shared structure across state-goal pairs in real-world Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (GCRL). While hierarchical policies have been motivated for horizon reduction via temporal abstraction in offline GCRL, we demonstrate that hierarchy also enables absolute abstraction. By introducing relativised options as well as distinct representations for different levels of the hierarchy, we demonstrate how an agent can reuse experience across similar contexts of the state-space. Based on this framework, we introduce two simple algorithms for learning relativised options and abstracting from the absolute frame of reference. Our experiments show that such inductive biases significantly improve performance in offline GCRL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离线目标条件强化学习中的抽象方法
马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）在现实世界的目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中常因状态-目标对之间的对称性和共享结构而表现出显著冗余。虽然分层策略已被提出用于通过时间抽象减少时间跨度，我们证明分层结构也可实现绝对抽象。通过引入相对化选项及不同层级的差异化表示，我们展示了智能体如何在状态空间相似上下文中复用经验。基于此框架，我们提出了两种简单算法，用于学习相对化选项并从绝对参考框架中抽象。实验表明，此类归纳偏置显著提升了离线GCRL的性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the redundancy in state-goal pairs within real-world goal-conditioned reinforcement learning, this work proposes using hierarchical policies to enable absolute abstraction through relativised options and distinct representations across hierarchy levels, allowing agents to reuse experience across similar state contexts. Two simple learning algorithms are introduced to acquire these relativised options and abstract from absolute reference frames. Experimental results demonstrate that these inductive biases substantially improve performance in offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning tasks.
鉴于现实世界中目标条件强化学习中状态-目标对存在的冗余，本文提出通过分层策略实现绝对抽象，利用相对化选项和分层状态表示，使智能体能在相似上下文状态间复用经验。文中设计了两种简单算法用于学习相对化选项并从绝对参考框架中抽象。实验结果表明，这些归纳偏置显著提升了离线目标条件强化学习任务的性能。
SpecBlock: Block-Iterative Speculative Decoding with Dynamic Tree Drafting
Authors: Weijie Shi, Qiang Xu, Fan Deng, Yaguang Wu, Jiarun Liu, Yehong Xu, Hao Chen, Jia Zhu, Jiajie Xu, Xiangjun Huang, Jian Yang, Xiaofang Zhou
First: 2026-05-08T04:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:49:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting a tree of candidate continuations and verifying it in one target forward. Existing drafters fall into two camps with opposite weaknesses. Autoregressive drafters such as EAGLE-3 preserve dependence along each draft path but call the drafter once per tree depth, making drafting a non-trivial share of per-iteration latency. Parallel drafters cut drafter calls by predicting multiple future positions in one forward, but each position is predicted without seeing the others, producing paths the verifier rejects. In this paper, we propose SpecBlock, a block-iterative drafter that combines path dependence with cheap drafting. Each drafter forward produces K dependent positions and we call this a block. The draft tree grows through repeated block expansions. Two mechanisms explicitly carry path dependence to keep later draft positions accurate. Within each block, a layer-wise shift carries the previous position's hidden state into every decoder layer. Across blocks, each new block can start from any position of the previous block, inheriting its hidden state to extend the path. To spend verifier budget where acceptance is likely, a co-trained rank head replaces the fixed top-k tree by allocating per-position branching during drafting. To avoid training the drafter on prefixes it never produces at inference, a valid-prefix mask drops the loss at later positions once an earlier one is wrong. Beyond static drafting, a cost-aware bandit at deployment uses free verifier feedback to update the drafter selectively, only when the expected throughput gain exceeds the update cost. Experiments show that SpecBlock improves mean speedup by 8-13% over EAGLE-3 at 44-52% of its drafting cost, and cost-aware adaptation extends this lead to 11-19%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpecBlock：基于动态树草稿的块迭代推测解码
推测解码通过草拟候选延续的树并一次性验证，加速大语言模型推理。现有草稿器分为两类，各有缺点：自回归草稿器（如EAGLE-3）保持每条路径的依赖性，但每层树深度调用一次草稿器，使草稿占迭代延迟的非微不足道部分；并行草稿器通过一次前向预测多个未来位置减少调用，但各位置独立预测，导致验证器拒绝的路径。本文提出SpecBlock，一种结合路径依赖与低成本草稿的块迭代草稿器。每次草稿前向生成K个依赖位置，称为一个块。草稿树通过重复块扩展生长。两种机制显式传递路径依赖，保持后续草稿位置准确：块内层间偏移将前一位置隐藏状态传入所有解码层；块间新块可从上一区块任意位置开始，继承其隐藏状态以延续路径。为在高接受概率处分配验证器预算，共训练的排序头替代固定top-k树，在草稿时按位置分配分支。为避免在推理中训练草稿器从未生成的前缀，有效前缀掩码在早期位置错误时屏蔽后续位置损失。除静态草稿外，部署时成本感知多臂老虎机利用免费验证器反馈选择性更新草稿器，仅当预期吞吐量增益超过更新成本时更新。实验表明，SpecBlock在44-52%草稿成本下比EAGLE-3平均加速提升8-13%，成本感知自适应进一步扩大优势至11-19%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-offs between autoregressive and parallel drafters in speculative decoding, SpecBlock introduces a block-iterative drafting method that preserves path dependence while reducing per-iteration cost. It generates K dependent positions per drafter forward (a block), expands the draft tree iteratively, and uses layer-wise hidden state shifting within blocks and state inheritance across blocks to maintain accuracy. A co-trained rank head dynamically allocates branching per position, and a valid-prefix mask prevents loss on incorrect prefixes. At deployment, a cost-aware bandit updates the drafter only when expected throughput gain outweighs update cost. Experiments show SpecBlock achieves 8-13% higher mean speedup than EAGLE-3 at 44-52% of its drafting cost, with adaptation extending the lead to 11-19%.
为解决现有投机解码器在路径依赖性与效率间的权衡问题，SpecBlock提出一种块迭代式草案生成方法，兼顾路径准确性与低成本生成。每个前向传播生成K个相互依赖的位置（一个“块”），通过迭代扩展草案树，同时在块内层间传递前一位置的隐藏状态，在块间继承任意位置的隐藏状态以延续路径。一个联合训练的排序头动态分配草案分支，优先生成高接受概率的位置，有效前缀掩码避免训练无效前缀。部署时，成本感知的多臂老虎机仅在预期吞吐量增益超过更新成本时才更新草案器。实验表明，SpecBlock在仅消耗EAGLE-3 44-52%草案开销的情况下，平均加速比提升8-13%，自适应更新后进一步提升至11-19%。
TriSweep: A Four-Drone Swarm Framework for Electromagnetic Side-Channel Analysis
Authors: Eric Yocam, Varghese Vaidyan
First: 2026-05-21T16:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:47:44+00:00
Comments: Simulation framework + systems design for a four-drone swarm performing standoff electromagnetic side-channel analysis. No hardware fabricated yet
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Electromagnetic (EM) side-channel analysis traditionally assumes a stationary, close-proximity probe - a threat model that underestimates aerial adversaries. TriSweep is a simulation framework that designs and evaluates a four-drone swarm architecture for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25-1.5 m. Three spatially specialized collector drones - Anchor (full-spectrum), Mask Probe (mask-register loading leakage), and Cipher Probe (masked SubBytes output leakage) - feed a stationary Accumulator drone that performs coherent combining (+4.8 dB SNR gain) and second-order mask cancellation via a centered product of the two spatially separated leakage streams. Evaluated against three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets (ATmega8515 masked AES-128 and 50/100-sample desynchronized variants), the framework achieves a simulated key rank of 18 +/- 1.7 (five-seed) at 0.25 m on the primary masked dataset. Profiling-trace cross-correlation alignment reduces single-drone rank from 89 to 21 on the 100-sample-jitter variant, demonstrating compensation for drone hover vibration. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator converges to a loss of 0.454 (vs. random baseline 5.545) and improves rank on desynchronized datasets. No physical hardware has been fabricated; prototype construction is the planned next step.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TriSweep：一种用于电磁侧信道分析的四无人机蜂群框架
传统电磁（EM）侧信道分析假设存在静止且近距离的探测器，这一威胁模型低估了空中对手的能力。TriSweep 是一个仿真框架，设计并评估了一种四无人机蜂群架构，用于在0.25-1.5米远的自主远距离电磁侧信道分析嵌入式微控制器。三个空间特化的采集无人机——锚点无人机（全频谱）、掩码探针无人机（掩码寄存器加载泄漏）和密码探针无人机（掩码SubBytes输出泄漏）——将数据传输给一个静止的累加器无人机，后者通过相干叠加（+4.8 dB SNR增益）和两个空间分离泄漏流的中心乘积实现二阶掩码消除。在三个真实的ANSSI ASCAD数据集（ATmega8515掩码AES-128及50/100样本不同步变体）上评估，该框架在0.25米距离下对主掩码数据集的模拟密钥排名为18±1.7（五种子）。通过配置文件迹线互相关对齐，单无人机排名从89降至21（100样本抖动变体），展示了对无人机悬停振动的补偿能力。累加器中的双通道CNN收敛至损失0.454（对比随机基线5.545），并在不同步数据集上提升排名。尚未制造物理硬件；原型构建是下一步计划。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional stationary EM side-channel analysis against aerial adversaries, TriSweep introduces a four-drone swarm framework for autonomous standoff EM-SCA of embedded microcontrollers at 0.25–1.5 m. The system employs three specialized drones—Anchor, Mask Probe, and Cipher Probe—to capture distinct leakage signals, which are combined coherently by a stationary Accumulator drone to achieve a +4.8 dB SNR gain and second-order mask cancellation. Evaluated on three real ANSSI ASCAD datasets, the framework achieved a simulated key rank of 18 ± 1.7 on the primary masked dataset at 0.25 m, with profiling-trace alignment reducing rank from 89 to 21 on a jittered variant, mitigating drone vibration effects. A two-channel CNN in the Accumulator achieved a loss of 0.454, significantly outperforming a random baseline of 5.545 and improving rank on desynchronized datasets.
为应对传统静态电磁侧信道分析对空中攻击者估计不足的问题，TriSweep提出了一种四无人机集群框架，用于在0.25–1.5米距离外自主执行嵌入式微控制器的电磁侧信道分析。系统由三类专用无人机——锚点无人机、掩码探针无人机和密钥探针无人机——分别采集不同泄漏信号，由静止的累加器无人机进行相干叠加（+4.8 dB信噪比增益）和二阶掩码抵消。在三个真实ANSSI ASCAD数据集上测试，该框架在主掩码数据集0.25米处实现18±1.7的模拟密钥排名；在100样本抖动变体中，通过轨迹对齐将单机排名从89降至21，补偿了无人机悬停振动。累加器中的双通道CNN收敛至0.454损失（随机基线为5.545），进一步提升了非同步数据集的排名表现。
Beyond the Org Chart: AI and the Transformation of Invisible Work
Authors: Stephanie Rosenthal, Shamsi Iqbal
First: 2026-05-21T16:46:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:46:44+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An increasing number of news and research articles report that AI adoption is allowing professionals to blur and extend the boundaries of their corporate roles. With the goal of understanding how work processes might be changing in an AI-forward company, we interviewed 24 product-focused individuals at a large technology firm about how AI has impacted their own work, their work within their product team, and their professional interactions. Our conversations suggest that AI is not only changing formal role responsibilities and collaborations between those roles, but also changing informal cultural practices like professional mentoring that are key to helping professionals settle in their positions, stay engaged with their work, and grow their careers. Some of these changes are positive, such as smoother collaboration between peers, but other changes are more nuanced and put the typical career growth opportunities, like receiving feedback from professional networks and promoting leadership and mentorship, at risk. We propose steps that AI companies can take to make the invisible work more visible. Additionally, we propose efforts that individuals and leaders can take to support their colleagues through AI transformation while preserving healthy company cultures that support diverse thinking, collaboration, and informal interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越组织架构：人工智能与隐形工作的转型
越来越多的新闻和研究文章指出，人工智能的采用正使专业人士模糊并扩展其在公司中的角色边界。为理解在人工智能驱动的公司中工作流程可能如何变化，我们采访了某大型科技公司24位以产品为中心的员工，了解人工智能如何影响他们的个人工作、产品团队内的工作以及专业互动。我们的对话表明，人工智能不仅改变了正式角色的责任分工和角色间的协作，也改变了诸如专业指导等非正式文化实践——这些实践对帮助专业人士适应岗位、保持工作投入度和职业成长至关重要。部分变化是积极的，如同事间协作更顺畅；但其他变化更为复杂，可能削弱传统职业发展机会，例如从专业网络获得反馈、推动领导力与指导能力等。我们建议人工智能公司可采取措施使隐形工作更显性化；同时，个人和领导者也可采取行动，在人工智能转型中支持同事，同时维护支持多元思维、协作与非正式互动的健康公司文化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing evidence that AI is reshaping professional roles beyond formal job descriptions, the study interviewed 24 product-focused employees at a major tech firm to explore how AI affects their work processes, team dynamics, and informal practices. Findings reveal that AI not only alters formal responsibilities and inter-role collaboration but also disrupts informal cultural mechanisms such as mentoring, which are vital for career development and engagement. While some changes—like improved peer collaboration—are beneficial, others threaten traditional career growth pathways, including feedback loops and leadership development through informal networks. The authors recommend organizational strategies to make invisible work more visible and suggest individual and leadership actions to sustain healthy, diverse, and collaborative workplace cultures amid AI adoption.
鉴于越来越多的研究表明AI正在重塑超出正式职位描述的职业角色，本研究对一家大型科技公司中24名产品导向员工进行了访谈，探讨AI对其工作流程、团队协作及非正式文化实践（如指导）的影响。研究发现，AI不仅改变了正式职责与协作模式，也影响了职业发展与参与度所依赖的隐形文化机制，部分结果提升了同事间协作，但另一些则威胁传统反馈、领导力与指导机会。作者建议企业采取措施使这些隐形工作更显性化，并提出个人与领导者可采取行动，在AI转型中维持健康、多元、协作的组织文化。
Tokenization with Split Trees
Authors: Craig W. Schmidt, Michael Krumdick, Adam Wiemerslage, Seth Ebner, Varshini Reddy, Yuval Pinter, Chris Tanner
First: 2026-05-21T16:46:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:46:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), a subword tokenization method that directly optimizes compression under a new recursive inference procedure. ToaST greedily splits each pretoken into a full binary tree using precomputed byte n-gram counts, independent of any vocabulary. Given a vocabulary, inference recursively descends each split tree and emits the first in-vocabulary node reached on each path. Vocabulary selection is formulated as an Integer Program (IP) that minimizes the total token count over all split trees under this inference procedure. The Linear Programming (LP) relaxation is near-integral in practice, yielding provably near-optimal vocabularies, with training time empirically scaling quadratically in the number of split trees. On English text, ToaST reduces token counts by more than 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at vocabulary sizes of 40,960 and above, reducing the number of inference tokens for models using this tokenizer, thus extending the effective context length. ToaST also uses common single-byte tokens less frequently than these baselines, leading to a substantial improvement in Renyi efficiency. In experiments training 1.5B parameter language models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%--7.6%, with significance for two of three, and scoring best on 13 of 22 individual tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于分裂树的分词方法
我们提出了基于分裂树的分词方法（ToaST），这是一种直接在新的递归推理过程中优化压缩的子词分词方法。ToaST 利用预计算的字节 n-gram 统计，独立于任何词汇表，贪婪地将每个预分词拆分为完整的二叉树。给定一个词汇表，推理过程会递归遍历每个分裂树，并在每条路径上输出第一个命中词汇表的节点。词汇表选择被建模为一个整数规划（IP）问题，旨在在该推理过程中最小化所有分裂树的总词数。其线性规划（LP）松弛在实践中接近整数解，从而得到可证明的近最优词汇表，且训练时间经验上随分裂树数量的平方增长。在英文文本上，当词汇表大小达到 40,960 及以上时，ToaST 相比 BPE、WordPiece 和 UnigramLM 分词数减少超过 11%，从而减少模型使用该分词器时的推理词数，延长有效上下文长度。此外，ToaST 比这些基线方法更少使用常见的单字节词元，显著提升了 Renyi 效率。在训练 1.5B 参数语言模型的实验中，ToaST 达到最高的 CORE 分数，相比基线方法高出 2.6% 至 7.6%，其中三项中有两项具有统计显著性，并在 22 项任务中的 13 项上表现最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more efficient subword tokenization that directly optimizes compression, the authors propose Tokenization with Split Trees (ToaST), which constructs a full binary tree for each pretoken using precomputed byte n-gram statistics, independent of vocabulary. During inference, the tree is traversed recursively to emit the first in-vocabulary node on each path, and vocabulary selection is modeled as an Integer Program minimizing total token count across all trees. The Linear Programming relaxation is near-integral, enabling near-optimal vocabularies with quadratic training time scaling. Experiments show ToaST reduces token counts by over 11% compared to BPE, WordPiece, and UnigramLM at 40,960+ vocabulary sizes, extends effective context length, and reduces single-byte token usage, improving Renyi efficiency. When training 1.5B parameter models, ToaST achieves the highest CORE score, outperforming baselines by 2.6%–7.6% on 13 of 22 tasks.
为提升子词分词的压缩效率，Tokenization with Split Trees（ToaST）提出一种方法，通过预计算的字节n-gram计数，独立于词汇表贪心地将文本拆分为满二叉树。推理时递归遍历每棵树，输出每条路径上首次遇到的词汇表内节点，而词汇表选择则建模为整数规划问题，以最小化该推理过程下的总词数。其线性规划松弛在实践中接近整数解，可获得近最优词汇表，训练时间随树数量平方增长。实验表明，当词汇表规模达40,960以上时，ToaST较BPE、WordPiece和UnigramLM减少超11%的词数，延长有效上下文长度，减少单字节词使用，提升Renyi效率。在训练1.5B参数语言模型时，ToaST获得最高CORE得分，较基线提升2.6%–7.6%，在22项任务中13项表现最佳。
Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filters for Probabilistic Neural Network Dynamics
Authors: Bernd Frauenknecht, Lukas Kesper, Daniel Mayfrank, Henrik Hose, Sebastian Trimpe
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:45:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predictive safety filters (PSFs) leverage model predictive control to enforce constraint satisfaction during deep reinforcement learning (RL) exploration, yet their reliance on first-principles models or Gaussian processes limits scalability and broader applicability. Meanwhile, model-based RL (MBRL) methods routinely employ probabilistic ensemble (PE) neural networks to capture complex, high-dimensional dynamics from data with minimal prior knowledge. However, existing attempts to integrate PEs into PSFs lack rigorous uncertainty quantification. We introduce the Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filter (UPSi), a PSF that provides rigorous safety predictions using PE dynamics models by formulating future outcomes as reachable sets. UPSi introduces an explicit certainty constraint that prevents model exploitation and integrates seamlessly into common MBRL frameworks. We evaluate UPSi within Dyna-style MBRL on standard safe RL benchmarks and report substantial improvements in exploration safety over prior neural network PSFs while maintaining performance on par with standard MBRL. UPSi bridges the gap between the scalability and generality of modern MBRL and the safety guarantees of predictive safety filters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向概率神经网络动力学的不确定性感知预测安全滤波器
预测安全滤波器（PSF）利用模型预测控制在深度强化学习探索过程中确保约束满足，但其依赖于第一性原理模型或高斯过程，限制了可扩展性和广泛应用。同时，基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）方法通常采用概率集成（PE）神经网络，以在数据驱动下捕捉复杂高维动力学并最小化先验知识。然而，现有将PE集成到PSF的尝试缺乏严谨的不确定性量化。我们提出不确定性感知预测安全滤波器（UPSi），通过将未来结果建模为可达集，利用PE动力学模型提供严谨的安全预测。UPSi引入显式置信度约束，防止模型被过度利用，并无缝集成到常见MBRL框架中。我们在Dyna式MBRL框架下评估UPSi于标准安全强化学习基准，结果表明其在探索安全性上显著优于现有神经网络PSF，同时保持与标准MBRL相当的性能。UPSi弥合了现代MBRL的可扩展性与通用性与预测安全滤波器的安全保证之间的鸿沟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and broadly applicable safety guarantees in deep reinforcement learning, this work introduces UPSi, an uncertainty-aware predictive safety filter that integrates probabilistic ensemble neural networks into safety-critical control. UPSi formulates future system states as reachable sets and enforces explicit certainty constraints to prevent unsafe model exploitation, enabling seamless compatibility with common model-based RL frameworks. Experimental results on standard safe RL benchmarks demonstrate that UPSi significantly improves exploration safety compared to prior neural network-based PSFs while preserving performance levels comparable to standard MBRL methods.
为提升深度强化学习中可扩展且普适的安全保障，本文提出UPSi，一种结合概率神经网络动态模型与模型预测控制框架的不确定性感知预测安全滤波器。UPSi将未来系统状态建模为可达集，并引入显式置信度约束以防止模型误用，从而无缝兼容主流模型基强化学习流程。实验结果表明，UPSi在标准安全强化学习基准上显著提升探索安全性，同时保持与标准模型基强化学习相当的性能水平。
Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals
Authors: Shuo Yang, Jinda Lu, Chiyu Ma, Kexin Huang, Haoming Meng, Qihui Zhang, Yuyang Liu, Bolin Ding, Guoyin Wang, Li Yuan, Jingren Zhou
First: 2026-05-21T16:45:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:45:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：裁剪瓶颈：通过近边界信号的随机恢复稳定RLVR
可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）已成为扩展大语言模型推理能力的核心范式，但其优化常面临训练不稳定和收敛不佳的问题。通过对基于裁剪的GRPO类目标进行系统性剖析，我们发现硬裁剪引发的刚性裁剪决策是所研究RLVR设置中的关键实践瓶颈。具体而言，我们的分析表明，具有信息量的信号可能位于略高于裁剪阈值的近边界区域，而标准硬裁剪规则会丢弃这些信号。值得注意的是，一旦识别出该瓶颈，即使在边界处进行简单的随机扰动，也能恢复显著的性能增益。基于此发现，我们提出近边界随机救援（NSR），这是一种最小化、即插即用的修改方法，通过随机保留略超出边界的标记以恢复丢失的信号。虽然NSR通过随机采样可被解释为在期望意义上诱导隐式梯度衰减，但我们的消融实验表明，其随机、局部边界救援机制比确定性梯度衰减更有效。NSR在7B至30B不同规模模型及稠密与MoE架构上经大量实验验证，作为即插即用方案，显著提升了训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线方法之上持续带来性能增益。

Motivated by the training instability and suboptimal convergence in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), this work identifies hard clipping as a key bottleneck that discards informative signals near the clipping boundary. The authors propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. Experimental results across 7B to 30B models and both dense and MoE architectures show NSR significantly improves training stability and consistently outperforms strong baselines like DAPO and GSPO.
为解决RLVR训练中不稳定和收敛不佳的问题，本文发现硬裁剪会丢弃边界附近含信息量的信号，成为关键瓶颈。作者提出近边界随机救援（NSR），一种可插拔的最小修改方案，通过随机保留略超边界令牌以恢复丢失信号。在7B至30B规模及密集与MoE架构的广泛实验中，NSR显著提升训练稳定性，持续优于DAPO和GSPO等强基线，其随机边界救援机制比确定性梯度衰减更有效。
Shallow ReLU$^s$ Networks in $L^p$-Type and Sobolev Spaces: Approximation and Path-Norm Controlled Generalization
Authors: Weizhao Li, Fanghui Liu, Lei Shi
First: 2026-05-18T14:27:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:40:00+00:00
Comments: 42 pages, 1 figure. Authors are listed in alphabetical order and contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study approximation by shallow ReLU$^s$ networks, $σ_s(t)=\max{0,t}^s$, and the generalization behavior of such networks under $\ell_1$ path-norm control. For the $L^p$-type integral spaces $\widetilde{\mathcal{F}}_{p,τ_d,s}$, $1\le p\le2$, we establish approximation bounds for shallow networks using spherical harmonic analysis. In particular, when the parameter measure is the uniform measure $τ_d$ and $p<p^*=(2d+2)/(d+3)$, we obtain the rate $O(m^{-1/2-d(2-p)/(2d(2-p)+2p(2s+d+1))}\log^{3/2}m)$, which improves the corresponding random-feature rate. We also derive approximation rates for Sobolev spaces $W^{α,p}$ in the range $1\le p<2$ by embedding them into spectral Barron spaces. Finally, for nonparametric regression with sub-Gaussian noise, we prove minimax-optimal generalization bounds for path-norm-regularized shallow ReLU$^s$ networks over Barron and Sobolev spaces, with matching lower bounds up to logarithmic factors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：浅层ReLU^s网络在L^p型与Sobolev空间中的逼近与路径范数控制的泛化行为
我们研究浅层ReLU^s网络（σ_s(t)=max{0,t}^s）在L^p型积分空间~F_{p,τ_d,s}（1≤p≤2）中的逼近能力，以及在ℓ_1路径范数控制下的泛化行为。利用球面调和分析，我们建立了浅层网络的逼近界；特别地，当参数测度为均匀测度τ_d且p<p^*=(2d+2)/(d+3)时，得到逼近率O(m^{-1/2-d(2-p)/(2d(2-p)+2p(2s+d+1))}log^{3/2}m)，优于相应的随机特征方法。此外，通过嵌入谱Barron空间，我们推导了Sobolev空间W^{α,p}（1≤p<2）中的逼近率。最后，对于次高斯噪声下的非参数回归问题，我们证明了路径范数正则化浅层ReLU^s网络在Barron与Sobolev空间中的最小最大最优泛化界，并给出至对数因子匹配的下界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the approximation and generalization capabilities of shallow ReLU^s networks under path-norm regularization, the paper analyzes their performance in L^p-type and Sobolev spaces. Using spherical harmonic analysis, it derives approximation rates for L^p spaces with uniform measure, achieving improved rates over random-feature methods when p < p*. It further embeds Sobolev spaces into spectral Barron spaces to obtain approximation bounds, and proves minimax-optimal generalization bounds for nonparametric regression under sub-Gaussian noise, with matching lower bounds up to logarithmic factors.
为理解浅层ReLU^s网络在路径范数约束下的逼近与泛化能力，本文研究其在L^p型与Sobolev空间中的表现。通过球面调和分析，推导出浅层网络在L^p空间中的逼近界，当p < p*且采用均匀测度时，其速率优于随机特征方法。进一步将Sobolev空间嵌入谱Barron空间，获得1 ≤ p < 2范围内的逼近率。针对子高斯噪声下的非参数回归，证明了路径范数正则化浅层网络在Barron与Sobolev空间中的最小最大最优泛化界，并在对数因子内匹配下界。
Cross-Domain Human Action Recognition from Multiview Motion and Textual Descriptions
Authors: Yannick Porto, Renato Martins, Thomas Chalumeau, Cedric Demonceaux
First: 2026-05-21T16:39:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:39:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICPR 2026. Code and trained models available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/OrientationAware-HAR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robustness to domain changes is a key capability for effective deployment of human action recognition systems in real-world scenarios, where action categories at inference can present important domain shifts or even unseen actions from training. In this context, improving the recognition capabilities of Zero-Shot Action Recognition models (ZSAR), without requiring strong annotation efforts, remains a central challenge. Most ZSAR approaches assume that actions are observed under geometric conditions similar to those seen during training. In practice, variations in human body orientation and camera viewpoint add a significant domain gap in ZSAR, substantially limiting generalization to novel action-motion combinations. In this context, this paper presents a novel orientation-aware action recognition approach with improved cross-domain capabilities. Our approach combines motion cues of multiple camera viewpoints and text descriptions of human actions in the training phase. We present a new orientation-aware motion encoding network to learn different motion features, and adapt a specific orientation-aware text prompt to match the corresponding features at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves ZSAR performance across different recognition benchmarks, outperforming recent state-of-the-art zero-shot approaches on NTU-RGB+D, BABEL, NW-UCLA, and on two surveillance datasets. In addition, the learned representations exhibit strong transfer learning capabilities, yielding competitive performance on both cross-domain and same-domain recognition of seen actions. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/OrientationAware-HAR

Improving Viewpoint-Invariance and Temporal Consistency for Action Detection
Authors: Yannick Porto, Renato Martins, Thomas Chalumeau, Cedric Demonceaux
First: 2026-05-21T16:38:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:38:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICIP 2026. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/HydraView-TAD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Viewpoint change invariance and action temporal consistency are critical aspects for the effective deployment of human action detection of untrimmed videos. Existing appearance-based video detection methods often struggle with limited viewpoint diversity during training, while motion-based detection approaches frequently fail to model fine-grained temporal relationships across consecutive motion windows. This paper introduces a novel two-stage action detection approach designed to improve both view-invariance and global temporal coherence properties. In the first stage, we extract motion features from augmented virtual viewpoints, solely used at training. Then, the second stage introduces a new view-invariant, multi-scale temporal encoder based on selective state-space sequence modelling to aggregate information across viewpoints and time scales. Experiments on PKU-MMD and BABEL benchmarks demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in all considered splits. Code and trained models are available at: https://icb-vision-ai.github.io/HydraView-TAD
中文标题/摘要
标题：提升动作检测中的视角不变性与时间一致性

Evaluated on PKU-MMD and BABEL datasets, the approach consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods across all splits, demonstrating strong generalization and robustness to viewpoint changes and temporal variations.
该论文针对未剪辑视频中视角变化和动作时序不一致的问题，提出一种两阶段方法。第一阶段在训练时从增强的虚拟视角提取运动特征以提升视角不变性，第二阶段引入基于选择性状态空间建模的多尺度时序编码器，以跨视角和时间尺度聚合细粒度时序信息。在PKU-MMD和BABEL数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有评估划分中均显著优于现有方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性和时序一致性。
Scout-Assisted Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams under Partially Known Environments
Authors: Hoang-Dung Bui, Abhish Khanal, Raihan Islam Arnob, Gregory J. Stein
First: 2026-05-21T16:36:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:36:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous robot teams navigating partially known environments face costly backtracking when ground robots encounter blocked roads that are only revealed upon physical traversal. We address this with Scout-Assisted Planning, a heterogeneous planning framework in which scouting Unmanned Aerial Vehicles proactively gather environmental information to improve Unmanned Ground Vehicle navigation. To focus scouting on the most consequential edges, we propose Information Gain-based Action Pruning, which scores candidate scouting actions by their expected impact on ground robot behavior. Since exact Information Gain-based Action Pruning computation is prohibitively expensive, we develop a Graph Neural Network based model that predicts information gain values directly from graph structure and belief state, reducing planning time to real-time levels without sacrificing solution quality. Experiments across three environment types show that SAP with Information Gain Action Pruning reduces ground robot travel cost by 31.9--37.7% over the Canadian Traveler Problem baseline, and outperforms proximity-based scouting guidance by an additional 8--14%, confirming that principled information-gain-guided scouting is both more effective and computationally feasible for real-world deployment
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于侦察机辅助的异构机器人团队在部分已知环境中的规划
自主机器人团队在部分已知环境中导航时，地面机器人遇到仅在物理穿越后才显现的阻塞道路，会导致昂贵的回溯。我们提出侦察机辅助规划（Scout-Assisted Planning），这是一种异构规划框架，其中侦察无人机主动收集环境信息以提升地面机器人导航效果。为聚焦于最具影响的边，我们提出基于信息增益的动作剪枝方法，通过评估候选侦察动作对地面机器人行为的预期影响进行评分。由于基于信息增益的动作剪枝计算成本过高，我们开发了一种基于图神经网络的模型，直接从图结构和信念状态预测信息增益值，将规划时间降至实时水平，且不牺牲解决方案质量。在三种环境类型中的实验表明，结合信息增益动作剪枝的SAP相比加拿大旅行者问题基线可降低地面机器人旅行成本31.9%至37.7%，并比基于邻近性的侦察引导方法进一步提升8%至14%，证实了基于信息增益的侦察策略在实际部署中既更有效又具备计算可行性。
Summary / 总结
To reduce costly backtracking in heterogeneous robot teams navigating partially known environments, this work introduces Scout-Assisted Planning, a framework where aerial scouts proactively gather environmental data to enhance ground robot navigation. It employs Information Gain-based Action Pruning to prioritize scouting actions by their expected impact on ground robot behavior, and uses a Graph Neural Network to approximate information gain efficiently, enabling real-time planning without quality loss. Experiments across three environments show that this approach cuts ground robot travel cost by 31.9–37.7% compared to the Canadian Traveler Problem baseline and outperforms proximity-based scouting by an additional 8–14%.
为降低部分已知环境中地面机器人因路径受阻而回溯的成本，该研究提出Scout-Assisted Planning（SAP）框架，利用无人机主动采集环境信息辅助地面机器人导航。通过基于信息增益的动作剪枝策略优先选择对地面机器人行为影响最大的侦察动作，并采用图神经网络高效近似信息增益值，实现实时规划。在三种环境中的实验表明，SAP较加拿大旅行者问题基线降低地面机器人行驶成本31.9–37.7%，相较基于距离的侦察策略进一步提升8–14%，验证了该方法在有效性与计算可行性上的优势。
Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning
Authors: Johannes Hirn
First: 2026-05-21T16:36:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:36:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful. We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first. In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：后验坍缩作为自动谱剪枝
我们证明，β-VAE中的后验坍缩实现了自动谱剪枝。若某一潜在模式对重建的贡献低于由β设定的阈值，则该模式会坍缩。不同β值下的平衡解揭示了潜在模式从最不有用到最有利的逐步坍缩过程。 我们通过朗道稳定性分析，将此现象推导为损失函数的后果。我们定义了一个与潜在变量重标度无关的序参量，用于排序活跃的潜在模式，其坍缩阈值可识别应优先检查的有效变量。 在线性高斯情况下，坍缩谱、效用谱和标准化PCA谱三者一致，每个坍缩遵循平均场定律。我们在WorldClim数据集上验证了这些预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding posterior collapse in β-VAEs as a mechanism for automatic feature selection, the authors show that latent modes collapse when their reconstruction contribution falls below a β-determined threshold, revealing a cascade of decoupling from least to most useful features. Using a Landau stability analysis of the loss function, they derive a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active modes and identifies collapse thresholds for prioritizing effective variables. In linear Gaussian settings, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum align, and each collapse follows a mean-field law, validated through experiments on the WorldClim dataset.
该研究旨在揭示β-VAE中后验坍缩现象本质上是一种自动谱剪枝机制，通过损失函数的朗道稳定性分析推导出：当潜在变量对重建的贡献低于β设定的阈值时，该变量即发生坍缩。作者提出一种对潜在变量重缩放不变的序参量，用于排序活跃变量，其坍缩阈值可指导优先检查哪些有效变量。在线性高斯情形下，坍缩谱、效用谱与标准化PCA谱三者一致，且每个坍缩遵循平均场定律，这一理论在WorldClim数据集上得到实验验证。
U-CECE: A Universal Multi-Resolution Framework for Conceptual Counterfactual Explanations
Authors: Angeliki Dimitriou, Nikolaos Chaidos, Maria Lymperaiou, Giorgos Filandrianos, Giorgos Stamou
First: 2026-04-09T14:30:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:35:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As AI models grow more complex, explainability is essential for building trust, yet concept-based counterfactual methods still face a trade-off between expressivity and efficiency. Representing underlying concepts as atomic sets is fast but misses relational context, whereas full graph representations are more faithful but require solving the NP-hard Graph Edit Distance (GED) problem. We propose U-CECE, a unified, model-agnostic multi-resolution framework for conceptual counterfactual explanations that adapts to data regime and compute budget. U-CECE spans three levels of expressivity: atomic concepts for broad explanations, relational sets-of-sets for simple interactions, and structural graphs for full semantic structure. At the structural level, both a precision-oriented transductive mode based on supervised Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and a scalable inductive mode based on unsupervised graph autoencoders (GAEs) are supported. Experiments on the structurally divergent CUB and Visual Genome datasets characterize the efficiency-expressivity trade-off across levels, while human surveys and LVLM-based evaluation show that the retrieved structural counterfactuals are semantically equivalent to, and often preferred over, exact GED-based ground-truth explanations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：U-CECE：一种通用的多分辨率概念反事实解释框架
随着AI模型日益复杂，可解释性对于建立信任至关重要，但基于概念的反事实方法仍面临表达能力与效率之间的权衡。将底层概念表示为原子集合虽快速但忽略关系上下文，而完整图表示虽更忠实却需解决NP难的图编辑距离（GED）问题。我们提出U-CECE，一种统一、模型无关的多分辨率概念反事实解释框架，可根据数据分布和计算预算自适应调整。U-CECE涵盖三层表达能力：原子概念用于宽泛解释，关系集合的集合用于简单交互，结构图用于完整语义结构。在结构层面，支持基于监督图神经网络（GNNs）的精度导向的转导模式，以及基于无监督图自编码器（GAEs）的可扩展归纳模式。在结构差异显著的CUB和视觉基因组数据集上的实验刻画了各层级的效率-表达能力权衡，而人工调查和基于LVLM的评估表明，检索到的结构反事实解释在语义上等价于甚至优于基于精确GED的真值解释。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to balance expressivity and efficiency in explaining complex AI models, U-CECE introduces a multi-resolution framework that adapts to data and compute constraints. It offers three levels of conceptual representation: atomic sets, relational sets-of-sets, and structural graphs, each progressively more expressive but computationally demanding. The framework supports both transductive GNN-based and inductive GAE-based methods for structural explanations, enabling scalable generation of counterfactuals. Experiments on CUB and Visual Genome demonstrate that U-CECE achieves a favorable trade-off between efficiency and semantic fidelity, with human and LVLM evaluations confirming that its structural counterfactuals are often preferred over exact GED-based ground truths.
随着AI模型日益复杂，可解释性对建立信任至关重要，但基于概念的反事实方法在表达力与效率之间存在权衡。U-CECE提出一种多分辨率框架，根据数据和计算资源自适应调整，支持原子概念、关系集合和结构图三层表达。在结构层，采用监督GNN实现高精度，无监督GAE实现可扩展性。在CUB和Visual Genome数据集上的实验表明，U-CECE在效率与表达力间取得良好平衡，且人工与LVLM评估显示其结构反事实解释在语义上等价，且常优于基于GED的精确解释。
A strongly annotated passive acoustic dataset for tropical bird monitoring
Authors: Daniela Ruiz, Juan Sebastián Ulloa, Zhongqi Miao, Nicolás Betancourt, Maria Paula Toro-Gómez, Andrés Hernández, Bruno Demuro, Eliana Barona-Cortés, Angela Mendoza-Henao, Andrés Sierra-Ricaurte, Sebastián Pérez-Peña, Rahul Dodhia, Pablo Arbeláez, Juan M. Lavista Ferres
First: 2026-05-20T00:36:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:34:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Passive acoustic monitoring enables continuous, non-invasive biodiversity assessment across diverse ecosystems. The scale of these datasets has driven the adoption of machine learning, with supervised approaches showing strong performance. However, supervised methods require time-resolved annotated datasets, which remain scarce, especially in complex tropical soundscapes. We present PteroSet, a curated dataset of strongly annotated Neotropical bird vocalizations recorded in Puerto Asis (Putumayo) and Pivijay (Magdalena), Colombia, between 2023 and 2025. The dataset comprises 563 recordings (73.62 h) and 15,372 time-frequency annotations, including 6,702 events identified to the species level across 168 species. We release the annotations in a COCO-inspired JSON schema that unifies audio files, taxonomic categories, and labels for machine learning workflows. Beyond providing annotated data, PteroSet serves as a realistic benchmark that highlights key characteristics of tropical soundscapes, including acoustic co-occurrence and domain shift across recording sites. We provide a deep learning baseline for binary bird detection, demonstrating PteroSet's usability and the challenges it presents.

Motivated by the need for annotated data to train machine learning models for tropical bird monitoring, this study introduces PteroSet, a strongly annotated passive acoustic dataset collected in Colombia between 2023 and 2025.
