A Short and Unified Convergence Analysis of the SAG, SAGA, and IAG Algorithms
Authors: Feng Zhu, Robert W. Heath, Aritra Mitra
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-02-05T04:57:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:32:29+00:00
Comments: To appear at the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic variance-reduced algorithms such as Stochastic Average Gradient (SAG) and SAGA, and their deterministic counterparts like the Incremental Aggregated Gradient (IAG) method, have been extensively studied in large-scale machine learning. Despite their popularity, existing analyses for these algorithms are disparate, relying on different proof techniques tailored to each method. Furthermore, the original proof of SAG is known to be notoriously involved, requiring computer-aided analysis. Focusing on finite-sum optimization with smooth and strongly convex objective functions, our main contribution is to develop a single unified convergence analysis that applies to all three algorithms: SAG, SAGA, and IAG. Our analysis features two key steps: (i) establishing a bound on delays due to stochastic sub-sampling using simple concentration tools, and (ii) carefully designing a novel Lyapunov function that accounts for such delays. The resulting proof is short and modular, providing the first high-probability bounds for SAG and SAGA that can be seamlessly extended to non-convex objectives and Markov sampling. As an immediate byproduct of our new analysis technique, we obtain the best known rates for the IAG algorithm, significantly improving upon prior bounds.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAG、SAGA 和 IAG 算法的简洁统一收敛性分析
随机方差缩减算法（如随机平均梯度 SAG 和 SAGA）及其确定性对应方法（如增量聚合梯度 IAG）在大规模机器学习中已被广泛研究。尽管这些算法广受欢迎，但现有分析方法各异，依赖于针对每种方法的特定证明技术。此外，SAG 的原始证明以复杂著称，需借助计算机辅助分析。本文聚焦于光滑且强凸目标函数的有限和优化问题，主要贡献是为 SAG、SAGA 和 IAG 三者建立统一的收敛性分析。该分析包含两个关键步骤：(i) 使用简单的集中不等式工具对随机子采样引起的延迟进行界估计；(ii) 精心设计一种新型李雅普诺夫函数以考虑此类延迟。所得证明简洁且模块化，首次为 SAG 和 SAGA 提供了高概率界，且可无缝扩展至非凸目标和马尔可夫采样。作为新分析技术的直接副产品，我们获得了 IAG 算法目前最优的收敛速率，显著优于先前结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmented and complex existing analyses of SAG, SAGA, and IAG algorithms, this paper presents a unified convergence analysis applicable to all three methods under finite-sum smooth and strongly convex optimization. The approach uses two key steps: bounding stochastic sub-sampling delays via concentration inequalities and constructing a novel Lyapunov function that incorporates these delays. The resulting proof is concise and modular, yielding the first high-probability convergence bounds for SAG and SAGA, and enabling seamless extension to non-convex and Markov sampling settings. As a direct outcome, the analysis also recovers improved convergence rates for IAG, surpassing prior theoretical guarantees.
为解决SAG、SAGA和IAG算法虽结构相似但缺乏统一分析框架的问题，本文提出了一种简洁且模块化的收敛性分析方法，适用于三者。该方法通过简单集中不等式处理随机子采样延迟，并设计了一种新Lyapunov函数以显式建模延迟，从而简化证明过程。实验结果表明，该统一分析首次为SAG和SAGA提供了高概率收敛保证，同时显著改进了IAG的最佳收敛速率，适用于凸与非凸场景。
Optimus: A Robust Defense Framework for Mitigating Toxicity while Fine-Tuning Conversational AI
Authors: Aravind Cheruvu, Shravya Kanchi, Sifat Muhammad Abdullah, Nicholas Ka-Shing Kong, Daphne Yao, Murtuza Jadliwala, Bimal Viswanath
First: 2025-07-08T04:40:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:31:21+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACM CODASPY 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Customizing Large Language Models (LLMs) on untrusted datasets poses severe risks of injecting toxic behaviors. In this work, we introduce Optimus, a novel defense framework designed to mitigate fine-tuning harms while preserving conversational utility. Unlike existing defenses that rely heavily on precise toxicity detection or restrictive filtering, Optimus addresses the critical challenge of ensuring robust mitigation even when toxicity classifiers are imperfect or biased. Optimus integrates a training-free toxicity classification scheme that repurposes the safety alignment of commodity LLMs, and employs a dual-strategy alignment process combining synthetic "healing data" with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to efficiently steer models toward safety. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Optimus mitigates toxicity even when relying on extremely biased classifiers (with up to 85% degradation in Recall). Optimus outperforms the state-of-the-art defense StarDSS and exhibits strong resilience against adaptive adversarial and jailbreak attacks. Our source code and datasets are available at https://github.com/secml-lab-vt/Optimus
中文标题/摘要
标题：Optimus：一种在微调对话式AI时缓解毒性行为的稳健防御框架
在不可信数据集上定制大型语言模型（LLMs）可能严重引入毒性行为。本文提出Optimus，一种新颖的防御框架，旨在缓解微调带来的危害同时保留对话实用性。与现有依赖精确毒性检测或严格过滤的防御方法不同，Optimus解决了当毒性分类器不完美或存在偏见时仍能稳健缓解的关键挑战。Optimus整合了一种无需训练的毒性分类方案，重用商用LLMs的安全对齐能力，并采用合成“修复数据”与直接偏好优化（DPO）相结合的双策略对齐过程，高效引导模型向安全方向发展。大量评估表明，即使依赖极度偏见的分类器（召回率下降高达85%），Optimus仍能有效缓解毒性。Optimus优于当前最先进的防御方法StarDSS，并对自适应对抗攻击和越狱攻击表现出强韧性。源代码和数据集可在https://github.com/secml-lab-vt/Optimus获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of injecting toxic behaviors when fine-tuning LLMs on untrusted data, Optimus introduces a defense framework that mitigates toxicity without requiring precise toxicity classifiers. It leverages a training-free toxicity classification method based on safety alignment from commodity LLMs and combines synthetic healing data with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to steer models toward safer outputs. Experimental results show Optimus effectively reduces toxicity even with severely biased classifiers (up to 85% Recall degradation) and outperforms StarDSS, while remaining resilient to adaptive adversarial and jailbreak attacks.
为应对在未受信任数据上微调大语言模型时可能引入毒性行为的风险，Optimus提出了一种无需依赖精确毒性检测器的防御框架。该框架利用商品化LLM的安全对齐能力构建无训练毒性分类方案，并结合合成修复数据与直接偏好优化（DPO）引导模型输出安全响应。实验表明，即使在毒性分类器严重失准（召回率下降85%）的情况下，Optimus仍能有效降低毒性，并优于当前最优方法StarDSS，且对自适应对抗攻击和越狱攻击具有较强鲁棒性。
Ray-Aware Pointer Memory with Adaptive Updates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction
Authors: Feifei Li, Qi Song, Chi Zhang, Rui Huang
First: 2026-05-07T06:42:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:29:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dense 3D reconstruction from continuous image streams requires both accurate geometric aggregation and stable long-term memory management. Recent feed-forward reconstruction frameworks integrate observations through persistent memory representations, yet most rely primarily on appearance-based similarity when updating memory. Such appearance-driven integration often leads to redundant accumulation of observations and unstable geometry when viewpoint changes occur. In this work, we propose a ray-aware pointer memory for streaming 3D reconstruction that explicitly models both spatial location and viewing direction within a unified memory representation. Each memory pointer stores its 3D position, associated ray direction, and feature embedding, allowing the system to reason jointly about geometric proximity and viewpoint consistency. Based on this representation, we introduce an adaptive pointer update strategy that replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with a retain-or-replace mechanism. Instead of averaging nearby observations, the system selectively retains informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, preserving distinctive geometric structures while maintaining bounded memory growth. Furthermore, the joint reasoning over spatial distance and ray-direction discrepancy enables the system to distinguish between local redundancy, novel observations, and potential loop revisits in a unified manner. When loop candidates are detected, pose refinement is triggered to enforce global geometric consistency across the reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed ray-aware memory design significantly improves long-term reconstruction stability and camera pose accuracy while maintaining efficient streaming inference. Our approach provides a principled framework for scalable and drift-resistant online 3D reconstruction from image streams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向流式3D重建的射线感知指针内存与自适应更新
从连续图像流中进行密集3D重建需要精确的几何聚合与稳定的长期记忆管理。近期的前馈重建框架通过持久化记忆表示整合观测结果，但多数方法主要依赖外观相似性更新记忆。此类外观驱动的整合常导致观测冗余累积，且在视角变化时几何结构不稳定。本文提出一种射线感知指针内存，统一建模空间位置与观察方向。每个记忆指针存储3D位置、关联射线方向及特征嵌入，使系统可联合推理几何邻近性与视角一致性。基于此表示，我们引入自适应指针更新策略，以保留或替换机制替代传统融合式记忆压缩。系统不再平均邻近观测，而是选择性保留信息性指针并丢弃冗余指针，既保留独特几何结构，又控制内存增长。此外，联合空间距离与射线方向差异的推理使系统能统一区分局部冗余、新观测与潜在闭环重访。检测到闭环候选时，触发位姿优化以强制重建全局几何一致性。大量实验表明，所提射线感知内存设计显著提升长期重建稳定性与相机位姿精度，同时保持高效流式推理。本方法为从图像流中实现可扩展且抗漂移的在线3D重建提供了一种原理性框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability in long-term 3D reconstruction from image streams due to appearance-based memory updates, this work introduces a ray-aware pointer memory that jointly encodes 3D position and ray direction for each memory entry, enabling geometric and viewpoint-aware reasoning. The method replaces traditional fusion-based memory compression with an adaptive retain-or-replace mechanism that selectively preserves informative pointers while discarding redundant ones, thus maintaining bounded memory growth and preserving geometric structure. Experimental results show improved long-term reconstruction stability and camera pose accuracy compared to baseline methods, with the system also detecting loop candidates to trigger pose refinement for global consistency.
为了解决图像流中长期3D重建因外观驱动记忆更新导致的几何不稳定问题，本文提出一种射线感知的指针记忆机制，每个指针同时编码3D位置与观测方向。系统采用自适应更新策略，根据空间距离与射线方向一致性选择性保留或替换指针，避免冗余累积并保留关键几何结构。实验表明，该方法显著提升重建稳定性与相机位姿精度，同时保持内存增长可控，并能有效检测闭环与优化全局几何一致性。
The efficiency-gain illusion: People underestimate the rate of AI use and overestimate its benefits on simple tasks
Authors: Sunny Yu, Myra Cheng, Ahmad Jabbar, Ilia Sucholutsky, Katherine M. Collins, Dan Jurafsky, Robert D. Hawkins
First: 2026-05-21T16:28:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:28:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
People are increasingly turning to AI assistance for simple tasks, e.g., arithmetic, spell-check, and answering simple questions. But does AI assistance actually save users time and effort? We investigate people's propensity to use AI for cognitively simple tasks and assess whether their reliance is well-calibrated. Across three pre-registered user studies (N = 2691), we find that people frequently choose to use AI even when doing so is inefficient (i.e. provides no meaningful time or effort savings). We identify systematic miscalibration at two levels: (1) a self-estimate miscalibration where people on average believe that they are using AI less than they actually are, and (2) efficiency-gain illusions where people overestimate how much time and effort savings AI use affords. We also identify a session-level carryover effect where a participant's prior AI use leads to further AI adoption and entrenches their miscalibration about time savings. Our results shed light on the mechanisms and biases underlying people's choice of whether to use AI as well as the risk of an overreliance feedback loop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：效率增益的错觉：人们低估了AI的使用率，高估了其在简单任务中的益处
人们越来越多地寻求AI辅助完成简单任务，例如算术、拼写检查和回答简单问题。但AI辅助是否真的节省用户的时间和精力？我们研究了人们在认知简单任务中使用AI的倾向，并评估其依赖程度是否合理。在三项预注册用户研究（N=2691）中，我们发现人们经常选择使用AI，即使这样做并不高效（即未能带来有意义的时间或精力节省）。我们识别出两个层面的系统性误校准：（1）自我估计误校准，即人们平均认为自己使用AI的频率低于实际频率；（2）效率增益错觉，即人们高估了AI使用所能带来的时间和精力节省。我们还发现了一个会话级的延续效应：参与者先前的AI使用会进一步促进其AI采纳，并巩固其对时间节省的误判。我们的研究揭示了人们选择是否使用AI背后的机制和偏见，以及过度依赖的反馈循环风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing public use of AI for simple tasks like arithmetic or spell-check, this study investigates whether users accurately perceive AI’s efficiency benefits. Through three pre-registered experiments with 2,691 participants, researchers found that users frequently chose AI despite it offering no real time or effort savings, revealing two key miscalibrations: users underestimated their own AI usage and overestimated the efficiency gains AI provided. Additionally, prior AI use in one session led to increased adoption in subsequent sessions, reinforcing these misperceptions and creating a feedback loop that risks overreliance.
鉴于公众日益依赖AI完成简单任务（如算术或拼写检查），本研究旨在考察人们是否准确感知AI的效率优势。通过对2691名参与者进行三组预注册用户实验，研究发现用户频繁选择使用AI，即使它并未带来实际的时间或精力节省，揭示了两种系统性误判：人们低估了自己的AI使用频率，同时高估了AI带来的效率提升。此外，用户在当前会话中使用AI会促使后续更多使用，强化其对时间节省的误判，形成过度依赖的反馈循环。
ChronoVAE-HOPE: Beyond Attention -- A Next-Generation VAE Foundation Model for Specialized Time Series Classification
Authors: José Alberto Rodríguez, Luis Balderas, Miguel Lastra, Antonio Arauzo-Azofra, José M. Benítez
First: 2026-05-21T16:26:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:26:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) have become a new component of the state-of-the-art in general time series forecasting. However, adapting them to specialized classification tasks remains constrained by two interconnected challenges: the quadratic cost of standard attention mechanisms and the inability to disentangle the structural components underlying time series variability. This technical report introduces ChronoVAE-HOPE, a next-generation TSFM that reconciles massive generalization with structured latent representation for time series classification. The core of the proposal is a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) framework built upon the HOPE Block, which replaces quadratic attention with a dual-memory system: Titans modules for dynamic short-term retention and a Continuum Memory System (CMS) for the abstraction of long-term historical context. A key architectural novelty is the disentangled latent space, which factorizes representations into independent trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and separate decoder pathways. ChronoVAE-HOPE undergoes self-supervised pre-training on the Monash archive, combining a Masked Time Series Modeling (MTSM) auxiliary objective with a disentangled VAE reconstruction loss. The pre-trained encoder is subsequently frozen and used to generate fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on the UCR benchmark datasets. Empirical results demonstrate strong performance across diverse temporal domains, particularly in settings characterized by strict causal structure. ChronoVAE-HOPE establishes a robust and interpretable framework for the adaptation of foundation models to time series classification through structured generative representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChronoVAE-HOPE：超越注意力机制——面向专用时间序列分类的下一代VAE基础模型
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）已成为通用时间序列预测领域的最新组成部分。然而，将其适配于专用分类任务仍受限于两个相互关联的挑战：标准注意力机制的二次计算成本，以及无法解耦时间序列变异性背后的结构成分。本技术报告介绍了ChronoVAE-HOPE，一种下一代TSFM，它在实现大规模泛化的同时，为时间序列分类提供结构化的潜在表示。该方案的核心是一个基于HOPE模块的变分自编码器（VAE）框架，用双记忆系统替代二次注意力机制：Titan模块用于动态短期记忆保留，连续记忆系统（CMS）用于抽象长期历史上下文。关键架构创新是解耦的潜在空间，通过专用编码器头和独立解码路径，将表示分解为独立的趋势和季节性成分。ChronoVAE-HOPE在Monash数据集上进行自监督预训练，结合掩码时间序列建模（MTSM）辅助目标与解耦VAE重建损失。预训练编码器随后被冻结，用于在UCR基准数据集上生成固定长度嵌入以支持下游分类任务。实证结果表明，该模型在多种时间领域均表现优异，尤其在具有严格因果结构的场景中表现突出。ChronoVAE-HOPE通过结构化生成表示，为基础模型适配时间序列分类提供了一种稳健且可解释的框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of attention mechanisms and the lack of structural disentanglement in time series classification, ChronoVAE-HOPE introduces a VAE-based foundation model using the HOPE Block to replace quadratic attention with a dual-memory system for efficient short- and long-term retention. Its disentangled latent space separates trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder-decoder pathways, enabling interpretable representations. Pre-trained on the Monash archive using masked modeling and VAE reconstruction, the model generates fixed embeddings for downstream classification on UCR datasets, achieving strong performance across diverse temporal domains, especially under strict causal constraints.
ChronoVAE-HOPE 为时间序列分类任务设计，通过引入基于变分自编码器的架构，用双记忆系统（Titans模块处理短期动态，连续记忆系统处理长期上下文）替代传统注意力机制，并通过专用编码器和解码器路径将潜在表示分解为独立的趋势与季节性成分。模型在Monash数据集上通过掩码时间序列建模与解耦VAE重建损失进行自监督预训练，随后冻结编码器生成固定长度嵌入用于UCR基准分类任务，在多种时序领域表现优异，尤其在需严格因果结构的任务中效果突出。
UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models
Authors: Yiqiao Jin, Yiyang Wang, Lucheng Fu, Yijia Xiao, Yinyi Luo, Haoxin Liu, B. Aditya Prakash, Josiah Hester, Jindong Wang, Srijan Kumar
First: 2026-05-07T17:22:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:55+00:00
Comments: Website: https://unifiedsd.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/Ahren09/UniSD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniSD：面向大语言模型的统一自蒸馏框架
自蒸馏（SD）为无需依赖更强外部教师的大语言模型（LLMs）适配提供了有前景的路径。然而，在自回归LLMs中，SD仍具挑战性，因为自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性依赖任务，且合理推理仍可能提供不稳定或不可靠的监督。现有方法主要考察孤立的设计选择，其有效性、作用及交互关系尚不明确。本文提出UniSD，一个统一框架系统研究自蒸馏。UniSD整合互补机制，解决监督可靠性、表征对齐与训练稳定性问题，包括多教师共识、EMA教师稳定化、token级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪。在六项基准与三类模型家族的六种模型上，UniSD揭示了自蒸馏何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动性能提升、以及这些组件在不同任务中的交互方式。基于这些洞见，我们构建UniSDfull，一个整合互补组件的流水线，实现最强整体性能，相较基线模型提升+5.4分，相较最强基线提升+2.8分。广泛评估表明，自蒸馏是无需更强外部教师的高效LLM适配实用且可控方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to adapt large language models without external teachers, this paper introduces UniSD, a unified self-distillation framework that systematically integrates multiple mechanisms—multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping—to address supervision unreliability, representation misalignment, and training instability. Evaluated across six benchmarks and six models from three families, UniSD identifies when self-distillation outperforms static imitation, which components drive performance gains, and how they interact across tasks. The resulting UniSDfull pipeline, combining all effective components, achieves the best overall results, improving the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points, demonstrating self-distillation as a practical and steerable adaptation method.
为实现无需外部教师的大语言模型高效适配，本文提出UniSD统一自蒸馏框架，系统整合多教师一致性、EMA教师稳定化、词元级对比学习、特征匹配与发散裁剪等机制，分别解决监督信号不可靠、表征对齐偏差与训练不稳定等问题。在六个基准和三类模型的六种模型上评估发现，自蒸馏在特定场景下优于静态模仿，各组件对性能提升的贡献及交互关系清晰可辨。基于这些发现构建的UniSDfull集成方案，综合所有有效组件，相较基线模型提升5.4分，相较最强基线提升2.8分，验证了自蒸馏作为实用且可调控的模型适配方法的有效性。
Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework
Authors: Baolin Peng, Wenlin Yao, Qianhui Wu, Hao Cheng, Xiao Yu, Rui Yang, Tao Ge, Alessandro Sordoni, Xingdi Yuan, Yelong Shen, Pengcheng He, Tong Zhang, Zhou Yu, Jianfeng Gao
First: 2026-05-14T16:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Orchard：一个开源的代理建模框架
代理建模旨在将大语言模型转化为能够通过规划、推理、工具使用和与环境的多轮交互来解决复杂任务的自主代理。尽管投入巨大，开放研究仍受限于基础设施和训练方面的差距。许多高性能系统依赖专有代码库、模型或服务，而大多数开源框架侧重于编排和评估，而非可扩展的代理训练。我们提出Orchard，一个用于可扩展代理建模的开源框架。其核心是Orchard Env，一个轻量级环境服务，提供跨任务领域、代理框架和流水线阶段的可复用沙箱生命周期管理原语。在Orchard Env之上，我们构建了三个代理建模方案。Orchard-SWE面向编码代理，从MiniMax-M2.5和Qwen3.5-397B中蒸馏10.7万个轨迹，引入责任分配监督微调以学习未解决轨迹中的有效片段，并应用平衡自适应展开进行强化学习。从Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking开始，Orchard-SWE在SWE-bench Verified上SFT后达到64.3%，SFT+RL后达到67.5%，在同等规模的开源模型中创下新纪录。Orchard-GUI仅使用0.4K蒸馏轨迹和2.2K开放式任务，训练出一个4B视觉-语言计算机使用代理，在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和DeepShop上分别取得74.1%、67.0%和64.0%的成功率，成为最强的开源模型，同时仍与专有系统保持竞争力。Orchard-Claw面向个人助理代理，仅用0.2K合成任务训练，即在Claw-Eval上达到59.6%的pass@3，在与更强的ZeroClaw框架配合时达到73.9%。总体而言，这些结果表明，一个轻量、开放、框架无关的环境层可实现跨领域的可复用代理数据、训练方案和评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of open-source infrastructure for scalable agentic modeling, the authors introduce Orchard, an open-source framework that enables training autonomous agents via planning, reasoning, and tool use. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service managing sandbox lifecycles across tasks and pipelines. Three agent-specific recipes are built on top: Orchard-SWE trains a coding agent using distilled trajectories and credit-assignment SFT plus RL, achieving 67.5% on SWE-bench Verified; Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language agent with minimal data, reaching 74.1% on WebVoyager; and Orchard-Claw trains a personal assistant agent with 0.2K synthetic tasks, scoring 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval. These results demonstrate that a modular, open environment layer enables efficient, cross-domain agent training without proprietary dependencies.
为解决开源领域缺乏可扩展的智能体建模基础设施的问题，作者提出了Orchard开源框架，支持跨领域智能体训练。其核心是Orchard Env，一个轻量级环境服务，负责沙箱生命周期管理与智能体交互。框架构建了三个专用训练方案：Orchard-SWE基于蒸馏轨迹与信用分配SFT+强化学习，使编码智能体在SWE-bench上达到67.5%准确率；Orchard-GUI仅用少量数据训练视觉-语言智能体，在WebVoyager上达成74.1%成功率；Orchard-Claw仅用合成任务训练个人助理智能体，在Claw-Eval上达59.6%通过率。实验表明，模块化、开放的环境层可高效支持跨领域智能体开发，无需依赖专有资源。
Hyperparameter Transfer with Mixture-of-Expert Layers
Authors: Tianze Jiang, Blake Bordelon, Cengiz Pehlevan, Boris Hanin
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-28T03:02:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:25:08+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers have emerged as an important tool in scaling up modern neural networks by decoupling total trainable parameters from activated parameters in the forward pass for each token. However, sparse MoEs add complexity to training due to (i) new trainable parameters (router weights) that, like all other parameter groups, require hyperparameter (HP) tuning; (ii) new architecture scale dimensions (number of and size of experts) that must be chosen and potentially taken large. To make HP selection cheap and reliable, we propose a new parameterization for transformer models with MoE layers when scaling model width, depth, number of experts, and expert (hidden) size. Our parameterization is justified by a novel dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) analysis. When varying different model dimensions trained at a fixed token budget, we find empirically that our parameterization enables reliable HP transfer across models from 51M to over 2B total parameters. We further take HPs identified from sweeping small models on a short token horizon to train larger models on longer horizons and report performant model behaviors.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用专家混合层进行超参数迁移
专家混合（MoE）层已成为通过将前向传递中激活参数与总可训练参数解耦来扩展现代神经网络的重要工具。然而，稀疏MoE增加了训练复杂性：(i) 新的可训练参数（路由权重），与其他参数组一样，需要超参数（HP）调优；(ii) 新的架构规模维度（专家数量和大小），必须选择并可能设置得较大。为使超参数选择廉价且可靠，我们提出了一种在扩展模型宽度、深度、专家数量和专家（隐藏）大小时，用于Transformer模型的新型参数化方法。该参数化方法由新颖的动力学平均场理论（DMFT）分析所支持。在固定token预算下，通过改变不同模型维度进行实验，我们发现该参数化方法能够在51M至超过2B总参数的模型间实现可靠的超参数迁移。我们进一步利用在短token时间范围内对小型模型进行超参数扫描所确定的超参数，训练在长token时间范围内的大型模型，并报告了其高性能表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complexity of hyperparameter tuning in sparse Mixture-of-Experts layers, this work proposes a novel parameterization for transformer models that scales with model width, depth, expert count, and expert size. Grounded in a dynamical mean-field theory analysis, the method enables reliable hyperparameter transfer across models ranging from 51M to over 2B parameters. Experiments show that hyperparameters optimized on small models with short token budgets effectively transfer to larger models with longer horizons, yielding strong performance without re-tuning.
为解决稀疏Mixture-of-Experts层带来的超参数调优复杂性，本文提出一种新的Transformer模型参数化方法，支持模型宽度、深度、专家数量和专家隐藏层大小的扩展。该方法基于动态平均场理论分析，实验证明其能在51M至20亿参数规模的模型间实现可靠的超参数迁移。在短token预算下优化的超参数可有效训练长token序列的大模型，且性能保持稳定。
Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Sean Wu, Pan Lu, Yupeng Chen, Jonathan Bragg, Yutaro Yamada, Peter Clark, David Clifton, Philip Torr, James Zou, Junchi Yu
First: 2026-05-21T16:23:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:36+00:00
Comments: 73 pages, 13 figures, 29 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用人工智能预测科学进步
人工智能（AI）正日益融入科学发现，但其能否预测科学进步仍不明确。为研究此问题，我们引入了一个基于时间的评估框架，用于在可控知识约束下预测科学进步。我们提出了CUSP（截止条件下的未见科学进步），这是一个跨学科、事件级基准，通过可行性评估、机制推理、生成性解决方案设计和时间预测来评估AI系统中的科学预测能力。在4760个科学事件中，我们观察到当前前沿模型存在系统性且领域依赖的局限性。尽管模型能从竞争性候选方案中识别出合理的研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学突破是否实现，且系统性地误判其发生时间。不同领域间表现差异显著，AI进展的时间预测比生物学、化学和物理学的进展更可预测。模型表现对事件是否发生在训练截止前或后基本无敏感性，表明这些局限性不能仅由训练数据中的知识暴露解释。在受控信息访问条件下，额外的截止前知识虽能提升表现，但无法弥补与全信息设置之间的差距，且对高引用突破的差距更为明显。模型还表现出系统性过度自信和强烈响应偏差，表明其不确定性估计不可靠。综上，当前AI系统作为科学进步预测工具尚显不足。获取先前知识并不能转化为可靠的预测能力，而表现更多受益于事后信息而非前瞻预测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing role of AI in scientific discovery, this study investigates whether AI can reliably forecast scientific progress. It introduces CUSP, a benchmark evaluating AI models across four dimensions—feasibility, reasoning, solution design, and temporal prediction—using 4,760 scientific events under controlled knowledge constraints. Results reveal that while models can identify plausible research directions, they fail to reliably predict whether advances will materialize or when, with performance varying significantly by domain (AI being more predictable than biology, chemistry, or physics). Performance is unaffected by whether events occur before or after training cutoffs, suggesting limitations stem beyond training data exposure. Additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves but does not close the gap to full-information settings, especially for high-impact advances. Models also show overconfidence and response biases, indicating poor uncertainty estimation. Overall, current AI systems are inadequate as tools for forecasting scientific progress.
鉴于人工智能在科学发现中的广泛应用，本研究旨在探究AI是否能可靠预测科学进展。研究构建了CUSP基准，通过可行性评估、机制推理、方案生成和时间预测四个维度，在知识受限条件下评估AI系统表现。在4760个科学事件中，模型虽能识别合理研究方向，却难以准确预测成果是否实现或何时实现，且表现因学科而异，AI领域预测更优，而生物、化学和物理领域表现较差。模型性能与事件是否发生在训练截止前或后关系不大，表明知识暴露并非主要限制因素。额外的预截止知识虽能小幅提升表现，但无法弥补与全信息设置的差距，尤其对高引用成果影响更显著。模型还表现出系统性过自信和响应偏差，说明其不确定性估计不可靠。总体而言，当前AI系统尚不足以作为科学进展预测的有效工具。
Conceptualizing Embeddings: Sparse Disentanglement for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Piotr Kubaty, Patryk Marszałek, Łukasz Struski, Adam Wróbel, Jacek Tabor, Marek Śmieja
First: 2026-05-21T16:23:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models learn powerful multimodal embeddings, yet their internal semantics remain opaque. While sparse autoencoders (SAEs) can extract interpretable features, they rely on expanding the representation dimension, which compromises the original geometry and introduces redundancy. We introduce CEDAR (Conceptual Embedding Disentanglement via Adaptive Rotation), a post-hoc method that reveals the compositional structure of pretrained embeddings without increasing dimensionality. By learning an invertible transformation with a top-$k$ sparsity bottleneck, CEDAR concentrates semantic information into axis-aligned disentangled coordinates. In CLIP-like architecture, individual coordinates can be interpreted with textual concepts, while for generative models such as BLIP, they can be decoded into natural language descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that CEDAR achieves a competitive reconstruction-sparsity trade-off while producing explanations that are more interpretable and better aligned with human perception. Our results suggest that the apparent entanglement in vision-language representations can be resolved through a suitable change of basis, eliminating the need for overcomplete expansions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：嵌入的概念化：视觉-语言模型的稀疏解耦
视觉-语言模型学习到强大的多模态嵌入，但其内部语义仍不透明。虽然稀疏自动编码器（SAEs）可提取可解释特征，但它们依赖于扩展表示维度，这会破坏原始几何结构并引入冗余。我们提出CEDAR（通过自适应旋转实现概念嵌入解耦），一种后处理方法，可在不增加维度的情况下揭示预训练嵌入的组合结构。通过学习一个具有top-k稀疏瓶颈的可逆变换，CEDAR将语义信息集中到轴对齐的解耦坐标中。在CLIP类架构中，单个坐标可被文本概念解释；而在BLIP等生成模型中，可解码为自然语言描述。实验表明，CEDAR在重建与稀疏性之间取得良好平衡，生成的解释更易理解且更符合人类感知。我们的结果表明，视觉-语言表示中的表观纠缠可通过合适的基变换解决，无需过度扩展表示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of interpretability in vision-language model embeddings despite their power, the authors propose CEDAR, a post-hoc method that disentangles semantic components without increasing dimensionality. CEDAR learns an invertible transformation constrained by a top-k sparsity bottleneck, aligning embeddings into axis-aligned, disentangled coordinates that map to interpretable textual concepts or natural language descriptions. Experiments show CEDAR achieves a strong balance between reconstruction accuracy and sparsity, yielding explanations more aligned with human perception than prior methods, suggesting entanglement in vision-language representations can be resolved via basis transformation without overcomplete expansions.
为了解释视觉-语言模型中不可见的嵌入语义，同时不增加维度，作者提出CEDAR方法，通过可逆变换与top-k稀疏约束对嵌入空间进行旋转，使语义信息沿轴对齐坐标分解，从而实现可解释的分解——CLIP类模型可映射为文本概念，生成模型如BLIP可解码为自然语言描述。实验表明，CEDAR在重建精度与稀疏性之间取得良好平衡，其解释结果比现有方法更符合人类感知。
Towards Open World Sound Event Detection
Authors: P. H. Hai, L. T. Minh, L. H. Son
Venue: Signal Processing, Article 110707, 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:23:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:23:09+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 3 figures. Accepted to Signal Processing (Elsevier)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sound Event Detection (SED) plays a vital role in audio understanding, with applications in surveillance, smart cities, healthcare, and multimedia indexing. However, conventional SED systems operate under a closed-world assumption, limiting their effectiveness in real-world environments where novel acoustic events frequently emerge. Inspired by the success of open-world learning in computer vision, we introduce the Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED) paradigm, where models must detect known events, identify unseen ones, and incrementally learn from them. To tackle the unique challenges of OW-SED, such as overlapping and ambiguous events, we propose a 1D Deformable architecture that leverages deformable attention to adaptively focus on salient temporal regions. Furthermore, we design a novel Open-World Deformable Sound Event Detection Transformer (WOOT) framework incorporating feature disentanglement to separate class-specific and class-agnostic representations, together with a one-to-many matching strategy and a diversity loss to enhance representation diversity. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves marginally superior performance compared to existing leading techniques in closed-world settings and significantly improves over existing baselines in open-world scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向开放世界的声事件检测
声事件检测（SED）在音频理解中起着关键作用，应用于监控、智慧城市、医疗和多媒体索引等领域。然而，传统SED系统基于封闭世界假设，难以应对现实环境中频繁出现的新声学事件。受计算机视觉中开放世界学习成功的启发，我们提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，要求模型检测已知事件、识别未知事件并从中增量学习。为应对OW-SED的特殊挑战（如事件重叠与模糊性），我们提出一种1D可变形架构，利用可变形注意力自适应聚焦显著时域区域。此外，我们设计了新颖的开放世界可变形声事件检测Transformer（WOOT）框架，结合特征解耦分离类特定与类无关表征，辅以一对多匹配策略和多样性损失以增强表征多样性。实验表明，我们的方法在封闭世界场景中略优于现有领先技术，在开放世界场景中显著超越现有基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of closed-world sound event detection in real-world environments where novel sounds frequently appear, this work introduces Open-World Sound Event Detection (OW-SED), requiring models to detect known events, identify unseen ones, and learn incrementally. To address challenges like overlapping and ambiguous audio, the authors propose a 1D Deformable architecture with deformable attention for adaptive temporal focus, and a WOOT framework that disentangles class-specific and class-agnostic features, employs one-to-many matching, and uses a diversity loss to improve representation robustness. Experiments show the method outperforms existing baselines in open-world settings and matches or slightly exceeds state-of-the-art in closed-world benchmarks.
为应对现实场景中频繁出现的未知声事件，本文提出开放世界声事件检测（OW-SED）范式，借鉴视觉领域的开放世界学习思路。作者设计了一种1D可变形架构，利用可变形注意力机制聚焦关键时域区域，并构建WOOT框架，通过特征解耦分离类别特异与类别无关表征，结合一对多匹配策略与多样性损失提升表征多样性。实验表明，该方法在开放世界场景中显著优于现有基线，在闭世界基准上也达到或略超当前最优水平。
HumanSplatHMR: Closing the Loop Between Human Mesh Recovery and Gaussian Splatting Avatar
Authors: Yeheng Zong, Pou-Chun Kung, Yike Pan, Seth Isaacson, Yizhou Chen, Ram Vasudevan, Katherine A. Skinner
First: 2026-05-04T16:26:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:22:38+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://scottyehengz.github.io/HumanSplat/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Accurately recovering human pose and appearance from video is an essential component of scene reconstruction, with applications to motion capture, motion prediction, virtual reality, and digital twinning. Despite significant interest in building realistic human avatars from video, this paper demonstrates that existing methods do not accurately recover the 3D geometry of humans. ViT-based approaches are not consistently reliable and can overfit to 2D views, while NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting-based avatars treat pose and appearance separately, limiting rendering generalization to new poses. To resolve these shortcomings, this paper proposes HumanSplatHMR, a joint optimization framework that refines 3D human poses while simultaneously learning a high-fidelity avatar for novel-view and novel-pose synthesis. Our key insight is to close the loop between geometric pose estimation and differentiable rendering. Unlike prior human avatar methods that rely on accurate human pose obtained through motion capture systems or offline refinement, which are impractical in in-the-wild scenarios, our approach uses only human mesh estimates from a state-of-the-art human pose estimator to better reflect real-world conditions. Therefore, instead of using the human pose only as a deformation prior, HumanSplatHMR backpropagates photometric, segmentation, and depth losses through a differentiable renderer to the pose parameters and global position. This coupling refines the global 3D pose over time, improving accuracy and alignment while producing better renderings from novel views. Experiments show consistent improvements over pose recovery baselines that omit image-level refinement and avatar baselines that decouple pose estimation from avatar reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanSplatHMR：闭合人体网格恢复与高斯点渲染人像之间的循环
从视频中准确恢复人体姿态与外观是场景重建的关键组成部分，应用于动作捕捉、动作预测、虚拟现实和数字孪生等领域。尽管已有大量研究致力于从视频构建逼真的人体虚拟形象，本文证明现有方法无法准确恢复人体的3D几何结构。基于ViT的方法不够稳定且易对2D视角过拟合，而基于NeRF和高斯点渲染的人像方法将姿态与外观分离，限制了对新姿态的渲染泛化能力。为解决这些问题，本文提出HumanSplatHMR，一种联合优化框架，同时优化3D人体姿态并学习高保真虚拟形象，以支持新视角与新姿态合成。我们的核心思想是闭合几何姿态估计与可微渲染之间的循环。与依赖运动捕捉系统或离线优化的现有方法不同，我们的方法仅使用当前最先进的姿态估计器输出的人体网格，更贴近真实场景。因此，HumanSplatHMR不仅将人体姿态作为形变先验，还通过可微渲染器将光度、分割和深度损失反向传播至姿态参数与全局位置，这种耦合机制随时间优化全局3D姿态，提升精度与对齐效果，同时生成更优的新视角渲染结果。实验表明，该方法在忽略图像级优化的姿态恢复基线和分离姿态估计与虚拟形象重建的虚拟形象基线上均取得持续改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing methods in accurately recovering human 3D geometry from video, this paper introduces HumanSplatHMR, a joint optimization framework that simultaneously refines human pose and learns a high-fidelity avatar for novel-view and novel-pose rendering. Unlike prior approaches that rely on motion capture or offline pose refinement, HumanSplatHMR uses only mesh estimates from a standard pose estimator and closes the loop between pose estimation and differentiable rendering by backpropagating photometric, segmentation, and depth losses to pose parameters. Experimental results demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines that lack image-level pose refinement or decouple pose estimation from avatar reconstruction, achieving more accurate and aligned 3D poses and better novel-view renderings.
为解决视频中人体3D几何重建不准确的问题——现有方法或过拟合2D视角，或割裂姿态与外观导致泛化能力差——本文提出HumanSplatHMR框架，通过闭合姿态估计与可微渲染之间的循环，同时优化人体姿态与高保真虚拟人模型。该方法不依赖运动捕捉或离线精修，仅使用先进姿态估计器输出的网格，通过反向传播光度、分割与深度损失来迭代优化姿态参数与全局位置。实验表明，该方法在姿态恢复与虚拟人渲染质量上均显著优于忽略图像级优化或割裂姿态与重建的基线方法。
RAGCap-Bench: Benchmarking Capabilities of LLMs in Agentic Retrieval Augmented Generation Systems
Authors: Jingru Lin, Chen Zhang, Stephen Y. Liu, Haizhou Li
First: 2025-10-15T04:13:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-21T16:22:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates key limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs)-such as factual errors, outdated knowledge, and hallucinations-by dynamically retrieving external information. Recent work extends this paradigm through agentic RAG systems, where LLMs act as agents to iteratively plan, retrieve, and reason over complex queries. However, these systems still struggle with challenging multi-hop questions, and their intermediate reasoning capabilities remain underexplored. To address this, we propose RAGCap-Bench, a capability-oriented benchmark for fine-grained evaluation of intermediate tasks in agentic RAG workflows. We analyze outputs from state-of-the-art systems to identify common tasks and the core capabilities required for their execution, then construct a taxonomy of typical LLM errors to design targeted evaluation questions. Experiments show that "slow-thinking" models with stronger RAGCap performance achieve better end-to-end results, underscoring the benchmark's validity and the importance of enhancing these intermediate capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAGCap-Bench：评估代理式检索增强生成系统中大语言模型能力的基准
检索增强生成（RAG）通过动态检索外部信息缓解了大语言模型（LLM）的关键局限，如事实错误、知识过时和幻觉。近期研究通过代理式RAG系统扩展了这一范式，其中LLM作为代理迭代规划、检索并推理复杂查询。然而，这些系统仍难以应对具有挑战性的多跳问题，其中间推理能力尚未充分探索。为此，我们提出RAGCap-Bench，一个面向能力的基准，用于细粒度评估代理式RAG工作流中的中间任务。我们分析当前最先进系统的输出，识别常见任务及其执行所需的核心能力，并构建典型LLM错误的分类体系，以设计针对性评估问题。实验表明，具备更强RAG能力的“慢思考”模型在端到端结果上表现更优，凸显了该基准的有效性及提升中间能力的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate intermediate reasoning capabilities in agentic RAG systems, this study introduces RAGCap-Bench, a benchmark designed to assess fine-grained skills such as planning, retrieval, and multi-hop reasoning. The method involves analyzing outputs from state-of-the-art systems to derive a taxonomy of core tasks and common LLM errors, which informs the construction of targeted evaluation questions. Experimental results show that models exhibiting stronger intermediate RAG capabilities—particularly those that engage in slower, more deliberate reasoning—achieve better overall performance, validating the benchmark’s utility and highlighting the importance of refining these internal processes.
为评估代理型RAG系统中的中间推理能力，本文提出RAGCap-Bench基准，用于细粒度评估检索、规划与推理等阶段所需的核心能力。方法通过分析当前最优系统输出，归纳常见任务及LLM错误类型，进而设计针对性评测题目。实验表明，表现出“慢思考”特征的模型在中间任务上表现更优，最终端结果也更佳，验证了该基准的有效性，并强调提升中间能力的重要性。
